patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

muṉṉāḷ tamiḻaka mutalvariṉ muṉṉuri

muṉṉuri

iraṇṭām patippiṉ muṉṉuri

﻿coṟpoḻivu—ka— (15-2-1965)

coṟpoḻivu 2- (16-2-1965)

coṟpoḻi—3— (16-2-1965)

piṟcērkki





﻿muṉṉāḷ tamiḻaka mutalvariṉ muṉṉuri



amiḻtiṉum iṉiya tamiḻmoḻi ilakkiya vaḷam mikuntatu. nam muṉṉērkaḷākiya pulavar perumakkaḷ iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpiruntē caṅkam vittu moḻiyi vaḷarttārkaḷ. kāppiyaṅkaḷ iyaṟṟi moḻiyiṉ ilakkiya vaḷattip perukkiṉarkaḷ. navaracaṅkaḷ tatumpum ik kāppiyaṅkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, makkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttuvataṟkeṉṟē nīti nūlkaliyum, pakti mārkkattukku aṭippaṭiyāṉa tōttirap pāṭalkaḷiyum pāṭit tantārkaḷ. ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiyum uruvākkiṉarkaḷ. avarkaḷ moḻiyiṉ vaḷarccikkākat tiṭṭamiṭṭu, ākka pūrvamāṉa ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭṭatāl tāṉ iṉṟu tamiḻmoḻi ivvaḷavu ilakkiya, ilakkaṇa vaḷaṅkaḷōṭu tikaḻkiṟatu.

orumoḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci eṉpatu pala amcaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya periya kāriyam. ‘vāḻka’ ‘vaḷarka’ eṉṟa veṟum vāḻttukkaḷāl maṭṭum oru moḻi orupōtum vaḷarcciyaṭiyātu. paḻiya nūlkaḷip pātukāppatu, vaḷarntuvarum makkaḷiṉattiṉ cintaṉikaḷukkēṟpap putumiyāṉa nūlkaḷi uruvākkuvatu, ilakkaṇa varampukaḷ nacittu viṭāmal kaṭṭik kāppatu, anta moḻiyiṉ ilakkiya, ilakkaṇa nayaṅkaḷippaṟṟi ārāycci nūlkaḷ eḻutuvatu ākiya palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa paṇikaḷāltāṉ moḻiyi vaḷarkka muṭiyum.

taḻiḻ moḻiyil, moḻi ārāycci nūlkaḷum, ilakkiya varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷum pōtumāṉa aḷavu illi eṉpatu aṉivarkkum vekukālamāka iruntuvarum oru maṉakkuṟiyākum. āṅkilamoḻiyil ittakiya nūlkaḷ aḷavillāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru nūṟṟāṇṭilum ovvōr irāja vamcattiṉariṉ ātikkattilum āṅkilam ﻿ettakiya māṟutal aṭintatu, evvāṟu vaḷarcci peṟṟatu eṉpatiyellām ārayntu pala nūlkaḷ am moḻiyil eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. āṅkila moḻi aṟiñarkaḷ intattuṟiyil eṭuttuk koṇṭa muyaṟciyē itaṟkuk kāraṇam ākum. atēpōl tamiḻmoḻiyilum ittakiya nūlkaḷ eḻutap peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam ciṟitu kālattukku muṉputāṉ nam nāṭṭil tōṉṟiyatu. kāla aḷavu ciṟitāyiṉum, antattuṟiyil ippōtu kaṇicamāṉa muṉṉēṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭiruppatu perumip paṭattakka oṉṟu.

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻmoḻiyil ēṟpaṭṭa urinaṭiyiṉ vaḷarcci paṟṟiya ilakkiya varalāṟṟi inta nūl vivarikkiṟatu. tamiḻp pērāciriyar srī a. mu. paramacivāṉantam teḷinta ārāycciyōṭum tīrkkamāṉa cintaṉiyōṭum pala nuṭpamāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷi itil tantirukkiṟar. oru vaṭivam peṟṟa urinaṭi yeṉpatu veku kālattukkup piṟakutāṉ tamiḻ moḻiyil tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyatu. iruppiṉum iṉṟu atu makattāṉa vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷatu. inta vaḷarcciyiṉ ārampam pattoṉpatāmnūṟṟāṇṭilēyē malarat toṭaṅkiyatu. iti, pala kaṭṭurikaḷ, periyōr eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭu aḻakākac cittirikkiṟār āciriyar.

ilakkiya varalāṟukaḷi eḻutum putiya muyaṟciyil in nūl oru kuṟippiṭṭuc collattakka cātaṉi. itaṟkāka srī a. mu. paramacivāṉantam avarkaḷip pārāṭṭukiṟēṉ itēpōl ivar iṉṉum pala ārāycci nūlkaḷit tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ vaḷarccikkākat taravēṇṭum.

moḻi ārvam mikuntu varum innāḷil makkaḷ ittakiya nūlkaḷukku nalla varavēṟput taravēṇṭum eṉṟu kēṭṭuk koḷkiṟēṉ.

paktavatsalam

24—2—66





﻿





muṉṉuri



ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattār 1964-65 āṇṭiṟkuriya ‘kalki niṉivuc coṟpoḻi’ vi āṟṟumāṟu eṉṉip paṇittaṉar. maṟinta kalki avarkaḷiṉ niṉivāka amiyappeṟṟa nilitta toṭarc coṟpoḻivu varici ituvākum. ivvariciyil mūṉṟṟāvatu āṇṭu itu eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.inta varicikku āyvuc coṟ poḻivukaḷ amitalē cālap poruttamākum. inta aṭippaṭiyil ‘pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tamiḻ uri naṭi vaḷarcci’ eṉṟa talippil 1965 pipravari 15, 16, 17-m nāṭkaḷil āṟṟiya coṟpoḻivukaḷē iṉṟu nūl vaṭivil veḷi varukiṉṟaṉa.



ittalippiṉ aṭippaṭiyil nāṉ ārāyntu kaṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷ pala—ēṭukaḷ pala—paḻam peru nūlkaḷ pala-kāṇātaṉa. iṉṉum palavāka irukkalām. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ ilakkiyam—ciṟappāka uri naṭi ilakkiyam vaḷarntatik kāṇap pala āṇṭukaḷ tēvi. eṉavē innūlil nāṉ aṉittiyum kāṭṭiviṭṭēṉ eṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. eṉṟālum avaṟṟiṉ vakikaḷiyellām orumukappaṭutti uriyavakiyil pirittu mūṉṟu coṟpoḻivukaḷāl muṭittuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. mutaṟ ceṟpoḻivil tamiḻ urinaṭiyiṉ tōṟṟa vaḷarcciyiṉiyum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ talivāyilil ataṉ niliyiṉiyum kāṭṭiṉēṉ. iraṇṭām nāḷ coṟ poḻivil vāḻviṉ palvēṟu camutāyat tuṟikaḷil- araciyal, vāḻviyal, kalvi, paṇpāṭu, āṭcimuṟi ākiya vaḻikaḷil urinaṭi vaḷarnta vakiyiṉik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. mūṉṟām nāḷil moḻi, ilakkiyam, camayam ākiya neṟikaḷil urinaṭi vaḷarnta vakiyiṉik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. ivvāṟu iyaṉṟavakiyil ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ uri naṭi vaḷarcciyiṉi ōraḷavu kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. ivaṟṟi ārāyumpōtu aracāṅkamum tamiḻ nāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum ittuṟiyil āyvu kāṇat taṉitta amippukkaḷi niṟuviṉ, nāṭṭukkum moḻikkum nalla araṇum ākkamum uṇṭākkum eṉa niṉittēṉ. āṉāl anta niṉivic ceyalāṟṟuvatu eṉṉal muṭiyāta oṉṟallavā! vallār vaḻi kāṇpārāka!



ippaṇiyil ūkkiya ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattārukkum innūlukku muṉṉuri tantu ciṟappitta nam tamiḻaka mutalamiccar uyartiru mī. paktavatcalam avarkaḷukkum eṉ naṉṟiyiyum vaṇakkattiyum .terivittuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ.

tamiḻkkali illam:

ceṉṉi-30

25—2—66



﻿paṇivuḷḷa,

﻿a. mu. paramacivāṉantam





﻿iraṇṭām patippiṉ muṉṉuri

innūliṉ mutal patippiṉic ceṉṉit tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar kūṭṭuṟavuc caṅkattār iṉṟikkup pattu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. iṭippaṭṭa kālattil tamiḻakattilum paranta intiya nāṭṭilum ettaṉiyō māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiyuḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum tamiḻ moḻiyil pōtumāṉa āyvu nūlkaḷ iṉṉum veḷivarātiruppatu eṇṇattakkatāka uḷḷatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum piṟapotuniliyaṅkaḷum nalla āyvunilikkaḷaṉkaḷāka amintu, pala āyvu nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum eṉa aṟiñar palar aṭikkaṭi kūṟi varukiṉṟamiyi īṇṭu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

innūliṉit tamiḻ nāṭṭup palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷum piṟa mānilaṅkaḷil uḷḷa palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷuḷ cilavum tamiḻ mutukali, pulavar mutaliya vakuppukaḷukkup pāṭanūlākavum vaḻi kāṭṭum tuṇi nūlākavum koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷukku eṉ vaṇakkamum naṉṟiyum.uriyaṉa.

tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarcciyil toṭarntu āyvu tēvi. vallār attuṟiyil vaḻi kāṭṭavēṇṭumeṉak kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṉṟēṉ.

mutal patippu viṟpaṉiyāvataṟkup pattu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉavāyiṉum itaṉpaṭikaḷ aṉittum takkār kippaṭṭuc ciṟantuḷḷami aṟintu makiḻkiṉṟēṉ. toṭarntu ippatippiṉiyum tamiḻulakam ēṟṟu ātarikkum eṉum tuṇipuṭiyēṉ. innūl veḷivaruvataṟku utaviya aṉivarukkum naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.

tamiḻkkali illam:

ceṉṉi-30

25—2—66

paṇivuḷḷa,

a. mu. paramacivāṉantam





mūṉṟām patippu

innūliṉ mūṉṟām patippu iṉṟu veḷivarukiṟatu; piṟa aṇṇāmali, maturi palkalik kaḻakac coṟpoḻivukaḷuṭaṉ iṇikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻulakam ēṟṟup purappatāka.

30-11-78 āciriyaṉ





﻿

coṟpoḻivu—ka— (15-2-1965)



* * *



muṉṉuri

pērāciriyar avarkaḷē! periyōrkaḷē!

maṟinta tiruvāḷar ‘kalki’ avarkaḷuṭiya niṉivuc coṟpoḻiviṉi eṉakku ippalkalik kaḻakattār aḷittu, ivvāṇṭu eṉṉip pēcumāṟu paṇittuḷḷārkaḷ. mutalāvatāka avarkaṭku eṉ naṉṟiyit terivittuk koḷḷukiṉṟēṉ. ic coṟpoḻivut toṭarukku aminta poruḷ, ‘pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tamiḻ uri naṭi vaḷarcci’ (Development of Tamil Prose in the 19th Century) eṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil urinaṭi vaḷartta perumikkuriya ‘kalki’ avarkaḷiṉ niṉivuc coṟpoḻivu allavā! avar vāḻnta nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ irunta urinaṭi vaḷarcciyip paṟṟi ārāyvatāka amital poruntu vatutāṉē? oru nūṟṟaṇṭil nam moḻiyil uṇṭāṉa nūlkaḷ-ilakkiya nūlkaḷ-eṇṇila. avaṟṟuḷ uri naṭi nūlkaḷākavum itaḻkaḷākavum ēṭukaḷākavum vēṟu palavakikaḷākavum amintaṉa uḷḷaṉa. avi aṉittum urinaṭi vaḷarcciyil paṅku koṇṭuḷḷaṉavēyām. iṅku avaṟṟi ārāyntu pārppataṉ muṉ. ‘tamiḻ ilakkiya vaḷarcci’ kālaṅkaṭanta varalāṟṟu elliyi uṭiyatākiyāl, akkāla aḷavil ivvurinaṭi tōṉṟi vaḷarnta varalāṟṟi aṟiya vēṇṭuvatum iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ talivāyilil kālvippataṉmuṉ, ataṟku muṉ pala nūṟṟaṇṭukaḷil ivvuri naṭi evvāṟu tōṉṟi vaḷarttu vaḷampeṟṟu vantateṉpatik kāṇal ēṟpuṭittākum.

tamiḻ moḻi

tamiḻ moḻi tolkāppiyar kālattukku muṉpē vaḷam peṟṟa moḻiyeṉpatum, avar kālattukku muṉpē immoḻiyil urinaṭiyum pāṭṭum palki iruntaṉa eṉpatum avartam nūl vaḻiyākavē nammāl aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu. tamiḻil urinaṭi eṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉa variyaṟukka muṭiyātu. ataṉ varalāṟṟi āyu muṉ urinaṭi eṉpatu eṉṉa eṉpatiyum atu vāḻviṉ tēviyi evvevvāṟu niṟivu ceykiṟatu eṉpatiyum ārāyavēṇṭuvatum tēviyāṉa oṉṟallavā?

etu uri naṭi?

etu uri naṭi? ēṉ uri naṭi? ivi patil iṟukkappeṟa vēṇṭiya viṉākkaḷ, tamiḻil maṭṭumiṉṟi āṅkilam mutaliya piṟa moḻikaḷilum ittakiya kēḷvikaḷukku viṭi kāṇal tēviyē. mēlināṭṭu āyvāḷarkaḷuḷ cilar ivaṟṟiṟkuriya viṭikaḷiyum āyntu kaṇṭu aṟutiyiṭṭu eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. maṉitaṉ piṟa uyiriṉattiṉum vēṟupaṭṭavaṉ; taṉ uḷḷak karutti uṭaṉ vāḻum maṟṟavarukku uṇarttak kaṭamippaṭṭavaṉ. vāy tiṟantu pēcum vāyppu ivaṉukku uṇṭu anta vāyppē ‘moḻi' eṉṟa oṉṟi ulakukku aḷittatu. irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭipaṭṭa nākarika ulakil, moḻi aṭippaṭiyil palvēṟu caṇṭikaḷi nām kāṇkiṉṟāēmāyiṉum, inta moḻiyē maṉitaṉi maṉitaṉāka vāḻa vaḻi kāṭṭi vaḻi vakuttu, vāḻa vitta oṉṟu eṉpati nammāl maṟakka muṭiyātu. inta moḻikaḷiṉ amippu nāṭutōṟum vēṟupaṭuvatu eṉṟālum, moḻi camutāyatti iṇikkum orē cātaṉam allatu karuvi eṉpati yārālum maṟukka iyalātu. immoḻikaḷ tattam tōṟṟa vaḷarcci aṭippaṭiyil ciṟantum citintum, vaḷarntum vāṭiyum, uyarntum tāḻntum iṉṟu ulakil ulavukiṉṟaṉa. nalla tiruntiya moḻikaḷuḷ palvēṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉ. kaṭanta kāla varalāṟṟu vāḻviyum iyaṟki vaḷattiyum kāṭṭi, aṉṟu vāḻnta makkaṭ camutāyattiyum iṉṟu vāḻum nammiyum varuṅkālac camutāyattiyum vāḻa vippaṉavē ilakkiyaṅkaḷ. inta aṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷē kālatti veṉṟu vāḻvaṉa. maṟṟavi ...? nām colla vēṇṭā.

ivvilakkiya aṭippaṭikaḷāka amintaṉavum pēccu vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉavumākiya uriyum pāṭṭum nām eṇṇattakkaṉa. uri eṉṟāl eṉṉa? inta 'uri' eṉṟa col neṭuṅkālamākavē tamiḻil vaḻakkattil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. 'urikkappaṭuvatu uri' eṉṟu curukkamākap patil colliviṭalām. 'uri' eṉṟa collukkut tamiḻil pala poruḷkaḷ uḷḷami aṟiñar aṟintatē. mutal mutal veṟum olikkē inta uri eṉṟa col payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭateṉpati uṇarkiṉṟāēm 'kuṉṟam kumuṟiya uri' (paripāṭal,8-35) eṉṟē paripāṭal kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. viṇṇiṉṟiraṅkit taṇmaḻi poḻiyum eḻili taṉ varavukāṭṭa eḻunta oli atu. atu urikka maḻi varum eṉpati ulakam uṇarkiṉṟatu. inta vakiyil makkaḷukku uṇarttaṭa uṇarviṉi aḷikkap payaṉpaṭṭatu-payaṉpaṭuvatu uriyākum.

uri eṉpatu poruḷiṉ taṉmiyi uḷḷavāṟu urippatākum. iti viḷakka oru cāṉṟu payaṉpaṭum. taṭṭāṉ poṉṉukku uri kāṇum vaḻakkam, palakaṭṭuppāṭukaḷukkiṭiyil, nam nāṭṭil iṉṟum uḷḷatti aṟivōm. poṉ ciṟantatā illiyā eṉpati-ataṉ uṇmi nili, paṇpu, taram mutaliyaṉa iṉṉa vakiyaṉa eṉpati-urittuk kāṇpatu nāṭaṟinta oṉṟu. ataṉ taṉmiyi uṇarttum kallukku 'uri kal' eṉṟē peyar uṇṭu allavā? eṉavē poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpi uḷḷatu uḷḷavāṟē ulakukku uṇarttuvatu uriyākum. itittāṉ ‘aṭippaṭi moḻi’ eṉpar āyvāḷar. kaṟpaṉi kalavātatāy-uṇarcciyō, piṟa uḷḷa nekiḻccikaḷō, kāytal uvattal eṉṟa vēṟupāṭukaḷō kalavātatāy-oru poruḷiṉ taṉmiyi uriyiṭṭu uṇarattakka vakiyil kāṭṭuvatē uri. (taṭṭāṉ poṉṉi uriyiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu pōṉṟu) āṉāl pāṭṭō itaṟku nērmāṟaka uṇarcci. kaṟpaṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iṭiyilē tōyntu aṇinalam mutaliyaṉa peṟṟup pāṭuvōṉ uḷḷattaḷavu uyarvō aṉṟit tāḻvō poruntap poruḷi viḷakkuvatākum. inta uṇmiyi mēli nāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷārkaḷ. moḻi, ilakkiyam iraṇṭiyum variyaṟutta āyvāḷar eṇṇatti uṇarttuvatu moḻiyeṉṟum uṇarvi uṇarttuvatu ilakkiyam eṉṟu kūṟuvar

I convey my thought to you—this is language



I have emotion —that is literature



If the primary object be to convey the thought and the emotion where with it is touched by a secondary consideration, serving only to make the thought apprehended more pleasantly and more completely, the writing is some form of Prose Literature, as history or criticism. If on the other hand, the emotion be of first importance, and the thought seems to take place in the mind through the avenues of feeling, then the writing is some form of better letters—probably in the poetry or fiction. (Winchester, P. 182)



ivvāṟu eḻuttukku varivaṭivam koṭukkum niliyilēyē ‘uri’ uṇṭākiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu uṇmiyaṉṟē uḷḷattut tōṉṟiya eṇṇatti maṟṟavarukku uṇartta niṉitta maṉitaṉ eṇṇi eṇṇi eḻutteḻuttāka uccarittu, colliṉ piṉ collāka mella urittiruppāṉaṉṟāē? eṉavē mutalil tōṉṟiyatu uri eṉakkoḷḷal poruttamāṉatākum. āyiṉum cila mēli nāṭṭu āyvāḷarkaḷ pāṭṭu mutalil tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉa eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. atu evvāṟu poruttum eṉat teriyavilli. kuḻanti mutalil piṟantatum aḻukiṟatu; piṟaku pēcukiṉṟatu. aḻum ici muṉṉātaliṉ atu pāṭṭāka muṉ tōṉṟiyirukkum eṉpatu avar vātam. kuḻanti aḻuvatu iciyāka irukkalām - āṉāl atu moḻiyiṭippaṭṭa pāṭṭō ceyyuḷō eppaṭiyākum? anta aḻukiyil-iciyilmoḻiyē illiyē. kuḻanti vaḷara vaḷara mella mella, peṟṟēr uṇartta ovvoru collākap pēcip pēciyaṉṟō uri naṭiyik kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu. piṉ vēṇṭumāyiṉ iciyum pāṭṭum iyiyap ‘peṟalām. eṉavē ikkūṟṟu ēṟṟukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatu tāṉa? aṟiñarkaḷē āyntu muṭivu kāṇaṭṭum.

“ceyyuḷ“ eṉpatu muṟkāla vaḻakkil ilakkiyac ceṟivuḷḷa uriyiyum taḻuvi yiruntatu. 'uric ceyyuḷ’ eṉṟa vaḻakku itaṟkuc cāṉṟu.

ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam uriyeṉap peyarpaṭ

ṭirukkumeṉ ṟeṇṇuka ceyyuḷiṉ iyalpē





eṉpatu ilakkaṇakkottu. ulaka vaḻakkākap pēcappaṭuvatum eḻutappaṭuvatum urinaṭi, uri vācakam eṉa vaḻaṅkum. uri vācakam eṉpatu iru peyaroṭṭu. tamiḻil vacaṉam eṉṟa vaṭamoḻiyē, cila kālamāy uri naṭiyik kuṟippatāyp peruvaḻakkiṉatāyiṟṟu. uṟutic col, ēṟṟa col, poruḷuṟṟa col eṉap poruḷpaṭum kaṭṭuri eṉṉum vārttiyum, urinaṭiyik kuṟikkiṟatu. itu ‘kaṭṭuri vaṉmi’ eṉ pataṉāl pōtarum. 'kaṭṭuri vakiyāṉ eṇṇōṭu puṇarntu’ eṉat tolkāppiyattilum varukiṟatu.

“paḻiya caṅka nūlkaḷellām pākkaḷāl āṉavi. muṟṟum uri naṭiyāṉa vacaṉa nūl yātoṉṟumilli. aṭivari yiṉṟi varum ceyyuḷiṉ vaki nūlum, uriyum, piciyum, mutumoḻiyum, mantiramum, kūṟṟiṭi vitta kuṟippum eṉa āṟeṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ urip pakuti, vaḻakku, pāṭṭiṭi vitta kuṟippum, pāviṉṟeḻunta kiḷaviyum, poruḷoṭu puṇarāp poymmoḻiyum, poruḷoṭu puṇarnta nakimoḻiyum eṉa nāṉkeṉṟum, vakippaṭutturittār tolkāppiyar avaṟṟuḷ pāṭṭiṭi vittu kuṟippeṉpatu,'oru pāṭṭu iṭiyiṭi koṇṭu niṟkum karuttiṉāl varuvatu eṉṟu tiru. celvakkēcavarāya mutaliyār, tam ‘tamiḻ viyācaṅkaḷ'eṉṟa nūlil ivvuri naṭiyiṉ toṉmi paṟṟi viḷakkaṅ kāṭṭic cellukiṉṟār.

tolkāppiyar, urikku ilakkaṇam kūṟavantavar.



'toṉmi tāṉē

uriyoṭu puṇarnta paḻami mēṟṟē' (cey. 229)





eṉa viḷakkam tarukiṉṟār. itaṉ karutteṉṉa? itaṟku uri eḻutiya iḷampūraṇar, 'niṟutta muṟiyāṉē toṉmic ceyyuḷ uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu’ eṉṟu kūṟit 'toṉmiyāvatu uriyoṭu poruntip pōnta paḻimittākiya poruḷ mēl varuvatu’ eṉkiṟār. pērāciriyarum, 'toṉmi yeṉpatu uri virāyp paḻimiyavākiya katip poruḷākac collappaṭuvatu’ eṉkiṉṟār. eṉavē, ivaṟṟāl urinaṭi pāṭṭilum paḻiyatu eṉpatu naṉku viḷakkap peṟum. nām mēlē kaṇṭa 'veṟṟāeliyum uri' yeṉak kāṭṭiya paripāṭal ivviḷakkattukkuc cāṉṟākalām.



uriyum pāṭṭum

tamiḻ ilakkiyattil ivvurinaṭi muṉ tōṉṟa, neṭukālattukkup piṉ ivvurinaṭi pōṉṟu aminta cūttiraṅkaḷ-nūṟpākkaḷ-tōṉṟiṉa. piṉṉarē pāṭṭut toṉṟiṟṟu eṉal poruntum. potuvākac cūttiraṅkaḷ uṇṭāṉa kālam kiṟittup piṟappataṟku aintāṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpar āyvāḷar.[1] vaṭamoḻic cūttiraṅkaḷum ēṟakkuṟiya anta nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiltāṉ tōṉṟiyirukkavēṇṭum.[2] eṉavē urinaṭiyi aṭuttu iccūttiraṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyamum ēṟakkuṟiya ikkālattiṟkuc caṟṟu muṉpu tōṉṟiya oṉṟu eṉpatu aṟiñar palarum orucēra ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṉṟa uṇmiyākum iṉi, ittolkāppiyattiṉ ceyyuḷiyalil uriyum pāṭṭum uḷḷa nilimi nammāl kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. maṟṟum ittolkāppiyattiṉ vaḻiyē itaṟku muṉṉum pala ilakkiya nūlkaḷ tōṉṟi vāḻntu vantaṉa eṉpatiyum uṇara muṭikiṉṟatu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ toṉmi pala pēraṟiñarkaḷāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappeṟṟa oṉṟāṉamiyāl, in nāṭṭup piṟamoḻikaḷik kāṭṭilum tamiḻil paḻaṅkālattilēyē uriyum, cūttiramum, pāṭṭum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākat tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vāḻa, avaṟṟit tolkāppiyar tokuttuk kūṟi ilakkaṇa varampiṉi uṟuti ceytār eṉak koḷvatu poruttamāṉatākum. eṉavē urinaṭi pāṭṭilum muntiyatu eṉpatu tēṟṟam. iṉi, itu paṟṟi nām iṅkē atikam ārāyat tēviyilli; atu vēṇṭātatumkūṭa. ittakiya urinaṭiyilakkiyam tamiḻil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta vakiyik kāṇavēṇṭuvatu namatu iṉṟiya kaṭamiyākum.



‘tamiḻil’ ‘ceyyuḷ’ eṉṟa oru col uḷḷatu eṉa mēlē kaṇṭōm. ceyyappaṭuvaṉa aṉittum ceyyuḷē. eṉavē urinaṭiyum ceyyappaṭuvatu tāṉē. ‘ām’ eṉavē uric ceyyuḷ eṉak koḷḷalāmā? koḷḷalām. palkalikkaḻaka tamiḻp pērakarāti iti namakku eṭuttuk kāṭṭuṭaṉ viḷakkukiṉṟatu. ceyyuḷ eṉpataṟku uri eṉṟē poruḷ uḷḷatu eṉpati atu kāṭṭukiṟatu.[3] tamiḻil maṭṭumiṉṟi āṅkilattilum inta viḷakkam naṉku kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟatu. āṅkilat ‘Prose’ eṉṟa collukku ‘Stright forward arrangement of words”[4] eṉṟum ‘Plin matter of fact quality’& “ordinary nonmetrical form of written or spoken language”[5] eṉṟum poruḷ uṇṭu. nām mēlē poṉṉuriyōṭu poruttik kāṭṭiya uṇmi viḷakkam tarum urinaṭik karuttiṉi ivi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, ceyyuḷ eṉṟa colli uri, pāṭṭu iraṇṭukkum payaṉpaṭuttiṉarkaḷ eṉavum kāṇamuṭikiṟatu. uric ceyyuḷ, pāc ceyyuḷ eṉavē avi vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa-peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭum viraviyavaṟṟiyē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, uriyiṭiyiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ’ eṉak kāṭṭic celkiṉṟār. ‘Poem’ eṉṟa āṅkilac col tamiḻil uḷḷa ‘ceyyuḷ’ eṉṟa collukku nēr poruttamāṉa oṉṟu eṉṟu koḷvatu takum. ‘Poem’ eṉṟa collukku ‘elevated composition in prose or verse’[6] eṉṟum ‘a composition of high beuaty of thought or language, although not in verse” [7] eṉṟum viḷakkam tarappeṟuvati kāṇkiruēm. mēlum ‘Poetry’ eṉṟa collukku work of the poet’s elevated expression or elevated thought or feelings”[8] eṉṟum, “The art of expressing in melodious words, the thoughts which are the creation of feeling and imagination”[9] eṉṟum urikaḷ eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē 'uri' yeṉappaṭuvatu cātāraṇa vaḻakkattil uḷḷatākavum ceyyuḷ eṉpatu pāṭṭiṉiyum iṟiyaṉar kaḷaviyal uri pōṉṟa uyar naṭi urikaḷiyum kuṟittatākavum koḷḷal cālum eṉa amikiṉṟēṉ.

iṉi, uri naṭiyi (Prose) ivvāṟu akarātikaḷ kāṭṭuvatōṭu 'Encyclopedia Britanicca' eṉṟa peru viḷakka nūl evvāṟu viḷakkukiṉṟatu eṉpatiyum īṇṭuk kāṇal nalam payappatākum. ataṉ viḷakkam varumāṟu:-

‘pāṭṭukku aminta ilakkaṇa varampiṉṟi, makkaḷāl eḷimiyāka oḷivu maṟivu iṉṟi eḻutavum pēcavum payaṉpaṭum oṉṟē uriyākum. pāṭṭiliruntu (Poetry) urinaṭiyip pirittuk kāṭṭuvatu eḷitāyiṉum iṉṉum tiṭṭamāka iraṇṭiyum variyaṟukka iyalātu. ēṉeṉil, uyarnta naṭiyil pāṭṭiṉ ilakkaṇat tōṭu kalavāmal nalla ōci nayattuṭaṉ eḻutum urinaṭiyi 'uric ceyyuḷ’ eṉṟu koḷvatum uṇṭu. eṉavē, pāṭṭiliruntu urinaṭiyip pirittuk kāṇpativiṭṭu, urinaṭi eṉpati uḷḷavāṟu viḷakka muyalalām. urinaṭi eṉpatu, ilakkiya marapu keṭāta nalla naṭiyil, āḻnta karuttōṭu, pāṭṭukkuḷḷa cantac cērkki iṉṟiyē nalla ōci nayam uṭiyatāki, oḷivu maṟivu iṉṟi uḷḷati uḷḷapaṭi uriyiṭṭuk kāṭṭum vakiyil nēriya muṟiyil, karuttukkum kāraṇattukkum poruttamāṉatāka — oṉṟiṉip paṟṟiyō oruvarip paṟṟiyō— viḷakki urippatākum.

‘eṉiṉum, uri naṭi eṉpatu kaṭṭukkaṭaṅkātu pēcum allatu eḻutum cātāraṇa ellā vacaṉaṅkaḷiyum koḷvatākātu. urinaṭit toṭarkaḷ iṉmoḻiyāl nalla oḻukici naṭiyāl, toṭar nalamuṭaṉ, ōci yuṇarttum neṟiyāṉ naṉku vaḷarcciyuṟṟa teḷḷiya naṭiyil teḷintu celluvaṉavāka amiya vēṇṭum. moḻikku iṉṟiyamiyāta peyar viṉikaḷuṭaṉ cērum uriccoṟkaḷiṉ takuti aḷavaṟintu avaṟṟic cērttu vaḻaṅkum muṟiyum urinaṭiyil variyaṟi ceyyap peṟal vēṇṭum. tēviyaṟṟa cilavaṟṟi viṭṭoḻittalum urinaṭikku iṉṟiyamiyātatu. eṉavē, urinaṭi yeṉpatu pāṭṭiṉ vitikaḷukku vilakkāy niṉṟu, ilakkiya nalam keṭāvakiyil makkaḷ uḷḷattil tōṉṟum uṇarvukaḷi ulakukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭap payaṉpaṭuvatu eṉṟu koḷḷal poruntum’[10].



ikkūṟṟum nām mēlē kāṭṭiya karuttiṉi naṉku valiyuṟuttuvatākum. tamiḻmoḻiyil ittakiya uyarnta cemmoḻiyiṉ pālatākiya urinaṭiyum cātāraṇa makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakkil uḷḷa urinaṭiyum mikap paḻaṅ kālantoṭṭē vaḷarntu vāḻkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu tēṟṟam. ippēccu variciyil nām iraṇṭu vaki ‘uri’ naṭikaḷiyum kāṇalē poruttamāṉatākum.



iṉi, ittakiya nalam vāynta urinaṭi irukka, pāṭṭu ēṉ eḻuntatu? ivvuri naṭikku ilakkaṇam ēṉ illi? ivaṟṟiṟku viṭi kaṇṭu mēlē cellalām. cātāraṇa eḷiya makkaḷ tattam karuttip parimāṟik koḷḷap payaṉpeṟṟa urinaṭi irukkap pāṭṭu ēṉ eḻuntatu? aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, uḷḷa uṇarvum kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉum mikku ōṅkap pāṭṭu uruvāyiṟṟu. tām uḷḷattuk koṇṭa karuttōṭu kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉ kalakka, avi uruppeṟa, collaṭukkukaḷum aṇi nalaṉkaḷum kalantu poruntap pāṭṭu uruvāyiṟṟu. mēlum, tām viḷakka vanta karuttiṉip pāṭṭāka vaṭittu vippiṉ appaṭiyē maṉattil paṉṉeṭuṅ kālam niṉittu nili niṟuttavum appāṭṭē payaṉpaṭṭatu. urinaṭiyi appaṭiyē maṉattil uruviṭṭu niliniṟuttuvataṉiṉum pāṭṭiṉi maṉattiṭip patittal eḷitallavā? eṉavē, ivipōṉṟa pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl pāṭṭu urup peṟṟirukkalām. maṟṟum uṇarvum teyvaneṟiyum piṟa paṇpāṭukaḷum vaḷara vaḷara, uḷaṅ kuḷirnta pōtelām uṇarntuṇarntu pāṭum neṟiyum vaḷarntirukkalām. eṉavē, urinaṭikkup piṉ pāṭṭu vaḷarak kāraṇaṅkaḷ pala. virippiṟ perukum.



pāṭṭiṟku iruppatu pōṉṟu ēṉ urinaṭikku ilakkaṇam illi eṉṟa viṉāviṟku viṭi kāṇalum tēviyē. ulakil aṉṟāṭam cātāraṇamāka nikaḻum ceyalkaḷip paṟṟi yārum eḻuti vikka virumpuvatilliyallavā? nāṭkuṟippiṉit tavaṟātu viṭātu eḻutupavarkaḷumkūṭa, aṉṟāṭam muṟiyākat tāmceyyum nittiyak kaṭaṉkaḷiyum eḻutuvatu aritallavā? anta muṟiyilē tāṉ inta urinaṭip paṟṟiyum yārum eḻuti vikka virumpavilli. makkaḷ nāḷtōṟum oruvari oruvar kāṇumpōtum kalakkumpōtum cātāraṇa urinaṭiyil pōcuvatu marapu. ippēccu vaḻakku iṭantōṟummāṟuvatu muṇṭē! eṉavē avipaṟṟi ilakkaṇa marapiṉi ēṭṭil eḻutātu eṅkō yārō cila vēḷikaḷil ciṟappāka eḻutum pāṭṭiṉukku maṭṭum ilakkaṇam eḻuti vittārkaḷ. eṉiṉum uriyip paṟṟiya palvēṟu kuṟippukkaḷ paḻaṅkālattilēyē uḷḷamiyi nām aṟikiṟōm. tamiḻil maṭṭumiṉṟi, tamiḻ nāṭṭōṭu paḻaṅkālattilēyē toṭarpu koṇṭirunta yavaṉa nāṭukaḷilum inta vakiyil urinaṭikkut taṉi ilakkaṇam illiyāyiṉum, atu paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷip paravalākak kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟaṇṭilēyē kirēkka ilattīṉ moḻikaḷil urinaṭi ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉavum avvurinaṭi kaṇṭavaruḷ ciṟantavar cicarō[11] eṉavum nammāl aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.[12] iru nāṭukaḷilum tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vāḻnta iruvēṟu vakip paṭṭa (tamiḻ, kirēkka) ilakkiyaṅkaḷil uriyum pāṭṭum iruntaṉa eṉpati āyvāḷar naṉku aṟivar. ‘uriyum pāṭṭum uṭiyōr cilarē' (puṟam. 27) eṉap puṟanāṉūṟu iraṇṭiyum iṇittē pēcukiṉṟatu. 'pāṭṭum uriyum payilātaṉa iraṇṭu, oṭṭic cevi yum uḷa’ eṉṟa piṟkālap pāṭṭum iti valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu. mēlum tolkāppiyamum veḷippaṭiyāka urikkup pērilakkaṇam vakukkavilli eṉṟālum uriyiṉ toṉmiyic cuṭṭik kāṭṭat tavaṟavilli eṉpati muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. ivvāṟu tolkāppiyar kālantoṭaṅki-ēṉ?-ataṟku muṉpiruntukūṭa-iṉṟu vari tamiḻil urinaṭi vaḷarntukoṇṭē varukiṟatu. iṉiyum toṭarntu vaḷarum eṉpatil aiyamilli.

varalāṟu eṅkē?

tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarntālum ataṉ varalāṟṟit toṭarcciyāka aṟiyamuṭiyavilliyēeṉpati eṇṇum pōtu caṟṟē varuttam uṇṭākattāṉ ceykiṟatu. eḻutappeṟṟa, variyaṟutta tiṭṭamāṉa varalāṟu illiyāyiṉum tamiḻil urinaṭi mikap paḻaṅkālantoṭṭē toṭarntu vāḻntu varukiṉṟa oṉṟu eṉpatik kāṭṭa ātāraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyar kālattilum ataṟku muṉpum irunta urinaṭiyi nammāl kāṇa muṭiyāviṭṭālum, an nūṟpāvālum ataṉ urikaḷālum, avvuriyiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākak kūṟum, iṉṟillātu, aḻinta cila nūṟpeyarkaḷālum (irāma caritamum, pāṇṭava caritamum, peruntēvaṉār pāratamum, takaṭūr yāttiriyum mutaliyaṉa eṉṟu tolkāppiya uriyāciriyarkaḷāl kāṭṭap peṟuvaṉa) uri toṉmi vāyntatē eṉa uṇara muṭikiṉṟatu. mēlum caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ cila uri naṭiyip pōṉṟē taṉiccol peṟṟum peṟāmalum amintu celvatiyum kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. kaṭic caṅka kālattu iṟuti elliyil tōṉṟiya cilappatikārattilēyē nammāl urinaṭiyit teḷivākak kāṇa muṭikiṟatu. itaṉālēyē tolkāppiyac cūttirattukku (cey. 238) uri kāṇa vanta nacciṉarkiṉiyar ataṟkuc cilappatikārattiyē mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.

cilampil

tolkāppiyar kālaṅkaṭantu, cilappatikārak kālattil nammāl teḷinta urinaṭiyik kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu allavā? accilappatikārak kālattik ki. pi. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar aṟiñar. eṉavē anta nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭaṅkic ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ talivāyil variyil vantōmāṉal tamiḻ uri naṭi vaḷarcciyi oruvāṟu uṇarntōmāvōm. cilappatikārattiṟkē 'uriyiṭiyiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ’ eṉṟa peyar uḷḷati mēlē kaṇṭōm. cilappatikārattē toṭarntu urinaṭiyik kāṇa iyalātu eṉpati ikkuṟippē uṇarttum. eṉiṉum, atil teḷinta urinaṭi iruppati iṭiyiṭiyē kāṇkiṟōmē. 'uri peṟu kaṭṭuri.' 'urip pāṭṭu maṭi' eṉṟa vakiyil cilampukku iṭiyiṭiyē uri taṉ oliyi eḻuppi nammiyum eḻac ceykiṟatu allavā? intac cilampiṉ urinaṭiyik kāṇum pōtutāṉ ataṉ ēṟṟamum namakku naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṉṟu cilar cātāraṇap pēccu vaḻakkil nam vaḻakkattil uḷḷavāṟē eḻutuvatu tāṉ urinaṭi eṉṟu vātittu niṉṟatik kaṇṭōm. antap pēccu naṭi 'urinaṭi'yēyāyiṉum atu ilakkiya urinaṭiyākavō, kālatti veṉṟu kavitiyeṉa vāḻum urinaṭiyākavō irukka vaḻiyilli. attakiya urinaṭi, nammuṉ niṟkum kaṭanta oru nūṟṟaṇṭil palavakiyil vaḷarntuḷḷatu. (avaṟṟiyē aṭutta nāṭkaḷil viḷakkamākak kāṇa irukkiṉṟāēm.) eṉiṉum, cilampiṉi nōkkumpōtu ataṉ uriyip paṟṟi ārāyum namakku aviyum tēviyāṉamiyiṉ avipaṟṟi āṅkāṅkē kāṇalām.



cilappatikārattiṉ toṭakkattilē ataṉ uri maṇam kamaḻkiṉṟatu. anta urinaṭi, pāṭṭiṉum kaṭiṉamāṉatu pōṉṟu kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟatu. cilampiṉ akavali aṟintu uṇarvatilum urinaṭiyi uṇarvatu kaṭiṉamē eṉavum niṉikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum, avvuriyiṉ ēṟṟattiyum ēṟupōṟ pīṭunaṭiyiṭṭup perumi peṟṟuc ciṟappatiyum eṉṟeṉṟum nammāl maṟakka iyalātē. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ vākkil oṉṟu kāṇal poruntuvatākum. atuvum viḻāvōṭu poruntiyatāka nāṭṭi vaḷaṅkoḻikka vaḻikāṭṭuvatāka amiyaṭṭum. ‘uri peṟu kaṭṭuri’ eṉṟa talippilē kaṇṇakikku viḻā āṟṟa, muṭiyuṭi vēntar maṭṭu maṉṟip piṟanāṭṭavarum pattiṉit teyvattip paravip payaṉ peṟṟa vakiyik kūṟuvatu atu. itō avar vākki nīṅkaḷē kēḷuṅkaḷ.





"aṉṟutoṭṭup pāṇṭiya nāṭu, maḻi vaḷam karantu, vaṟumi eyti, veppu nōyum kuruvum toṭarak koṟkiyil irunta veṟṟi vēṟceḻiyaṉ naṅkikkup poṟkollar āyiravarik koṉṟu kaḷavēḷviyāl viḻavoṭu cānti ceyya, nāṭu maliya maḻipeytu nōyum tuṉpamum nīṅkiṟṟu.



atukēṭṭu, koṅkiḷaṅ kōcar, taṅkaḷ nāṭṭakattu naṅkikku viḻavoṭu cānti ceyya maḻi toḻil eṉṟum māṟātāyiṟṟu.





atukēṭṭu, kaṭalcūḻ ilaṅkik kayavāku eṉpāṉ naṅkikku nāṭpali pīṭikik kōṭṭa muntuṟuttu, āṅku 'aranti keṭuttu varam tarum ivaḷ' eṉa, āṭit tiṅkaḷ akaviyiṉ āṅkōr pāṭi viḻākkōḷ paṉmuṟi eṭuppa, maḻi vīṟṟiruntu, vaḷampala perukki, piḻiyā viḷiyuḷ nāṭāyiṟṟu.



atukēṭṭu, cōḻaṉ peruṅkiḷḷi, kōḻiyakattu, 'ettiṟattāṉum varantarum ivaḷ ōr pattiṉik kaṭavuḷākum' eṉa naṅkikkup pattiṉik kōṭṭamum camittu nittam viḻāvaṇi nikaḻvittōṉē."





ivvurinaṭiyip paṭikkum yārum uḷḷattu uyarvu peṟuvaraṉṟē? inta aḷavukkuc cilampil urinaṭi uyarntirukkavēṇṭumāyiṉ ataṟku muṉ ettaṉi ettaṉi nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ avvurinaṭi tavaḻntu, vaḷarntu, taḻittu, ōṅki, uyarntu, uṟṟa peruniliyil vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati uṇara muṭikiṉṟataṉṟāē?

kaḷaviya luri

kāppiya kālattukkup piṟaku tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ iruṇṭa kālam varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷati yāvarum aṟivar. eṉavē ilakkiyamum attuṇi ēṟṟam peṟavilli eṉpatik kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. ākiyāl uri naṭiyiṉ niliyiyum nammāl uṇarntukoḷḷa iyalavilli. eṉiṉum, tamiḻar tam vāḻvum, vaḷamum, paṇpāṭum, nākarikamum piṟanāṭṭār paṭiyeṭuppālum piṟa cūḻalkaḷālum nīṟupūtta neruppeṉa maṅkiyiruntu kālam māṟiya nāḷil maṟupaṭiyum vīṟupeṟṟami pōṉṟē urinaṭiyum maṅkit tavaḻntu eṭṭām nūṟṟaṇṭaḷavil taṉṉi viḷakka, oru ciṟanta uri nūlāka urup peṟṟatu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟaṇṭi oṭṭiya kālattil tōṉṟiya avvurinaṭi, cevivaḻiyāka oruvar vāy oruvar kēṭṭuk kēṭṭu—āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭu elliyi mellak kaṭantu—eṭṭām nūṟṟaṇṭil ‘iṟiyaṉar kaḷaviyal uri’ eṉṟa peyaroṭu ulakil uruppeṟṟatu. tamiḻ urinaṭiyi viḷakkavē annūl eḻuntatō eṉṉum aḷaviltāṉē atu amikiṉṟatu! iraṇṭaṭi nūṟpāviṟkup pala pakkaṅkaḷ uri viḷakkaṅ kāṇappeṟukiṟōm. atu paṟṟiya āyvu iṅkē namakkum tēviyilli. anta urinaṭiyiṉ amippiyum, pōkkiyum, aḻakiyum kaṇṭu mēlē cellalām.

talimakaḷ—pala nūṟu tōḻiyarōṭu kūṭi vāḻnta talimakaḷ —eppaṭit tamiyaḷāy niṟkiṉṟāḷ eṉpati viḷakkum nakkīrar nānalam kāṇa nalam payappatākum.

yāṅṅaṉam niṟkumōveṉiṉ, cantaṉamum caṇpakamum tēmāvum tīmpalāvum āciṉiyum acōkum kōṅkum vēṅkiyum kuravamum virintu, nākamum tilakamum naṟavum nantiyum mātaviyum mallikiyum meḷavaloṭu maṇaṅ kamaḻntu, pātiriyum pāvi ñāḻalum piṅ koṉṟiyum piṇi yaviḻntu, porippuṉ kumpuṉṉākamum murukkoṭu muki ciṟantu, vaṇṭaṟintu tēṉōrntu varikkuyilkaḷ icipāṭa, taṇteṉṟal iṭivirāyt taṉiyavari muṉivu ceyyum oru poḻilatu naṭuvaṇ; oru māṇikkac ceykuṉṟiṉ mēl, vicumpu tuṭittup pacuppoṉ pūttu, vaṇṭu tuvippat taṇtēṉ tuḷirppatōr veṟiyuṟu naṟimalar vēṅki kaṇṭāḷ; kaṇṭu periyatōr kātal kaḷikūrntu, taṉ cemmalarc cīṟaṭi mēl cilampu kiṭantu cilampu puṭippa, ammalar aṇikkompar naṭi kaṟpateṉa naṭantu ceṉṟu, naṟiviri vēṅki nāṇmalar koytāḷ; koytaviṭattu marakatamaṇi viḷimpaṭutta māṇikkac cuṉi maruṅkiṉatōr mātavi valli maṇṭapattup pōtu





vēynta pūnāṟu koḻuniḻaṟ kīḻk kaṭikkuruk kattik koṭi piṭittut takaṭupaṭu pacum poṟ cikaraṅkaḷiṉ mukaṭu toṭuttu vantu iḻitarum aruvi āṭakap pāṟi mēl, atirkural muraciṉ kaṇṇiraṭṭa, vaṇṭum tēṉum yāḻ murala, varik kuyilkaḷ ici pāṭat taṇtātu tavicupaṭap pōrttatōr paḷikkup pāṟi maṇittalattu mīmici, nīla ālavaṭṭam virittāṟpōlat taṉ kōlak kalāvam koḷa virittu, muḷi iḷañāyiṟu iḷaveyi leṟippa, ōr iḷamayilāṭuvatu nōkki niṉṟāḷ.”





eṉpatu nakkīrar vākku. ām! avaḷ niṟkum aḻakiṉik kāṭṭilum avaḷi īrttu āṭum mayiliṉik kāṭṭilum avaṟṟi viḷakkiya urinaṭiyiṉ aḻakaṉṟō aṉivar uḷḷattiyum kavarntu urukkukiṉṟatu.

uriyāciriyarkaḷ

iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalurikkup piṉ nāṭṭil pal vēṟu nūlkaḷukku urikaḷ eḻutappeṟṟaṉa. uri naṭi ilakkiyam vēṟu vakiyilum vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. avvaḷarccik kiṭiyil ettaṉiyō māṟṟaṅkaḷum ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷum iṭam peṟṟaṉa. vēṟṟumoḻik kalappiṉāl tamiḻ urinaṭi urumāṟiṟṟu—piṟaḻntatu—piṉ ciṟantatu. ettaṉiyō vakikaḷil urinaṭi vaḷaralāyiṟṟu. oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākat toṭṭuk kaṇṭu virintu nām pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ talivāyilukku varalām.

caṅkakālattilum ac caṅkam maruviya kālaṅkaḷilum piṉ iruṇṭa kālattilum pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. avaṟṟiṟkum tolkāppiyam eṉṉum ilakkaṇattiṟkum nāṭṭil uri kaṇṭa nallavarkaḷ pallōr. avarkaḷuḷ taliciṟantavarākap pōṟṟap peṟupavar pērāciriyar, nacciṉarkkiṉiyar, iḷampūraṇar, aṭiyārkku nallār, parimēlaḻakar, cēṉavariyar, teyvacciliyār, kallāṭar eṉpavarkaḷē. ivartam urikaḷ iṉṟaḷavum tamiḻi uriyiṭṭu ēṟṟaṅkaṇṭu pōṟṟum vakiyil amintuḷḷati yāvarē aṟiyātār? uccimēṟ pulavar koḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri, ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷatu. ivar tolkāppiyam muḻutukkumē uri eḻutiṉar eṉpar (atil kaṭici nāṉku iyalukku uri kāṇakkiṭikkavilli pōlum). iḷam pūraṇar, cēviriyar, pērāciriyar, teyvacciliyār, kallāṭar eṉṟa perumakkaḷ tolkāppiyattuḷ ciṟcila pakutikaḷukku uri eḻutiṉavarkaḷ eṉpati iṉṟum avartam urivaḻiyē kāṇkiṟōm. cīvakacintāmaṇi, pattup pāṭṭu, kalittoki eṉṟa kāppiya, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri eḻutiyuḷḷār. kuṟuntokikkum ivar uri eḻutiṉār eṉṟu aiyar avarkaḷ annūliṉ muṉṉuriyil kuṟittuḷḷārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiyaṉṟi iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷiṉ cilappatikārattukku aṭiyārkku nallārum, tirukkuṟaḷukkup parimēlaḻakarum eḻutiya urikaḷ ulakam uḷḷaḷavum vāḻum tiṟaṉ peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. kuṟaḷukkup patiṉmar urikaṇṭaṉar eṉavum avaṟṟuḷ cilavē kiṭikkappeṟṟaṉa eṉavum aṟikiṟōm cilampiṉ arumpata uriyāciriyarum nammāl pōṟṟat takkavaraṉṟāē? tirukkōviyār mutaliya camaya ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkum uri kaṇṭa perumi akkālattiyatē. ivvāṟu,tamiḻaka varalāṟṟiṉ iṭikkālattil paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷi ulakukku uṇarttavum avaṟṟi elliyaṟṟa kālam niṟivuṟṟu vāḻa vikkavum karutip pala nallavarkaḷ uri eḻutiṉarkaḷ. avarkaḷ tamiḻ urinaṭiyi vaḷarttatōṭu appaḻam perum ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum vāḻavitta perumikkum ilakkāki viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷaṉṟāē? ivartam urinaṭikaḷuḷ oru cilavaṟṟik kaṇṭu mēlē cellal poruttamāṉatākum. ivartam kālam ēṟakkuṟiyak ki. pi. patiṉērām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum patiṉāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭipaṭṭa kālamē eṉpar āyvāḷar. ōriruvar kālattāṉ muṉṉē cella vāyppum uṇṭu. ikkālattil tamiḻil vēṟṟu moḻikaḷ, ciṟappāka vaṭamoḻi, viravik kalantuḷḷamiyi nammāl kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu; eṉṟālum cila uriyāciriyarkaḷ paḻiyatilum paḻiyatāṉa vakiyil tam urippōkki amittuḷḷamiyi aṟikiṉṟāēm. ivar tam urikaḷ mūlaṅkaḷukku urikaḷē eṉṉum niṉivi maṟappittu, tāmē pērilakkiyaṅkaḷō eṉṉum niṉiviyum ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatip payiṉṟāēr naṉku uṇarvar. oru cila iṭaṅkaḷi nōkkiṉ 'nūlāciriyar ittakiya nuṇṇuṇarvuṭaṉ pāṭiṉarā? aṉṟi uriyāciriyar uḷavaṇṇamum uri vaṇṇamum ittakiya ēṟṟattit tarukiṉṟaṉavā?’ eṉṟu viyakkat tōṉṟum. eṉavē, uriyāciriyarkaḷ āyntaminta kalvi aṟivum muṭivilā āṟṟaluṭimiyum peṟṟamiyē avartam ciṟanta urikaḷi ulakil aṉṟu maṭṭumaṉṟi iṉṟum eṉṟum vāḻavikka utaviṉa—utavukiṉṟaṉa—utavum eṉpatu poruntum. oru cila kāṇalām.

iḷampūraṇar ilakkaṇattukku uri vakukkiṉṟār. kaḷaviyal mutaṟ cūttirattil tolkāppiyar tam nūlil mupporuḷi vitta muṟimiyi ivar,

‘aṟaṉum poruḷum iṉpamum' eṉṉātu ‘iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum' eṉṟatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, palavaki uyirkaṭku varum iṉpam iruvakippaṭum. aviyāvaṉa pōkam nukartalum vīṭu peṟutalum eṉa. avaṟṟuḷ vīṭupēṟu tuṟavaṟattil niṉṟark kallātu eytal aritāyiṟṟu. pōka nukartal maṉiyaṟattārk keytuvatu. avareytum iṉpamum avviṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇamāya poruḷum apporuḷukkuk





kāraṇamāya aṟaṉum eṉakkāriya kāraṇam nōkka vittār eṉka,





eṉa viḷakkuvatu ciṟantaṉṟāē?

'kiḷaviyākkam' eṉṟa toṭarukkup poruḷ kūṟa vanta cēṉāvariyar.

'vaḻukkaḷintu coṟkaḷiyākkik koṇṭamiyāṉ ivvōttu 'kiḷaviyākkam' āyiṟṟu. ākkam - amittuk kōṭal. noyyum nūṟuṅkum kaḷintu arici amittāri ariciyākkiṉar eṉpavākaliṉ. coṟkaḷ poruḷ mēl āmāṟu uṇarttiṉamiyāl kiḷaviyākkam āyiṟṟu eṉiṉum amiyum. potuvakiyāṟ kiḷavi eṉṟamiyāl, taṉi moḻiyum toṭar moḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum. kiḷavi, col, moḻi eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa vellām oru poruṭ kiḷavi





eṉa viḷakkuvār.

pērāciriyarō, tolkāppiyar kāṭṭa niṉitta poruḷ nilikaḷi naṉku uṇarntavar. avar pulliyum marattiyum tolkāppiyar kāṭṭiya vaḻiyē ceṉṟu kāṭṭukiṟār.

'puṟakkāḻaṉa eṉavē, avvaḻi veḷiṟeṉpa taṟiyappaṭum. avi paṉiyun teṅkum kamukum mutalāyiṉa pulleṉappaṭum: iruppiyum puḷiyum āccāvum marameṉappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam variyaṟi kūṟip payantateṉṉē? puṟattum akattum koṭi mutalāyiṉa kāḻppiṉṟiyum akiṉ maram pōl vaṉa iṭiyiṭip poypaṭṭum pullum maraṉum varuvaṉa uḷavāleṉiṉ-iraṇṭiṭattum ēkāram pirittuk kūṟiṉamiyālum 'eṉa moḻipa’ eṉṟu iruvaḻiyum ciṟappittu vitantamiyāṉum ciṟu pāṉmi aviyum pullum maraṉumeṉa aṭaṅku meṉpatu’





eṉpatu avar viḷakkam.

nācciṉarkkiṉiyar mullippāṭṭiṟku viḷakkaṅkūṟa vantakāli, ‘ippāṭṭiṟku mulliyeṉṟu peyar kūṟiṉār, mulli cārnta kaṟpup poruntiyataṉāl, illaṟam nikaḻttutaṟkup pirintu varuntuṇiyum āṟṟiyiruveṉṟu kaṇavaṉ kūṟiya collip piḻiyāmal āṟṟiyiruntu illaṟam nikaḻttiya iyaṟki mulliyāmeṉṟu karuti irutta leṉṉum poruṭara mulliyeṉṟu icceyyuṭku nappūtaṉār peyar kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, kaṇavaṉ varuntuṇiyum āṟṟiyiruntāḷākap poruḷ kūṟalē avar karuttāyiṟṟu. ‘tāṉē cēṟal’ (tol. aka. 27) eṉṉum vaḻiyāl, aracaṉ tāṉē ceṉṟatu ippāṭṭu’





eṉa viḷakkaṅ kāṭṭukiṉṟār. ivvāṟē, aṭiyārkku nallār, parimēlaḻakar mutaliya uriyāciriyarkaḷ kūṟiya uri viḷakkaṅkaḷ pala. vēṟupala uriyāciriyarkaḷum akkālattil palvēṟu nūlkaḷukku urikaḷ eḻutit tamiḻ urinaṭiyi vaḷarttaṉar eṉal poruntum. ittuṟiyil iṉṉum virippiṟ perukumātalāl mēlē cellalām.

kalveṭṭiṉ urinaṭi

irupatām nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ pala ārāyccikaḷuḷ oṉṟu paḻamiyit turuvi ārāytal. tamiḻnāṭṭup paḻiya varalāṟṟi avvāṟu turuvi ārāyap payaṉpaṭuvataṟku mikka utaviyāka iruppaṉa iṭikkālattil uṇṭāṉa kalveṭṭukkaḷē yākum. kalmēl veṭṭappaṭṭu iṉṟaḷavum vāḻkiṉṟa avvilakkiyaṅkaḷi ārāyntu iṉṟu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉar. avi pāṭṭum uriyum viraviyaṉavākavē amintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭup palvēṟu pakutikaḷilum kalveṭṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ muṟpakuti avvak kālattu aracartam pukaḻ parappum meykkīrttikaḷā yamiyap piṟpakutikaḷ perumpālum aṟamāṟṟum niliyi viḷakkuvaṉa. muṟpakuti perumpālum akavaṟ pākkaḷāl amiya, piṟpakutikaḷ urinaṭiyākavē uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭil paṭi eṭutta kalveṭṭukkaḷ cila; eṭukka vēṇṭuvaṉa pala. eṉavē, tamiḻ urinaṭi, cevivaḻiyāka vantu, ōli ēṭṭilēyum utaṭṭilēyum taṉ vāḻvi amittukkoṇṭa tōṭamiyātu, eṉṟum nilipeṟum kallilum taṉ vāḻvi amittu, akkaṟkaḷiyē taṉ pukaḻ pāṭum kavik kūṭaṅkaḷāka māṟṟik koṇṭatu. mikap paḻaṅkālan toṭṭu vanta inta vaḻakkam piṟkālac cōḻar pāṇṭiyar kālattil mikunta ucca niliyil iruntatu. nām kāṇa irukkum kāla elliyilum—ēṉ? iṉṟum kūṭak kallil urinaṭiyiyum pāṭṭiyum cetukkip parappum niliyik kāṇukiṉṟōm. cōḻa pāṇṭiyar kālak kalveṭṭukkaḷil, avaṟṟiṉ urinaṭi nalattik kaṇṭu mēlē cellalām. itu caṟṟē putiyatākavum māṟupaṭṭatākavumkūṭat tōṉṟum.

‘srī tirupuvanac carkira viṟttikaḷ srī rāca rāca tēvarukku yāṇṭu patiṉāṟāvataṟketirām āṇṭu nāḷ irunūṟṟeḻupattiraṇṭu kāṅkaya rāyaṉum tirumañcaṉam aḻakiyaṉum teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉum āḷuṭiyayiyaṉāyaṉāṟṟ kōvilukku srī kāriyam ceyvārkaḷum camutāyam ceyvāṟkaḷum kōvil nāyakam ceyvāṟkaḷum camutāyam ceyvāṟkaḷum ceyyat tiruvāy moḻintaruḷiṉapaṭi, uyyakkoṇṭār vaḷa nāṭṭu ampar nāṭṭu ampāruvanti arayaṉ civēta vaṉa perumāṉi toṇṭamāṉ perum peṟṟap puliyūṟ muṭittalik koṇṭa perumāḷ tiruvīti mēlit tiruvīti kīḻic cirakil yeṭuppitta aṟap peruñ celvi cāliyil uṇṇum ātulaṟku ariciyum kaṟi viṟaku uḷḷiṭṭa viñcaṉattukkum aṭuvārt taṇṇīr vārppāṟkkuñ cāli aḻuvu koravum





uṉṉiṭṭu vēṇṭuvaṉa viyarattukku iri mikuti koṇṭu ceyya ivaṉ kāṇiyāṉa nilattukkut tanta rācātirāca vaḷaṉāṭṭu pāṇṭūrāṉa kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ vallattu nilamāy rāca rācaṉ ampar yeṉṉum peṟavēṇu miṭukkac coṉṉa nilam nāṟpattinālēkālum patiṉārāvatiṉetirāmāṇṭu picāṉa mutal yiccālikkuk kāṇikkik koḷḷavum, inta nilattukku ivaṉ tanta cātaṉam tirukki oṭṭiyilē oṭukki vittukkoṇṭu ippaṭik kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷikiyilē kalveṭṭavum kaṭavatākap peṟavēṇum yeṉṟu ivaṉ namakkuc coṉṉamiyil yippaṭi ceyyak kaṭavatākac coṉṉōm. ippaṭi ceyyappaṇṇuvatu, yeḻutiṉāṉ tirumantira ōli neṟiyuṭiya cōḻa vēnta vēḷāyeṉṟum tiruvāy moḻintaruḷiṉār ivi cetirāyaṉ eḻutteṉṟum ivi viḻuppātirāyaṉ yeḻutteṉṟum ivi kāliṅkarāyaṉ yeḻutteṉṟum ivi cekutirāyaṉ yeḻutteṉṟum ivi kāṅkeyarāyaṉ yeḻutteṉṟum piratācam ceyitaruḷi vanta ceyyumpaṭi kalveṭṭiyatu.’[13]





maṇip piravāḷam

ikkalveṭṭuc citamparam kōyilil uḷḷatu eṉa aṟikiṟōm. itaṉ naṭiyil peru māṟutal nammāḷ uṇara muṭikiṉṟatu. eḻuttup piḻikaḷum nirampa uḷḷatiyum kāṇkiṉṟāēm. ituvari nām kaṇṭu vanta urikaḷukkum itaṟkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiṉiyum nammāl uṇara muṭikiṉṟatu. ivi kalveṭṭukaḷāṉamiyiṉ kallil cetukka ﻿muṭiyātaniliyilō aṉṟic cetukkiṉavaṉ nalla eḻuttaṟivaṟṟavaṉ eṉṟa kāraṇattālō ikkuṟi nērntirukkalām eṉa eṇṇavum muṭiyātu. tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟiṉ iṭikkālattilē ittakiya māṟṟaṅkaḷ moḻiyil maṭṭumaṉṟi vāḻvu, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam, camutāya nili iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟilum uṇṭākiyiruppatik kāṇamuṭikiṉṟamiyiṉ immāṟṟamum avaṟṟi oṭṭi vantatu eṉak koḷvatē poruntamākum. piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷum itil atikam iṭam peṟṟiruppatik kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. ēṟakkuṟiya ikkālattil eḻunta urinaṭi nūlkaḷilum ittakiya vāṭi vīcak kāṇalām. nālāyirap pirapantattukkup pērurikaḷ emunta kālattilē vaṭamoḻi atil atika iṭam peṟṟiruppati nammāl kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. ati ‘īṭu’ eṉṟu nām pōṟṟip pārāṭṭukiṉṟōm. anta naṭiyiyē maṇiyum pavaḷamum kalanta naṭi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭumpaṭiyāṉa ‘maṇippiravāḷa’ naṭi eṉa aḻikkiṉṟāēm. akkālattil attakiya naṭi eḻutuvatutāṉ ciṟantatu eṉa makkaḷum maṉṉarum karutiyirukkakkūṭum. tamiḻ urinaṭi varalāṟṟil itu oru tiruppamēyākum. eṉiṉum, inta naṭi atikamāka vaḻakkattil illi. viṇavap peru nūlkaḷukku aminta urikaḷilum, kalveṭṭukaḷilum vēṟucila camaya vētānta nūlkaḷilum innaṭiyik kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. inta amippum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭilum uḷḷatip piṉṉark kāṇalām. ataṟkumuṉ ‘maṇippiravāḷa’ urinaṭikkuc cāṉṟākavum-īṭṭuriyiliruntu iraṇṭoru pakutikaḷ iṅkē kāṇal poruttamāṉatākum.

teyvat tiruppācuramākiya tiruvāy moḻikku 'īṭu’ uyarnta uriyāka amikiṉṟatu. eṉṟālum viḷaṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyāta-piṟa urikaḷukkumāṟupaṭṭa-vakiyil maṇippiravāḷa naṭiyil atu amintakāraṇattālēyē nāṭṭil avvaḷavu viḷakkam peṟavilliyō eṉa eṇṇa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum nam urinaṭi vaḷarcci elliyil atuvum oru mil kallāka amikiṉṟa kāraṇattāl anta naṭiyilēyum nām oṉṟu kāṇal tēviyē. nam palkalik kaḻaka muyaṟciyiṉāl atu paṭittu uṇarattakka vakiyil veḷivantuḷḷami makiḻccikkuriyatē. ‘añciṟiya maṭanārāy’ eṉṟa pāṭṭiṉ ‘nalkittāṉ kāttaḷikkum’ eṉṟa toṭarukku uriya uriyik kāṇpōm.

‘(nalki) vipūti rakṣaṇam paṇṇumpōtu karttavya puttiyāvaṉṟikkē pēṟu taṉṉattākak kiṭīr rakṣippatu. eṉakkut taṉ pakkal uṇṭāṉa viyā mōkam taṉakku vipūtiyilē uṇṭāyiṟṟu. rakṣippatu (tāṉ) apēkṣippāriṉ ṟikkē yirukkat tāṉē rakṣikkum avaṉ. (poḻilēḻum) kīḻum mēlum oṉṟāka niṉittuc collukiṉṟatāl, sva carīra rakṣaṇam paṇṇuvatu snēka purassaramākiyiṟē. ‘nalki tāṉ’ ityāki) nāmarūpa vipākā narhamāy kiṭantavaṉṟu yāriṭamiruntu apēkṣikka itti uṇṭākkiṟṟu. caktiya vasta pirapi (nīṟupūtta neruppu) pōla, tāṉ eṉkiṉṟa collukku uḷḷēyāyt taṉṉiyiṭṭu vivarikkavēṇṭiṉa aṉṟu taṉmēlē ēṟiṭṭuk koṇṭu nōkkiṉāṉāyiṟṟu (kāttaḷikkum) kāttuk koṭukku meṉṉutal (‘viṉiyēṟkē nalkattā ṉākātō’) illātavaṉṟu uṇṭākkiṉāy, uṇṭākkiṉataṟkup palam karmatti ēṟiṭṭuk kiviṭukiyō? sva jaṉarakṣaṇam paṇṇavākātō? nāṭṭuk kiṭṭa niṉippu antappurattukku aritāka vēṇumō? (‘viṉiyēṟkē’) kaṭalilē ēṟṟa miṭṭut taṇṇīr aritāmpaṭiyāṉa pāvattip paṇṇuvēṉē. (‘nāraṇaṉik kaṇṭakkāl’) jīva camūkattiṉuṭiya svarūpa stitiyātikaḷ svātaṉamāy ivi prakāramāka, tāṉ pirakāriyāy, ivaṟṟilē oṉṟu kuṟiyilum tam





iḻavāmpaṭi irukkiyālē nārāyaṇaṉ eṉṟu virutuḻatit tirikiṟavarit kaṇṭakkāl, ippēr yōka rūṭiyō eṉṟiruntōm mahā viruṭcattōpātiyō eṉṟu kēḷuṅkaḷ (kaḷḷi, malkunīr ityāti) uṉceyal parārttamāy iruntatī: perukā niṉṟuḷḷa nīri uṭittāṉa nīr nilam uṇṭu—koṭit tōṭṭam atilē (iri tēr) peṭiyiṉ vāykku aṭaṅkuvatu tēṭā niṉṟatu. puḷḷup piḷḷikku iritēṭum eṉak kaṭavatiṟē? kayalukaḷā niṟkac ceytēyiṟē, piḷḷi vāykkaṭaṅkuvatu tēṭukiṟatu.’





itil kaṭavatiṟē, ceytēyiṟē eṉṟa collamippu muṟikaḷiyum vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi eḻuttukkaḷum appaṭi appaṭiyē eṭuttāḷap peṟuvatiyum kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu.

tiruppam

ēṟakkuṟiya patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ tamiḻ urinaṭi maṟupaṭiyum vīṟunaṭiyiṭṭu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu eṉalām. eṉiṉum, anta nūṟṟāṇṭil ciṟanta urinūlkaḷik kāṇa muṭiyavilli. āyiṉum, aṭutta nūṟṟāṇṭil kāṇum urinaṭiyi nōkkumpōtu ataṟkumuṉ nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṉ ceḻumi pulappaṭāmal pōkātu. piṟkālattiya uriyāciriyar niliyil ciṟu māṟutalkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟāēm. nūleḻutiyavarkaḷē uri eḻutal nalam payakkātu eṉṟa kāraṇattiṉalō ēṉō paḻaṅkālattil uriyāciriyarkaḷ nūlāciriyariṉum vēṟu paṭṭavarāka iruntu avvan nūl paṟṟiya āyvukaḷit teḷḷat teḷiya veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. āyiṉum, patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭi oṭṭi vāḻnta ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiya vittiyanāta tēcikar, cāmināta tēcikar mutaliyōr tāmē tam nūlkaḷukku uriyum eḻutiṉar eṉpar. ataṉ kāraṇattālētāṉ avi ittuṇi virivil vaḻakkoḻiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉavō eṉa niṉikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. akkālattil kallāṭattukku uri eḻutiya mayilēṟum perumāḷ eṉpavarum vāḻntiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ urikaḷiyellām īṇṭu viḷakkat tēviyilli. akkālatti oṭṭi vāḻnta caṅkara namaccivāyar naṉṉūlukkuc ciṟanta uri kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ivi aṉittum tamiḻ urinaṭikku ūṟṟamāka iruntaṉa eṉak koḷvatil tavaṟu illi.

mēli nāṭṭār

aṭuttu varum nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻnāṭṭil perum māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa. mēli nāṭṭu makkaḷ nam nāṭṭil vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭum aṭuttu āṭci ceytaṟ poruṭṭum kālūṉṟi vaḷara ārampitta kālam atu. avaṟṟuṭaṉ ammēli nāṭṭiliruntu kiṟattava camayattip parappa vanta pātiriyar cilar, tamnāṭṭu niliyiyiyum vāḻviyum viṭṭu, tamiḻ nāṭṭu aṟa vāḻviṉ aṭippaṭiyil makkaḷoṭu makkaḷāy vāḻntu, ēḻikaḷoṭu ēḻikaḷāka niṉṟu avarkaḷiṭiyil tam camayattip parappiṉar. avarkaḷ tamiḻiyum naṉku kaṟṟu, tamiḻilē makkaḷōṭu kalantu uṟavāṭi, tamiḻil-urinaṭiyilum pāṭṭilum-ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum tam camaya uṇmikaḷiyum eḻuti vittuḷḷaṉar. attakiya mēlināṭṭu aṟiñaruḷ akkālattavarkaḷākac ciṟanta vakiyil pōṟṟattakkavar rāparṭ-ṭi-nōpili eṉṟa iyaṟ peyaruṭiya tattuva pōtakarum, peski eṉṟa vīramāmuṉivarum āvar. avarkaḷ tamiḻ urinaṭiyi vaḷartta perumikku uriyavarkaḷ eṉṟum collalām. avartam urinaṭiyil vaṭamoḻi atikamāka viravik kāṇkiṉṟa kāraṇattāl, avarkaḷ innāṭṭil kōyilkaḷil vāḻnta arccakar pōṉṟu tāmum vāḻa niṉittārkaḷ eṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu appaṭiyē pōtakar, muṉivar eṉṟa peyarkaḷiyum pūṇṭu uyarntār eṉṟa niliyil piṟar niṉikka vāḻntaṉar. eṉiṉum avarkaḷ ēḻikaḷōṭu kalantu uṟavāṭi niṉṟa kāraṇattālēyē avartam camayattit tāḻanta makkaḷiṭam parappa muṭintatu. avaruḷ tattuva pōtakar patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil (20-5-1605) innāṭṭukku vantār. avar ceyta toṇṭukaḷ pala avi paṟṟiya āyvu namakkut tēviyilli. avar eḻutiya urinaṭi nūluḷ orucilavaṟṟi eṇṇalē cālum. avar, ñāṉa upatēca kāṇṭam, āttuma nirṇayam, puṉarceṉma ākṣēpam, tūṣaṇattikkāram, nittiya cīvaṉa callāpam, ñāṉatīpiki, pōtaka parīṭci, ñāṉa caṅkṣēpam, vicuvāca callāpam, upatēca callāpam, pattuc callāpam, ciluviyiṉ vicēṣa callāpam mutaliya pala nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiyuḷḷār. ivaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷik koṇṭē ivartam urinaṭi entavakiyil amintirukkum eṉpati ōraḷavu uṇaramuṭiyum. eṉiṉum ōriru mēṟkōḷkaḷik kaṇṭu cellalām. ivar tam nūlkaḷuḷ cila pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭilum (āttuma nirṇayam 1889; akkilāṉa nivāraṇam 1891) cila inta nūṟṟāṇṭilum accākiyuḷḷaṉa. ipperu nūlkaḷit tavirttu avvappōtu aminta ciṟuciṟu tuṇṭup piracuraṅkaḷ pōṉṟa pala ciṟu urinaṭit tāḷkaḷum eḻutiyuḷḷār eṉa aṟikiṉṟāēm ivar nūlkaḷilum ikkālattil vēṟu cila nūlkaḷilum māṟṟuc camaya māṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ malintuḷḷami kāṇkiṉṟāēm. inta aṭippaṭiyil 17ām nūṟṟaṇṭil pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷ vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa. avar urinaṭikku ōriru cāṉṟu kāṇalām.

piṟa matattiṉar tūṣaṇaṅkaḷi maṟukkum vakiyil aminta nūlē ‘tūṣaṇat tikkāram’ eṉpar. innūlil pala coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakkukaḷ namakku viḷaṅkātaṉavākak kūṭa uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ āyvu namakkut tēviyilli atiliruntu avaratu urinaṭikku oru ciṟu mēṟkōḷ māṭṭum kāṇalam. avar naṭiyil vaṭamoḻik kalap pu iruppatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, anta naṭi koccimoḻi naṭiyākavum amintuḷḷami aṟiyalām.

‘kaṭavuḷukku etirākac ceyyum pāvattik kaṭavuḷē maṉṉikka vēṇṭum. ātalālē karttarukku maṉuṣaṉivaṉ turōkiyumāy avaruṭiya iṣṭap piracārattip pōkkaṭittavaṉumāy irukkiyilē, ippaṭippaṭṭa karttarāṉavarukkup pāviyiṉalē paṇṇappaṭṭa kuṟṟamākiya pāvatti avar māttiram pōkkaṭikkiṟatukkum taṉ iṣṭap piracātatti maṉuṣaṉukkuk koṭukkiṟatukkum vallavarāy irukkiruroḻiya, vēṟe poruḷilē tēva iṣṭap piracātamāṉatu maṉuṣaṉukkup piratāṉa kāraṇamāṉa mēriyākak koṭukkappaṭalām eṉṟum allatu pāvattip pōkkaṭikkalām eṉṟum muḻumati keṭṭavaṉ coṉṉāl colluvā ṉoḻiyap puttimāṉāy irukkiṟavaṉ, appaṭippaṭṭa vārttiyip parikācattukku iṭamāy irukkiṟa mutirnta payittiyam eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭu varuvāṉ eṉkiṟatukkut tappātu.’





makkaḷōṭu makkaḷāyk kalantu paḻakiya ivarum piṟarum pala pēccu vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷi appaṭiyē āṇṭuḷḷaṉar. (pak. 401). ivar kālattil cātāraṇa vaḻakkattilirunta cila coṟkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷiyum aṟiyiṉ avi pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭil vaḻakkattil iruntami kāṇumpōtu, eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷa vāyppākum. (akōcaram—eṭṭātatu; aṅkiṣam—aṉṉappaṟavi. acaṉam-uṇavu; aṭici-ceṟintu; anumāṉam-uṭaṉ kaṭṭiyēṟal: am maruvātiyē—appaṭiyē; appiratāṉam—aṟpam; arccayam-kaṭṭiṉmi; iṅkirēci-āṅkilēyar; itamittal-iṉpuṟuttal; uttām-piṟaku; ekkaccakkam-tāṟumāṟu: kaṣṭam-malam;﻿kaṭṭu﻿ varkkam-āṭi﻿ aṇikalaṉ; cañcitam-putiyal; capalam-payaṉoṭu; carappaḷi-taṅkac caṅkili; cūcaṉi-pōtaṉi, cikkam-meṉmi; tappaḷi-tavaḷi; taruma carittiram-naṟceyal; tāvaṭam-kaḻuttaṇi, turitam-tuṉpam; pattuppattāy-kavaṉamāy, paṟaṅki-pōrttukkīciyar; paḷḷuvili-oru cāti (yāḻppāṇam) piramāṇikkam-oḻuṅku pūra-puka: pūta-puka; pūtilam-pūmi; pōrum-pōtum; macciyam-mīṉ; mokkiṣam-mōṭcam; vaṅkāram-poṉ; varccittu-vilakki; varuntu-muyaṟci cey; vittuvāṅkiṣar-kaviñar; virattar-piramaccāri, vituṣyam-kalvi; lāvakam-eḷimi; lēkkiṉ-eḻutu vōṉ; lēkiṉi-eḻutukōl; kṣūtika-keṭṭa).

iṉi, at tattuva pōtakar tam piracaṅkam oṉṟaṉi iṅkē kāṭṭi amiyalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

‘iṟṟivarikkum collappaṭṭa piracāraṅkaḷilē carvēcuvaraṉ uṇṭeṉṟum, oruttarāy irukkiṟāreṉṟum, tāṉāy aṉātiyāy carīramillāmal irukkiṟāreṉṟum, aḷavillāta cakala naṉmiyāy irukkiṟār eṉṟum, ñāṉattiṉalēyum, pelattiṉalēyum, kāraṇamāy eṅkum niṟintirukkirureṉṟum veḷippaṭuttiṉōm. inta pirakāramāy intap piracaṅkattilē carvēcuraṉ ellāttiṟkum ātikāraṇamāyirukkiṟār eṉṟu veḷippaṭuttuvōm.





‘ateppaṭiyeṉṟal, paramaṉ palattiyum, pūlōkattiyum atukaḷil uṇṭāyirukkiṟa cakala vastukkaḷiyum cakalattukkum vallavarāyirukkiṟa carvēcuvaraṉ uṇṭākkiṉāreṉkiṟatu avar ellāttiṟkum āti kāraṇamāyirukkiṟār eṉkiṟatukku... itilē carvēcuraṉivar inta ulōkatti uṇṭākkiṉar eṉṟum ati





uṇṭākkiṟapōtu etālē eppaṭi etukkāka

uṇṭākkiṉar eṉṟum collik kāṭṭuvōm.

(aintām piracaṅkam)’





ivar ‘callāpam’ eṉṟu kuṟikkum nūlkaḷellām viṉaviṭiyāka amikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ naṭi iṉṉum kocciyākavum mikac cātāraṇamākavum amintuḷḷatu.

kuru: iraṇṭāñ callāpattilē nammālē upatēcikkappaṭṭatellām naṉṟāyt teḷiñ cāyāṉāl, ippālum aṟiya vēṇṭiyati caṅkōcappaṭāmalk kēṭpāyāka;-



cīṣaṉ: parāpara vastuviṉiṭattilē paṭippuṇṭa puttikkuḷḷē aṭaṅkāta cattiyaṅkaḷ uṇṭeṉṟum appaṭippaṭṭa aṭaṅkāta cattiyaṅkaḷi aṟiya vēṇṭiṉatāyirukku meṉṟum aruḷic ceytīr. pirattiyakṣa tericaṉattilē yāṉālum (aṉumāṉa tericaṉattilē eṉkilum?) atukaḷip puttiyāṉatu ceṉṟu puṭikkak kūṭāmalirukkac ceytē, atukaḷi yentak kāraṇattiṉalē aṟiyalām?





ivvāṟāka avar naṭi cellukiṉṟatu. avar naṭiyik kaṇṭa atē niliyilē viramāmuṉivaruṭiya urinaṭiyilēyum iraṇṭoṉṟu kaṇṭu teḷiyalām, avar tam nūluḷḷum pala ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil accēṟiṉavēyām. ‘aṟaṉ’ eṉum poruḷ paṟṟi avar kāṭṭiya viḷakkam ciṟantatāka uḷḷamiyiṉ atik kāṇalām.

‘vētanūl mutalevvaki nūluk kallā tuṇaravuñ collāluṇarttavum vallavarāki, meyñ ñāṉat tirukkaṭalākiya vorumeyk kaṭavuṭaṉ ṟiruvaṭimalarē talik kaṇiyeṉak koṇṭētti, iruḷirāviṭattu viḷaṅkiyavoru





mīṉ pōlavum, pāliccurat taritalarnta patumam pōlavum meyyāñ curuti viḷakkātiruḷē moytta nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇuṅ kaṭavuḷēṟṟiya ñāṉat tiruviḷak kerippat teḷintu, uṇarn teṅkum oru viḷakkeṉa niṉṟuyarnta tiruvaḷḷuvaruritta palavaṟṟeṉṟi nāṉ terinturippat tuṇintēṉ. annāyaṉar tanta ‘payaṉ’ eṉum peruṅkaṭalāḻattiṉ mūḻkiyāṅkuṭi yarumaṇi yoruṅ keṭuttoru ciṟu ceppiṉaṭittāṟ pōlat tiruvaḷḷuvaratu payaṉellām virittup pakarumpaṭi nāṉ vallaṉallē ṉākayiṉ, akkaṭaṟṟuṟi nērntoru maṇiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṇarntēṉ. avar coṉṉa kuṟaḷiṉoṉṟē yiṅṅaṉa nāṉ vivarittu rippat tuṇintēṉ. aḵtāvatu,



maṉattuk kaṇ mācila ṉāta laṉittaṟaṉ

āka nīra piṟa



eṉpatu. illaṟat tuṟavaṟa meṉṟa ivviraṇṭaṉuḷḷum aṭaṅki niṟkumellāvaṟaṅkaḷum maṉattiṉ tūymiyāṟ peṟum perumiyē taruma meṉavum, maṉattiṉuḷ mācu koṇṭavaṉ ceyyun tavamum tāṉamum maṟṟi yāvu maṟat tiṉaravamāvataṉṟi yaṟattiṉ payaṉuḷavallaveṉavu makkuṟaḷiru payaṉiviyeṉa virittuk kāṭṭutum. virippavē, meyyum poyyum viḷakkiyuṭ payaṉṟaru meyyaṟattiṉ naṉmiyē veḷi yāviḵtoṉṟuṇarntu nāmataṟkoppa naṭantālitu vīṭeytum vaḻiyeṉak kāṇappaṭum. perum poruṇērntu poymmaṇi koḷvatu kēṭāyiṉum poruḷi nērntum uṭaliṉi vāṭṭiyum uyiri varuttiyu mēṟkati vīṭṭiṟ cellāc cila poyyaṟaṅkaḷi yīṭṭuvattiluṅ kēṭāmaṉṟāē? itaṉi vilakkit taṉatu





uyirākkaṅ kāppatu vēṇṭiyik kuṟaṭpayaṉarāy

vatu naṉṟē.





ivvāṟu mēlināṭṭuk kiṟittavarkaḷ patiṉēḻām nūṟṟaṇṭilum ataṟkup piṟakum tattam camaya uṇmikaḷip parappap palvēṟu vakiyil muyaṉṟu urinaṭi nūlkaḷi eḻutiya ñāṉṟu innāṭṭil tōṉṟiya aṟiñaruḷ cilarum ciṟanta uri viḷakkaṅ kaṇṭuḷḷamiyiṉiyum nammāl aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. avaruḷ ciṟantavar ‘civañāṉa muṉivar' āvar. ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattilum civa cittāntac camaya neṟiyilum ivar ciṟanta pulami peṟṟavar. ivartam civañāṉa pōta uri ‘civañāṉa pāṭiyam’ eṉap pōṟṟap peṟukiṉṟatu. ovvoru tuṟiyilum ivar ākkiyuḷḷa urikaḷ pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ talivāyilil niṟkum namakku nalla viḷakkaṅkakaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa.

camaya ilakkaṇa cāttira nūlkaḷ

caṅkaranamaccivāyar naṉṉūlukku nalla uri kaṇṭami mēlē kuṟittōm. anta uriyōṭu tam karuttiyum nirappi ivar anta viruttiyi muḻumi peṟac ceytār. atil oṉṟu kāṇalām:

makkaḷākap piṟantu puṇṇiya pāva miyaṟṟip puṇṇiya mikutiyāl tēvarāyum pāva mikutiyāl narakarāyum piṟattaliṉ, makkaḷ tēvar narakareṉak cātaṉa cāttira muṟiyē vittāreṉka. aṉṟiyum, āciriyar tolkāppiyar, “uyar tiṇi eṉ maṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē” eṉa muṉṉark kūṟip piṉṉar 'ivveṉa vaṟiyu mantan tamakkila’ vākiya 'teyvañ cuṭṭiya peyar' mutaliyaṉavum “uyartiṇi maruṅkiṟ pāl pirinticikkum' eṉak kūṟaliṉ, uyartiṇip pāṟ paṭuttu, “makkaḷ tēvar narakar uyartiṇi” eṉa muṟi terintu ōtiṉareṉak kōṭalumāmeṉka.





﻿ ivar tolkāppiyap pāyirattukkum mutaṟ cūttiraktiṟkum virutti uri ceytuḷḷār. aḵtu oru peru nūlākavē amintuḷḷatu. atil oru kāṭṭu:

iṅṅaṉam vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum payiṉṟārkku avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ oru nikarāṉavāya coṟkaḷum oṉṟāēṭoṉṟu ovvātatumākiyaceykivēṟu pāṭukaḷum coṉ muṭipu poruṇ muṭipu vēṟu pāṭukaḷum nōkkum vaḻi, ‘iviyellām vaḻā niliyō? cila vaḻuvamitiyō? vaḻuvō? kaṭṭaḷi yilliyō?’ eṉavum ācaṅki nikaḻu maṉṟē? avvācaṅki nīṅka eḻuttu mutal mūṉṟum ārāyntu centamiḻiyaṟki civaṉutaṟku, innūl oru taliyāṉ vēṇṭappaṭum eṉpatūum, avi payilātārkku ācaṅkiyē nikaḻāmiyiṉ innūl payaṉpaṭātu eṉpatūum viḷakkiya, ‘āyiru mutaliṉ nāṭic civaṇiya kaṇṭu eṇṇit tokuttāṉ’ eṉṟār. eṉavē, vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ārāyntu piṉṉar innūl kēṭṭaṟpāṟṟu eṉpatu pulappaṭṭatu.[14]



nāṭic civaṇiya, eṉpatu ‘ōti uṇarntāṉ’ eṉpatu pōlak kāraṇa kāriyap poruṭṭāy niṉṟatu. ‘civaṇiya’ eṉpatu ‘ceyyiya’ eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu viṉiyeccam. iḵtu uṇaramāṭṭātār, 'nāṭikkaṇṭu eṇṇit tokuttāṉ'eṉak kūṭṭi, oru viṉimutal viṉiyākki nāṭiya ārāycciyiṟ kuṟipāṭu uṭiyavaṟṟiṟku, muntu tuāl kaṇṭu eṉṟum, ‘āyiru mutaliṟ centamiḻ iyaṟki civaṇiya’ eṉak koṇṭu kūṭṭiyum iṭarp paṭupa.[15]



aṟṟēl ‘akattiyam niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ’ eṉṉātu, ‘aintiram niṟinta





tolkāppiyaṉ’ eṉṟa teṉṉi eṉiṉ, akattiyam niṟintami ellārālum teḷiyappaṭṭamiyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyilum vallavaṉāyiṉāṉ eṉpatu viḷakkiya aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉar eṉpatu. ivvāṟu aṉṟi aintirattiṉ vaḻit tōṉṟiya nūl eṉpatu viḷakkiya aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉavē, aintiram mutal nūl eṉṟu amin tiruppa muntu nūl kaṇṭu eṉak kūṟiyatu eṟṟukku eṉavum kūṟi maṟukka.[16]





ivar tam tirāviṭa māpāṭiyattukku oru cāṉṟu vēṇṭāmā? itō kēḷuṅkaḷ:

maṟippāvatu, anātiyāya mūlamalattiṉ kāriyamākaliṉ, atu mutalvaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭuṅ kāriyameṉpatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, kūṟutum-; mutalvaṉāvāṉ muṟṟuṇarvuṭimiyiṉ, ulakat tuyirkaḷ paṭuntuṉpa muḻuvatum uṇarntōṉākiyum, pērāṟṟaluṭimiyāṉ, uyirkaḷiyellām oruṅkē vīṭu pēṟaṭivikka vallavaṉākiyum, civaṉ, caṅkaraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷālum aṟiyappaṭuvatāy, viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi, ellā uyirkaṇ māṭṭum oppa nikaḻvatāy, toṭarpu paṟṟātu. piṟar tuṉpaṅkaṇṭuḻi ataṉi akkaṇamē nīkkutaṟku viḷiyum maṉaveḻucciyākiya peruṅ karuṇiyuṭimiyāṉ, avaṟṟi oruṅkē vīṭu pēṟaṭivittaṟkaṇ oruppāṭu mikkuṭiyaṉākiyum ittaṉmikaḷellāmuṭiya iṟivaṉ uyirkaḷāl aṉātimutal īṭṭappaṭṭup palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa tuṉpaṅkaḷiyellām viḷivikkum viṉi valiyiṉiyum, ellāp peruṅkoṭumikkum mūlamākiya cakaca mala caktikaḷiyum avaṟṟaṉ peṟuntuṉpaṅkaḷiyum keṭuttu, ellā uyirkaḷiyum oruṅkē vīṭupēṟu aṭiviyāmikkuk kāraṇam





kuṭaṅkiyiṉēntiya tīyiṉ cūṭṭiṉi nikaḻa voṭṭātu taṉakku māṟākiya mantira muccarikkuṅkāṟun taṭuttu niṟkuṅ kuḷiki pōla, ammutalvaṉic ciṟappiyalpāṉ uṇarum meyyuṇarvu tōṉṟuṅkāṟum avaṉatu peruṅkaruṇi uyir kaṇ māṭṭu oruṅkē nikaḻāta vaṇṇam taṭuttu niṟkum uyirk kuṟṟamākiya avvam mala caktiyiṉ vaḻi niṉṟu avaṟṟi naṭattuvatākiya avaṉatu caṅkaṟpamē yaṉṟip piṟitilliyeṉpatu peṟappaṭutaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam naṭattuvatākiya mutalvaṉatu caṅkaṟpamē īṇṭu maṟippeṉappaṭum (pāṭiyam, iraṇṭām cūttira uri, pak. 126)





nāṭkuṟippu naṭi

ivvāṟu tolkāppiyar kālantoṭṭup patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil tamiḻ uri vaḷarntu vantatu. iṉip pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭil urinaṭi vaḷarnta varalāṟṟik kāṇal naṉṟē. intap pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil ituvari vaḷarāta aḷavukkut tamiḻ uri naṭi vaḷarntuḷḷatu. atu evvevvakiyil evvettuṟiyil evvevvāṟu vaḷarntuḷḷateṉpatit toṭarntu iru nāṭkaḷilum kāṇa irukkiṉṟōm. iṅku avvāṟu urinaṭimikku vaḷarak kāraṇamāyirunta nāṭṭuc cūḻnili, araciyal, camayanili, poruḷ nili, piṟanilikaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟic caṟṟē eṇṇip pārkkalām. aviyē nāṭṭu ilakkiya vaḷarccikkum piṟa uṇarvukaḷukkum kāraṇaṅkaḷāka amikiṉṟamiyiṉ avaṟṟi eṇṇal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. ataṟkumuṉ patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil mēli nāṭṭār iṅku nilipeṟṟa kālattu nāṭṭil cilar mēṟkoṇṭa ‘nāṭkuṟippu’ eḻutum paḻakkattiyum eṇṇal cālum; avarkaḷum urinaṭi vaḷarttavarkaḷātaliṉ. avvāṟu kuṟippu eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷ ciṟantavar āṉantaraṅkam piḷḷi avarkaḷ. avar nāṭkuṟippiliruntu ōriru pakkaṅkaḷ ivi:

kālamē tuvakki cāyaṅkāla pariyantiram āṟkāṭu vēlūr vantavāciyilē yiruntum piṉṉiyum cuttup paṭṭilē yiruntum pirāmmaṇarum, kōmuṭṭikaḷum, tattuvātikaḷumāy vanta pēr āyiram caṉattukku atikamē uṇṭu. atiya ceṭṭiyār āṉanta ceṭṭi maṉuṣaṉ veḷḷic ceṭṭiyum nammuṭiya cakalāttu mattatukaḷi eṭuttukkoṇṭu vantu cērntār. avar coṉṉa cēti eṉṉa veṉṟāl, nicām piṉṉi irupatiyiairaṅ kutiriyuṭaṉ pirakālē varukiṟatākavum iṉṉē nāṟpatiyiairam aimpatiyiairam kutiriyi aṉuppi vittāreṉṟum antak kutirikaḷ kaṇavāykkiṭṭa vantirukkiṟateṉṟum kaṇavāyilē aptul napiyiṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷuṭaṉē caṇṭi ceytu aptul piḷḷikaḷi yellām muṟiñcupōy ataṉ pērilē aptul napikāṉ camātāṉam pēci lakṣattimpatiṉāyiram rūpāy tīrttuk koṇṭu tiramattukaḷellām koṭuttu pēṭṭi paṇṇikkoṇṭatākavum, marāṭṭiyar ippuṟam varukiṟatu ūrccitamāyt tāṉē coṉṉārkaḷeṉṟu coṉṉāl navāpu acaṉalli-makkō cāyapu ivarkaḷellām iraṇṭāyiraṅ kutiriyuṭaṉē pōy kaṇavāy makā pattiramāy kaṭṭik koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟum ippaṭippaṭṭa cētikaḷāyc coṉṉār. irukkiṟataṉāl kāriyamō pātiyāyirukkiṟa teṉṟu coṉṉar.





﻿★﻿★﻿★⁠

kālamē eṭṭu maṇikku ciṉṉa turiyavarkaḷukkup periya turiyavarkaḷ periya turittaṉam koṭuttu 21 pīraṅkip pōṭṭu cakalamāṉa veḷḷikkārar kumiceliyēr maṟṟap pēr aṉivarum vantu pēṭṭi paṇṇik koṇṭārkaḷ.

cāviyum oppikkappōṭṭu tammiṭa atikāramum avar vacam paṇṇiṉārkaḷ.



kālamē cīrmiyākiya pirāṉcukku oru kappal pāyeṭuttu ōṭippōṉāṉ antak kappal pēr piṉiccu. kappal kappittāṉ pēr mucēpu rōcēl. intak kappalilē ēṟṟiya taṭṭu 1001. ﻿ cemmaram pāram 2000,﻿ miḷaku pāram 600. piṉṉiyum cillaṟi tiṉucukaḷum ēṟṟi kālamē kappal pāyeṭuttuc cīrmikkup payaṇam pōṉāṉ. kōṭṭikkup patiṉoru pīraṅkiyum pōṭṭup pāyeṭuttārkaḷ.





ilaṅkiyil

tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumiṉṟi aṇṭi nāṭākiya ilaṅkiyilum 18 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urinaṭi irunta niliyik kāṇal nallataṉṟāē! ōriru mēṟkōḷ kaṇṭu cellalām;

irācaciyatatiṉa naṉamipa pakutikakāka veṇaṭi apapaṭika kotatavitamākaca ceyakiṟatu nalala teṉaṟu eṅkaḷukakuta toṟaṟapapaṭukutu ākāpaṭiyāla itatica cakalaruma aṟinatu koṇaṭirukakuma paṭikakuma itatika koṇṭu yātorutataruma aṟiyamāṭaṭōmeṉaṟu colalaka kūṭāta vitatatukakuma cakalamāṉa peruma taṅkaḷa taṅkaḷuṭiya naṭaṭatatukkupa paravacamāyirukakuma paṭikakuma ulānatā pāṣiyila uṭapaṭapa pāvikakapapaṭaṭa inta ūraṭa pāṣikaḷāluma inta eṅkaḷuṭiya paṇaka kātati iṅkeyuṭapaṭa itaṟakaṭutata koṭaṭi sarataṅkaḷilum piṟa citatama paṇaṇavum āṇiyaṟinatu vikakavuma paṭukiṟatuṇṭu.



ipapaṭikaku ilaṅkita tīvilē 97⁰⁰⁰5 āṇaṭu aṟapaci mātama 24 tikati koḻumapuka kōṭaṭiyilē koṭukakapapaṭaṭatu.





inta naṭi perumpālum anta nūṟṟāṇṭil vaḻaṅkiyatēyām; eḻuttukkaḷum perumpālum it taṉmiyākavē iruntaṉa. nām muṉṉar mēlē kaṇṭa tattuvap pōtakar, vīramāmuṉivar mutaliyavartam eḻuttukkaḷum inta vakiyilēyē amintiruntaṉa. iṉṟu avaṟṟit tirutti amippavarkaḷ puḷḷiyiṭṭum kompiṉic kaḻittum eḻutukiṟārkaḷ. ōliyil puḷḷi iṭal arumiyāṉamiyiṉ perumpālum puḷḷi otukkappaṭṭirukkalām. ‘ē’ ‘ō’ eṉṟu iraṇṭum muṉpu cuḻi yum kōṭumiṉṟi ‘ē’ ‘o’ eṉṟu puḷḷi peṟātu neṭilōci peṟṟamiyum ‘e’ ‘o’ eṉṟu puḷḷi peṟṟuk kuṟilōci peṟṟamiyum aṟivōm. ‘eytum ekaram okaram mey puḷḷi’ eṉpatu ilakkaṇamallavā? eṉavē uyirmey ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ kompiṉ cuḻi iṉṟi neṭṭoliyip peṟṟu varum vakiyilē avaṟṟi eḻutiṉar. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil acciṭap peṟṟa cila nūlkaḷilum inta ekara okara amippik kāṇa iyalum. ulāntakkārarkaḷ (Dutch) patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ iṭiyil ilaṅkiyil itupōṉṟu pala āṇikaḷi avar moḻiyilum ciṅkaḷa moḻiyilum eḻutiyatu maṭṭumiṉṟit tamiḻilum eḻutik kōṭṭi mutaliya potu iṭaṅkaḷil nili peṟumāṟu ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. nam nāṭṭuk kalveṭṭu, ceppēṭukaḷ pōṉṟu avi iṉṟaḷavum vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. 1727 ām āṇṭu mē 17 ām nāḷiṭṭa avartam maṟṟāeru ceppēṭṭiṉ (Palakkaat) vācakattil oṉṟiraṇṭu kāṉalām. (tiruntiya amippilēyē avaṟṟit tarukiṟēṉ; itil oṉṟilum puḷḷiyum neṭiṟkuṟiyum kiṭiyā).

3.﻿ itik kāṇkiṟa allatu vācikkiṟati kēṭkiṟa cakalamāṉa pērkaḷukkum ārōkkiyamāka aṟiyap paṇṇukiṟatāvatu.



4.﻿ inta ilaṅkiyilē kōppi yuṇṭu paṇṇu tavip pēr koñcak kālattilē niṉṟu





palapala campa (vī)ppukkaḷiṉālēyum pāraparippillātatiṉālēyu mettavun tēviyāyirukkiṟa kōppi yuṇṭākkutal.



[17]

ivvāṟu atu toṭarntu cellukiṉṟatu. itaṉ amippiṉiyum col vaḻakkiṉiyum piṟa oliyiyalpukaḷiyum annāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ āyntu varukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻnāṭu, ilaṅki eṉṟa mēṟkaṇṭa iru nāṭukaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟit tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya-tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻnta malēyā, teṉṉāppirikkā, parmā mutaliya palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilē tamiḻ urinaṭi inta vakiyil vaḷarcci aṭintatu eṉpati naṉku kāṇalām. avaṟṟiyellām īṇṭu nām tokuttu viḷakkappeṟiṉ, avi elliyaṟṟup perukikkoṇṭē cellumātaliṉ, tolkāppiyar kālan toṭaṅkip pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ talivācalil kālvikkum variyil tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarnta varalāṟṟu āyviṉi inta aḷavōṭu niṟutti amivōm. iṉit tamiḻ urinaṭi mika atikamāka vaḷarnta kālam pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉa aṟivōm. ataṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷiyum cūḻalkaḷiyum ārāyvōm.

nāṭṭu nili

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum ataṟkumuṉ oru cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilum tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟilum intiya varalāṟṟilum perum māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiyiruppatik kāṇkiṟōm. muculīm āṭciyil nāṭṭut talimi 'tilli'yil amiya, ataṉ aṭippaṭiyil, palar, nāṭṭiṉ palvēṟu pākaṅkaḷiyum aṅkaṅkē iruntukoṇṭu āṇṭārkaḷ. mēli nāṭṭilēyiruntu pōrccukkēciyarum avarukkuppiṉ ṭaccukkārarum pireñcukkārarum, aṅkilēyarum oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭu innāṭṭil kālvittu, piṉ tam vaḷamperutki, piṟaku āṭciyiyē iṅku nili niṟuttap pōṭṭiyiṭṭaṉar. mēlum kiṟittava camayam parappa vanta pātirimāruḷ palar tamiḻ nāṭṭil vantu palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil taṅki, avartam camayam vaḷarat takka vakiyil pāṭupaṭṭaṉar eṉpatiyum muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. avarkaḷuṭaṉ﻿ putuvakiyāṉa cātaṉaṅkaḷum vaḷaralāyiṉa. ōliyil﻿ eḻutiya nilimāṟik kākitattil eḻutavum,﻿ kākitattil accaṭikkavum takka palvēṟu vacatikaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. nāṭṭup poruḷātāra, camutāya, araciyal vāḻvu muṟikaḷilum palvēṟu vēṟupāṭukaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. nila aḷavi, makkaṭ pirivu mutaliyavaṟṟilum cila vēṟupāṭukaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭil iṉṉum uṇṭāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷ ettaṉi ettaṉiyō! ivaṟṟukkiṭiyil aracāṅkattārum camayat talivarkaḷum camutāya amippāḷarkaḷum piṟarum tattam koḷki, camayaneṟi, vāḻviyal, araciyal mutaliyavaṟṟi makkaḷiṭiyē parappa muyaṉṟaṉar. anta muyaṟcikku innāṭṭu moḻiyē aṭippaṭit tēviyāka amintatu. piṟa nāṭṭu aracāṅkam kālkoḷḷat toṭaṅkiya annūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilē makkaḷ tam tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻilēyē avaruṭaṉ toṭarpu koḷḷa muṭintatu. ataṟkup pāṭṭu naṉku payaṉpaṭavilli; uri naṭiyē uṟṟuḻi utaviṟṟu. eṉavētāṉ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil urinaṭi tamiḻnāṭṭil atikamāka vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. mēlum kalvi muṟiyum, kati eḻututal, nāḷitaḻ, kiḻami, tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉa acciṭṭu makkaḷiṭi aṟivip parappum nalliyakkamum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭil naṉku vaḷarcciyaṭittamiyik kāṇkiṟōm. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyilēyum pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷi ulakukku uṇarttap pala nūlkaḷukkuciṟappāka aṟanūlkaḷukku urikaḷ eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉar kāviyaṅkaḷāka irunta pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷi ellā makkaḷum uṇarntu koḷḷumāṟu ‘vacaṉa’ naṭiyil eḻutiya aṟiñarum palar paṇṭamāṟṟu, viṟpaṉi mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi ēṟpāṭu ceytukoḷḷavum, viṇṇappam aṟikki mutaliyaṉa amiyavum urinaṭiyē iṭantantatu. aṟiviyal vaḷarcci paṟṟiya palvēṟu karuttukkaḷi makkaḷiṭam parappat tuṇiceytatum ivvurinaṭiyēyākum. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil palvēṟu cūḻalkaḷāl urinaṭi vaḷarntatōṭu, taṉ vaḷarcciyuṭaṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭil palvēṟu amippukkaḷiyum kalikaḷiyum camayaneṟi muṟikaḷiyum aṟiviyal uṇmikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum vaḷarttu utaviyatu.

pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ

tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ itayamākiya tañci, 1799-il-pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ talivāyilil-marāṭṭiya maṉṉariṭamiruntu āṅkilēyar kiyil vīḻntatu. ataṟku muṉpum piṉpum tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatum aṇṭi nāṭukaḷākiya micūr, āntiram mutaliya pakutikaḷum avar vacamāyiṉa. tañciyil vāḻnta carapōci maṉṉaṉ (1798-1833) taṉ vāḻnāḷil moḻikkum aṟivu vaḷarccikkum pala ākkap paṇikaḷ ceytuḷḷāṉ. iṉṟum tañciyiluḷḷa perunūl niliyam-carasvati mahāl nūl niliyam - avaṉ uruvākkiyatēyākum. eṉavē nam āyvil amiyum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil uṇṭāṉa anta nūlniliyam iṉṟum nilittu-iṉiyum ōṅki vaḷarattakka vakiyil-palvēṟu tuṟikaḷil nāṭṭu makkaḷ aṟivu ākkam peṟap paṇi purivataṟiya makiḻcci uṇṭākiṉṟataṉṟāē? akkālattilēyē atil tamiḻ, vaṭamoḻi mutaliyavaṟṟil 22000 nūlkaḷ iruntaṉavām. nāṇayap poruṭkāṭciyum avaṉ amittiruntāṉ. pala nāṭakaṅkaḷ eḻutavum avaṉ utaviṉaṉ; antaṇarkaḷukkut tāṉaṅkaḷ ceytāṉ. kirāmaṅkaḷil ‘nāṭṭuk kaṇakku’ amikka avaṉ uṇṭākkiya kirāma muṉucīp, kaṇakkappiḷḷi amippē iṉṟaḷavum vāḻntu varukiṉṟatu. nāṭṭil pala pākaṅkaḷilum nila amippu, nilappirivu, nilatāṉam aḷittu variyaṟuttal ākiya pala cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ anta nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil āṅkilak kampeṉi āṭciyāḷarāl ēṟṟuc ceyalākkappeṟṟaṉa. akkālatti oṭṭiyum ataṟkumuṉ āṇṭa vicayanakara, nāyakka maṉṉar āṭciyi oṭṭiyum atuvari nāṭṭil kāṇappeṟāta nāṉāvita cātikaḷ nāṭṭil uṇṭāyiṉa. avi paṟṟiya varalāṟu mutaliyaṉavum tamiḻ urinaṭiyil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

vantavar kaṟṟaṉar

vantavaruḷ piṟa nāṭṭavarākiya āṅkilēyar innāṭṭi āḷat toṭaṅkumpōtu ōr uṇmiyi uṇarntaṉar. mutalil ‘nāṭṭu makkaḷ tāy moḻiyilēyē tam vaḻiyi amittuk koḷḷalē nalla vaḻi’ eṉa avarkaḷ uṇarntaṉar, camaya varalāṟṟu uṇmikaḷi ārāyntu uṇarvār kaṇṭa muṭivum atutāṉē.[18] inta aṭippaṭiyil āḷavanta āṅkilēyar maṭṭumiṉṟi, camayam parappa vanta camaya pōtakarkaḷum tam ceyalkaḷiyum eḻuttukkaḷiyum tamiḻarōṭum tamiḻōṭum iṇittuk koṇṭaṉar. ivvuṇmiyip patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil camayap pōtakarāka vanta tattuva pōtakar (Robert de Nobili) vāḻvik kuṟikkumpōtu, maṟit tiru ca. irācamāṇikkam avarkaḷ kuṟittuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻ makkaḷōṭu paḻaka iṉṟiyamiyātu tuṇi puriyum tamiḻ moḻiyi irāppakalākap payiṉṟār. āṟu mātattiṟkup piṟaku evar utaviyumiṉṟi entat talippup paṟṟiyum pala maṇik kaṇakkāka uriyāṭa avarāl muṭintatu. anta aḷavukku moḻip payiṟci peṟṟar. .. tamiḻ moḻi muṉivaratu maṉattik kavarntatu. ‘aḻaku mikuntu vaḷappam porunti mikavum paṇpaṭṭu viḷaṅkuvatu’ eṉṟu mēli nāṭṭavarukkut tamiḻi aṟimukappaṭuttukiṟār. virivil tāy moḻiyāṉa ittāliya moḻiyum, paṭitta pōrttukkīciya moḻiyum maṟantu pōyiṉa. avaṟṟiṟkup patilākat tamiḻmoḻi oṉṟē avar karuttukkaḷi veḷiyiṭum karuvi moḻiyāka amiyalāyiṟṟu. immoḻiyil pēcuvatōṭu amiyātu 1607ām āṇṭu toṭaṅki 1656ām āṇṭu caṉavari 16ām nāḷ–uyir tuṟakkum vari ariya periya nūlkaḷit tamiḻilum camaskirutattilum teluṅku moḻiyilum iyaṟṟiṉar.[19]





inta nili tattuva pōtakar māṭṭu maṭṭumiṉṟi, anta nūṟṟāṇṭilum aṭutta ōriru nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilum intiyāvukku vanta mēli nāṭṭu aṟiñar māṭṭu uṇmiyāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu. inta aṭippaṭiyil, nām mēli nāṭṭiliruntu, palvēṟu vakiyil ittamiḻ nāṭṭukku vanta mekkaṉci (Mackenzie), vilcaṉ (H. M. Wilson) jāṉ murṭāk (John Murdock) mutaliya aṟiñartam ariya toṇṭiṉik kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil nilaviya palvēṟu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiyum, urinaṭi vaḷarcciyiṉiyum, piṟanāṭṭu eḻuttukkaḷiyum ivarkaḷ vaḻiyē nammāl aṟintukoḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.appaṭiyē ivar pōṉṟāēr—ciṟappāka kālṭuvel, pōp, vīramā muṉivar, cīkaṉpālku mutaliyōrāl tamiḻiṉ ciṟappu, toṉmi, vaḷaṉ. vaṟṟānili paṟṟiyum aṟintukoḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu. ﻿ivarkaḷip paṟṟiyellām toṭarntu kāṇa irukkiṉṟāēmātaliṉ iṉṟu inta aḷavil ivarkaḷippaṟṟi niṉṟu mēlē cellalām.

mēli nāṭṭu āṭciyāḷarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻar perumpāṉmiyiṉarāka vāḻnta ilaṅki, malēyā mutaliya pakutikaḷilum tamiḻil aṟikki mutaliyavaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. ilaṅki aṟikki oṉṟi muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. maṟṟum irayilvē, tapāl, tanti mutaliya vacatikaḷ uṇṭāṉa kāraṇamum kaṭanta nūṟṟāṇṭil uṇṭāṉa palvēṟu nāṭṭu, camutāya māṟutalkaḷum tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷara utaviṉa. mēli nāṭṭiṉar vāṇipam, toḻil, vaṅki (paṇavakam) mutaliyaṉa vaḷartta kāraṇattālum eṭi (niṟuttal, mukattal), aḷavu, nāḷ kāṭṭi, pañcāṅkam, nāṇayam mutaliyaṉa nāṭṭu nilikkēṟpa aminta puḻakkattil vantu viṭṭatālum avvattuṟi paṟṟiya tamiḻ aṟikkikaḷ veḷi varak kāraṇamāyiṉa; aviyum perumpālum urinaṭiyilēyē amintaṉa. iviyellām tippucultāṉ kālattilum ataṟku muṉpum iruntaṉa veṉṟālum, 1799-il carapōci-vellasliyiṉ tañci oppantattukkup piṟakē—tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ perumpakuti āṅkilak kampeṉi āṭcikku vanta piṟakē —ceppam ceyyap peṟṟaṉa; ivaṟṟuṭaṉ aṟaṅkūṟu aviyaṅkaḷākiya nīti maṉṟaṅkaḷum, nāṭṭāṭci, ūrāṭci, nakarāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷum tōṉṟi vaḷarntu nilipeṟṟa kāraṇattāl avvattuṟi paṟṟiya aṟikkikaḷ, viṇṇappaṅkaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa tamiḻ uri naṭiyil tōṉṟa, urinaṭi vaḷarcci peṟṟatu.

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil nāṭṭu makkaḷōṭu paḻakiya mēli nāṭṭār–ciṟappāka āṅkilēyar tām—ivvāṟu innāṭṭu makkaḷip paṟṟiyum avartam camutāyac cīrkēṭukaḷip paṟṟiyum kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. “nalla iyaṟki vaḷamuṭiya nāṭṭu makkaḷ pala cūḻalkaḷāl—vāḻkki amippu muṟikaḷāl camutāya vēṟupāṭukaḷāl nilikeṭukiṉṟaṉar”[20] eṉṟum, “araca araṇmaṉiyik kāṇātu ēḻi makkaḷ kuṭiciyik kāṇal vēṇṭum; avarkaḷukku urimi vaḻaṅkum āṭci amiya vēṇṭum”[21] eṉṟum, “makkaḷ aṭimi vāḻvu nāṭṭu vāṇipatti vaḷarkkavilli; ammakkaḷiṭiyil paṭikkum niliyilum kalvi amippilum ēṟṟat tāḻvu kūṭātu“[22] eṉṟum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil āḷuvōr koḷḷa vēṇṭiya varaṉmuṟi, nāṭṭunili mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷakkiyuḷḷaṉar. intac cūḻalilētāṉ nāmum nuḻintu anta nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarcciyik kāṇa niṟkiṉṟāēm. 1797 ām āṇṭil āṅkilēyar innāṭṭup palvēṟu vaki nilikaḷi ārāya oru kuḻuviṉi ēṟpaṭuttip pala uṇmikaḷik kaṇṭaṉar. civil, kirīmiṉal amippu muṟi cemmi ceyyappeṟṟa kālamum atuvē.

kalvi, toḻil mutaliyalaṟṟilum akkālam, nāṭu talitūkki vaḷara ārampitta kālamākum. kiṟittuva camayattavar akkālattil nāṭṭiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷilum kāl koṇṭu vaḷarat toṭaṅka, 'celviruntōmpi varuviruntu nōkkum' tamiḻ makkaḷum maṉṉarum avarkaḷ irukkavum vaḷaravum tam camayatti vaḷarkkavum utaviṉar. mēlē kaṇṭapaṭi nam nāṭṭiṟku vanta palvēṟu mēli nāṭṭu makkaḷ innāṭṭuk kali, vāḻvu, ceyal muṟikaḷi ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭut tāmum payaṉ peṟalāyiṉar. nāṭṭu āṭci muṟiyilēyum pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ nērntaṉa. 1784 il kavarṉar–jeṉaralukku muḻu urimi tantaṉar ‘kampeṉiyār’. 1858 il vikṭōriyā makā rāṇiyār nēraṭiyāka nāṭṭāṭciyi ēṟṟavariyil, kampeṉiyiṉ kīḻ, kavarṉar–jeṉaral āṭciyē toṭarntu vanteṉa aṟikiṟōm.

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭil nāṭṭu nīti paṟṟiya pala tiruttaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. palappala caṭṭaṅkaḷum nīti maṉṟaṅkaḷum uṇṭākkap peṟṟaṉa. avi paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ pala tamiḻil — urinaṭiyil — potu makkaḷ uṇarntu koḷḷa avvappōtu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 1802-06 il nāṭṭu nīti paṟṟiya potu amippuc caṭṭamē uruvāyiṟṟu. 1700il civil kirimiṉal caṭṭam vantapōtilum atu cemmiyākkap peṟṟatu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilēyām, ceṉṉi mānilam 1,40,000 caturamil uṭiyatāy 1808 il (pala moḻi mānilamāy) amikkap peṟṟatu, aracāṅkak kavarṉar, iruvar allatu mūvar koṇṭa kuḻuvōṭu tam āṭciyi naṭattiṉar. ‘cūri’ (Jury) muṟi 1827 il nāṭṭil koṇṭuvarap peṟṟatu. 1816 il naṭuvar, kuṟṟa vicāraṇiyāḷar (Judge & Magistrate) vēṟupaṭuttap peṟṟaṉar. 1845 il māvaṭṭa nīti maṉṟaṅkaḷ uruvāyiṉa. nila urimiyum cemmip pakuppuc caṭṭamum (Land Settlement) 1820-27 il uruvāyiṉa. 1833 il nāṭṭu makkaḷ yārum eppataviyum peṟalām eṉac caṭṭattāl urimi vaḻaṅkiṉar. āṅkilēyap pārāḷumaṉṟa nēraṭi āṭci 1858 il toṭaṅkiṟṟu kirāma āṭci amippu 1877 il uruvāyiṟṟu. ireyil vēkkaḷ 1853 il tōṉṟalāyiṉa. intiya pīṉalkōṭu caṭṭam 1861 il amikkap peṟṟatu. ivvāṟu nāṭṭāṭciyilum āṇi aṟap pirivukaḷilum uṇṭāṉa palvēṟu māṟutalkaḷit tamiḻ urinaṭiyilē avvap pōtu vanta aracāṅka itaḻkaḷilum, taṉi aṟikkikaḷilum veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ orucila piṉpu kāṇalām.

accuppoṟi

moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭikku ātāramāṉa maṟṟeṉṟi īṇṭu eṇṇalām. inta āyvukkuriya nūṟṟaṇṭil moḻi vaḷarcciyi aḷavil atikamāka vaḷartta perumi accup poṟikkum uṇṭu. mēli nāṭṭiliruntu kiṟittuva camayam vaḷarkka vantavarkaḷ tam camayam vaḷarkka vēṇṭi nūlkaḷ acciṭa, cila accuccālikaḷi muṉṉarē amittaṉar eṉṟālum inta nūṟṟāṇṭil atu perukiṟṟu. intiyāvilēyē mutal mutal accāṉa nūl tamiḻ nūlē (6–9–1556 il mutal mutal kōvāvil accakam amikkappeṟṟatu). 1578 ām āṇṭilē intiyāvilēyē kollattil ceyyappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷikkoṇṭu (Indian Type of Malabar letters) ōru nūli mēlik kaṭaṟkariyil (ampalakkāṭu) acciṭṭaṉar (akkālattil mēli nāṭṭiṉar tamiḻ nāṭṭi ‘malapār’ eṉṟum tamiḻi ‘malapār moḻi’ eṉṟum aḻittaṉar). anta mutal nūl kattōlikkar nūlākiya ‘tampirāṉ vaṇakkam’[23] eṉpatākum. ataṉ pirati oṉṟu amerikka aikkiya nāṭṭil hārvarṭu[24] palkalik kaḻakattil uḷḷatām. piṉ 1586 il vēṟucila nūlkaḷ accāyiṉa. nakaraṅkaḷilum ceṉṉiyiltāṉ 1711 il mutal mutal accakam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. (kalkattāvil 1779 il uṇṭāyiṟṟu.) 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭil patiṉ mūṉṟu urinaṭi nūlkaḷum 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 21 urinaṭi nūlkaḷum ākiyavi, tamiḻk kattōlikka nūlkaḷāka veḷiyāyiṉa eṉak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. taraṅkampāṭi[25], yāḻppāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum ceṉṉiyilum kiṟittuva camaya nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil pala accāyiṉa. taraṅkam pāṭiyil cīkaṉ pālku jayar (1683-1719) nalla nūlkaḷi acciṭṭaṉar. 3–1–1714 il putiya ēṟpāṭu acciṭappeṟṟatu. 1761 il putuviyil accakam tōṉṟiṟṟu. ceṉṉiyil putitāka 1786 il vēppēriyil accakam uṇṭāka atil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷ accāyiṉa. akkālattiya acceḻuttukkaḷ 1870 vari vaḻakkattil iruntaṉa. itaṉ vaḷarcci aṭippaṭiyi ārāyiṉ ceṉṉi mānilattil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil cumār irunūṟu (198) accakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉak kāṇa ḻuṭikiṉṟatu. taṉiyār maṭṭumiṉṟi aracāṅkac cārpāṉa kuṟippukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi acciṭa, avarkaḷum accakaṅkaḷ amittami aṟikiṟōm. avvaccakaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻi veḷi vanta itaḻ tūlkaḷiyum paṟṟi ārāya 1878 mutal 1900 vari irakaciyat taṉik kuḻukkaḷ (C. I. D.) iruntaṉavām. ivipaṟṟi yellām ciṟu nūlkaḷum āyvukaḷum taṟpōtu veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.[26]

itaḻkaḷ

inta accaka aṭippaṭiyil palvēṟu itaḻkaḷum ceytittāḷkaḷum ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭil veḷi vantaṉa. tamiḻil irupatukku mēlāṉa itaḻkaḷ iruntaṉa. aracāṅka itaḻkaḷ āṅkilattil maṭṭumiṉṟi, tamiḻ mutaliya innāṭṭu moḻikaḷilum viḷakkam tantaṉa. avvappōtu uṇṭāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ, vitikaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷit tamiḻ moḻiyil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.mutalil itaḻkaḷ veṟum ceytikaḷ maṭṭum tāṅki veḷivantaṉa. 1775 il mutal muyaṟci naṭipeṟṟatu. piṟaku mutal itaḻ car ārcipālṭu kāmpal (Sir Archibald Compball) kavarṉarāka irunta kālattil (1786—1789) perumpālum āṅkilamum ōraḷavu tamiḻum koṇṭu veḷivantatu[27] (intiyāvil mutal āṅkila itaḻ 1780 il kalkattāvil veḷivantatu). 1830 vari tamiḻil taṉi itaḻkaḷ toṭaṅkap peṟavilli. ataṟkup piṟaku, cila itaḻkaḷ veḷivantaṉa. (vaṅkāḷa moḻil tāṉ mutal nāṭṭu moḻi itaḻ 1816 il veḷivantatu). ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ talivāyilil, 1799il (13—5—1799), kavarṉar-ceṉaral,pattiriki urimiyāḷar, āciriyar mutaliyōr paṟṟiya kuṟippukakaḷ (kaṭici pakkam acciṭuvatu) veḷiṭa vēṇṭiya uttaravip piṟappittaṉar.aracāṅka veḷiyīṭu (Fort St. George Gazette) 4—1—1832 il veḷivantatu. akkāla āṭciyāḷar lūṣiṅṭaṉ (S. R. Lushington) āvār. ittakiya itaḻkaḷ paṟṟi aracāṅkam ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil palvēṟu kaṭṭupāṭukaḷi uṇṭākkiyum, taḷarttiyum, vaḷarttum vantuḷḷatu. avaṟṟāl ciṟcila vēḷikaḷil nalivum tāḻvum uṇṭāṉa pōtilum akkāla uṇarvuṭi makkaḷiṉ viṭāmuyaṟciyē iṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭil eṇṇaṟṟa tamiḻ itaḻkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉvaḻit tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarcciyiyum kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. avaṟṟukkup palavaki añcal caluki mutaliyaṉavum tarappeṟṟami kāṇkiṉṟōm. ilavaca añcal muṟiyum cila kālam iruntirukkiṟatu.

ceṉṉi mānilattil tamiḻilum teluṅkilum āṅkilattilum cila pattirikikaḷ veḷivaralāyiṉa, vittiyārtti (1838 allatu 40), intu (1878), cutēcamittiraṉ (1882), meyil (15–12–1867), rājavartti pōtiṉi (1855), tiṉavarttamāṉi (2–12–1814), kiṟistava maṟuppu (1814) mutaliyaṉa avi. kāṅkiras 1885 il tōṉṟiya piṟaku urimi uṇarccimikkut tamiḻil pala kaṭṭurikaḷ vantaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu muḻutum pattirikik kaṭṭuppāṭu atikamāka iruntatu eṉalām. 'kampeṉi' āṭciyil (1785-1857) pala kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. 1816 vari atikak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ iruntaṉa; 1818-23 il cila taḷarttappeṟṟaṉa. 1823–28 vari mikak kaṭumiyāka iruntatu. 1835–57 il pala cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ ceyyappeṟṟaṉa. āṅkilap ﻿pārāḷumaṉṟa nēraṭi﻿ āṭcikku vanta piṟakum (1857) ittuṟiyil pala māṟāṭṭaṅkaḷ vantaṉa.[28] ivikaḷukkiṭiyil pala itaḻkaḷ tamiḻiyum tamiḻ urinaṭiyiyum vaḷarttaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi ilaṅki, malēyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum itaḻkaḷ pala tōṉṟi vāḻntaṉa.

camayat tuṟi

camayat tuṟiyum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ vaḷarccikku–ciṟappāka urinaṭi vaḷarccikku–utaviyatu. kiṟattavar accaka vaḻiyum viviliya nūlmoḻi peyarppu vaḻiyum ceyta toṇṭiṉik kaṇṭōm. tañcic carapōci accamayattārukku naṉku utaviṉaṉ. kiṟittava camaya accakam (Society for the Propagation of Christian knowledge) 1711 il accakam amittatu. tam camaya nūlkaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭatu. annūlkaḷ yāvum uri naṭi nūlkaḷē. cetvārṭ (Setwartz 1728–1798) pala vakiyil ittuṟiyil uḻittami kāṇkiṟōm. nākiyil ṭēṉiṣ miṣaṉ, taraṅkampāṭi accakam, putuviyil accukkūṭam ivvāṟu palaviṭaṅkaḷil kiṟittavar ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṉṉum toṭakkattum pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. mēlum ataṟku muṉ vāḻnta vīramāmuṉivar mutaliyavar eḻutiya nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. piṟpakutiyil pala moḻi peyarppu nūlkaḷum, pāṭa nūlkaḷum, aṟiviyal nūlkaḷum ivarkaḷāl veḷiyiṭap peṟṟaṉa. purāṭṭasṭaṇṭu kiṟittavarkaḷum tam nūl veḷiyīṭukaḷāl ciṟanta toṇṭiṉi innūṟṟaṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil ceytuḷḷaṉar. putitāka vanta intac camayattār ivvāṟu veḷiyīṭukaḷik koṇṭu vantapōtu, iṅkēyē tōṉṟi vaḷarnta civa camayatta varum piṟa vētānta vitīka camayattārum vāḷāyiruppārkaḷō? kiṟittava camayattukku māṟākap palvēṟu uri naṭi nūlkaḷi uṇṭākkiyatōṭu, tam camaya uṇmikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil cila nalla eḷiya urinaṭi nūlkaḷi makkaḷ muṉ vittaṉar. āṟumuka nāvalar mutaliyavarkaḷ viviliya nūlit tamiḻil moḻipeyarttu utaviya tōṭu, civacamayam paṟṟi eḷiya vakiyil pala nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. tamakkeṉac conta accakam amittuk koṇṭu avar veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷuḷ pala iṉṟum vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. avi paṟṟip piṉpu kāṇalām. viṇarkaḷum vētānta vitīkarum avvāṟē pala nūlkaḷi urinaṭiyil eḻutiṉar. iviyaṉittum vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷi atikam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. varavara ataṉ aḷavu kuṟintuḷḷamiyiṉiyum aṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu camayattuṟiyum ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ tamiḻ urinaṭiyi vaḷarttatu.

ilakkiyam

ilakkiyamum ataṉ vaḷarcciyum urinaṭikkut toṇṭāṟṟiya taṟivōm. pala pāṭa nūlkaḷ uri naṭi allatu vacaṉattil emutap peṟṟaṉa. palavaṟṟiṟku urikaḷum inta nūṟṟaṇṭil eḻutiṉavar pallōr. coṟkaḷukkum, toṭarkaḷukkum nūṟpāviṟkum pērurikaḷ eḻutik kuvitta kālam intap pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭēyām. ivaṟṟi yaṉṟi, mēli nāṭṭiṉar cārpāl aṟiviyal vaḷara vaḷara avaṟṟiṉ vaḻi veḷivanta āṅkilam mutaliya moḻikaḷil varum nūlkaḷi moḻi peyarttō, viḷakkiyō, curukkiyō palappala urinaṭi nūlkaḷ veḷivantaṉa. pala mēli nāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum ilakkiya vaḷam ceṟinta nāṭakam mutaliyavaṟṟiyum taḻuviyum toṭṭum, ēṟṟum kuṟittum palvēṟu uri naṭi nūlkaḷum veḷivantaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭu maruttuvam, cōtiṭam mutaliya palvēṟu kalikaḷ paṟṟiyum pala nūlkaḷ makkaḷ muṉ vikkappeṟṟaṉa. potuvākac camayam, ilakkiyam, kali, vāḻviyal, ﻿moḻi, ﻿araciyal﻿ mutaliya﻿ ellāt ﻿ tuṟikaḷilum aṟivum viḷakkamum peṟṟa aṟiñar ‘tām peṟṟa iṉpam peṟuka ivviyakam’ eṉṟa uṇarvil tattam karutti ēṭṭil vaṭittu accup poṟiyiṉ tuṇiyāl nāṭṭil ulava viṭṭaṉar. (annūlkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷi viḷakkum akarātikaḷum akkālattil veḷi vantaṉa.) avi makkaḷ maṉatti-perumpālum potumakkaḷ maṉatti-kavarum vakiyil amiya vēṇṭumeṉa muyaṉṟamiyāl avvāciriyarkaḷ perumpālāṉavaṟṟi urinaṭiyilēyē eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. kirāmaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkum ciṟukatikaḷ, kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ, katip pāṭṭukaḷ, vēṭikki viṭukatikaḷ, nallataṅkāḷ kati mutaliyaṉa pāṭṭā uriyā eṉap pakukka muṭiyātavi eṉa ettaṉiyō vakiyil tamiḻnāṭṭu mūlimuṭukkukaḷilellām, tamiḻil eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷ ceṉṟu, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ulāk koṇṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavē nām iṉṟu peṟṟirukkiṉṟāēm. iṉṟum peṟāta ettaṉiyō kēṭṭaṟiyāk kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ paraṇkaḷil paṭuttu uṟaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. pala cellukkum cellum veḷḷattiṟkum ilakkāyiṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ arumi perumikaḷi uṇarāta pallōr, kirāmaṅkaḷil veḷḷak kālaṅkaḷil inta ēṭukaḷi āṟṟu nīril āḻtti, viṭutali tantu aṉuppi viṭuvār. ﻿ittuṟiyil pala vakiyil ārāya aracāṅkam āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu vēṇṭukōḷ.
