﻿coṟpoḻivu 2- (16-2-1965)

muṉṉuri

ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻnāṭu irunta niliyiṉiyum ataṉ cūḻniliyum oruvāṟu mutaṟ coṟpoḻivil kaṇṭōm. antac cūḻalkaḷukku iṭiyil nāṭṭu moḻiyākiya tamiḻ palvēṟu vakikaḷil vaḷarcciyaṭiyattakka vāyppiṉip peṟṟa niliyiṉiyum ōraḷavu teḷintōm. iṉṟu atē nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarnta vakikaḷi ārāytal poruttamuṭittu eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

potu makkaḷ ilakkiyam

ilakkiya amippil vaḷarvaṉa maṭṭumiṉṟi, potu makkaḷ vāḻvi viḷakkuvaṉavum vāḻvil iṭam peṟuvaṉavum urinaṭiyiṉ pāl cārtti eṇṇattakkaṉavē eṉṟu ōraḷavu kaṇṭōm. eṉavē iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluri pōṉṟum, civañāṉa pāṭiyum pōṉṟum, peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷiṉ urinaṭi pōṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. orupuṟam ilakkiya uri naṭiyeṉa tōṉṟi vīṟunaṭiyiṭṭu vaḷarntu tamiḻnāṭṭil vāḻntuvanta atē vēḷiyil, kallā makkaḷiṭattum naṉku eḻuta paṭikkat teriyāta pāmara makkaḷiṭattum tattam karuttu parimāṟṟattukkākavum potumakkaḷ nalaṉukkākavum, aracāṅkattiṟkum makkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpi vaḷarppataṟkākavum, palvēṟu nāṭṭu nalaṉkaḷukkākavum vāḻntu vaḷarntu vanta urinaṭiyiyum nām eṇṇip pārkka kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷōm. ivi pērilakkiyaṅkaḷeṉak kālatti veṉṟu vāḻat takkaṉa eṉak kāṇa muṭiyāviṭṭālum, ivitām avvakkālaṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ uṇmiyāṉa vāḻkki muṟi, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam iṉṉapiṟavaṟṟi ulakukku uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ivaṟṟip ‘potumakkaḷ urinaṭi’ allatu 'potumakkaḷ ilakkiyam’ eṉa pakuttuk kāṇal cālapporuttamāṉatākum. makkaḷ vāḻkki muṟiyil ettaṉi ettaṉi pirivukaḷuṇṭō-avartam cūḻal ettaṉik kettaṉi māṟupaṭukiṉṟatō-attaṉikkattaṉi vakiyil avartam pēccum vaḻakkum-cātāraṇa eḻuttu vaḻakkum māṟṟam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi āṅkila muṟippaṭi ‘kocci moḻi’ eṉṟālum ammoḻiyē nāṭṭup peṟumpāṉmiyāṉa makkaḷiṉ vāḻvip paṭampiṭittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟamiyiṉ atu paṟṟiya āyvu iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu. iṉṟu moḻittuṟiyi ārāykiṉṟa ulaka aṟiñarkaḷ palarum ittakiya makkaḷ moḻiyākiya koccimoḻiyi ārāyntu uṇarttum neṟiyilēyē pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatum kaṇkūṭu.eṉavē iṉṟu nām potumakkaḷiṭiyē karuttup parimāṟṟattukkākavum: viḷakkam, viḷamparam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkākavum, vēṭikki poḻutupōkku mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkākavum vaḻaṅkap peṟṟa urinaṭi vakikaḷiyum kati, nāval. nāṭakam mutaliyavaṟṟiyum ārāyalām eṉak karutukiṟēṉ.

mekkaṉci tuṟiyiṉ paṇi

potu makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu avar tam moḻi nalattiyum piṟa vāḻviyalpukaḷiyum ārāynta aṟiñar palar ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntiruntaṉar. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṟku muṉṉum vāḻnta makkaḷ vāḻkkiyi ārāynta atē vēḷiyil avar tam ilakkiya vaḷaṉiyum vaḻakku moḻiyiyum ārāynta mēli nāṭṭavar nāṭṭil palar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum vāḻntu, ciṟanta paṇiyāṟṟi tikaḻnta-tam ūtiyap paṇiyiyum tamiḻ uṇarvup paṇiyiyum tiṟampaṭac ceyta-lepṭiṉaṇṭ karṉal kāliṉ mekkaṉci (Lient. Col.Colin Mackenzie) avarkaḷ ciṟantavar eṉpatu maṟakka muṭiyāta uṇmi. araciyal tuṟip paṇiyāḷarāka āṅkila nāṭṭiliruntu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku 1782-il vanta avar, tam paṇiyōṭu paṇiyākac cellumiṭaṅkaḷilellām vāḻnta kallā makkaḷōṭu kalantu uṟavāṭi, avar tam kocci moḻiyiyum vaḷamuṟṟa ilakkiya vaḷaṉiyum ārāyntu eḻuti vittuḷḷār. muppatteṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ (1783—1821) palvēṟu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum cuṟṟip paṇiyāṟṟiya nāṭkaḷil avar tokuttut tantaṉa pala. avar tam aluval nili uyara uyara-avar tam āṭcip poṟuppiṉ elli viriya viriya. avartam moḻiyāyvum virintukoṇṭē vantatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumiṉṟi ilaṅki, jāvā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟiya avar iṟutiyil intiyap peru nilapparappiṟkē, oru tuṟikku-aḷavi āyvuttuṟikku- (Surveyor General of India) talivarāṉār. eṉavē avar ceyalakam kalkattāviṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. eṉiṉum, avartam tamiḻmoḻi āyvu muṟṟuppeṟātu, avaratu 68 vatu vayatu vari, avar iṟuti nāḷ vari (1821) vaḷarntu koṇṭē tāṉ iruntatu. avartam āyvukku utaviyavaruḷ ‘karṉal vilks’ (Col.Wilks) eṉṟa aṟiñarum oruvarāvar. avartam āyvukaḷ pala āṅkila nāṭṭiṟku aṉuppap peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟi veḷiyiṭa akkālak ‘kavarṉar jeṉaralāṉa’ vāraṉ hēsṭṭiṅ avarkaḷāl uttaravum tarappeṟṟatu. avartam attakiya tamiḻ, teluṅku mutaliya ēṭṭup piratikaḷic cērkkap patiṉiyāyiram pavuṇṭu (E15,000/–) celavāyiṟṟeṉavum kaṇakkiṭappeṟṟatu. avartam maṟivukkup piṟaku 1828-il pōrāciriyar vilcaṉ (Prof. Wilson) avarkaḷ tāmē valiya vantu attokuppi veḷiyiṭṭār anta veḷiyīṭṭil perumpāṉmi tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa. oru cila teluṅkum kaṉṉaṭamum iṭam peṟa amikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cila āṅkila nāṭṭu ‘irāyal ēciyāṭṭik cociṭṭi’ (Royal Asiatic Society) yiṉarāl peyarkkap peṟṟu veḷiyiṭavum peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ittokuppip paṟṟi ‘mārk vilks’ eṉpār.

﻿The collection gives all that is necessary to illustrate the antiquities, the civil, military and religious institutions and ancient History of South India.





(Col. Mark Wilks-History of Mysore)

eṉṟu pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār. revareṇṭu villiyam ṭeylar (Rev. William Taylor) mutaliya palar avaṟṟi ārāyntu aṭikkaṭi āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷum āyvu nūlkaḷum veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ 1837-38 il cila tokukkap peṟṟaṉa. piṟaku 1858il ‘kiḻakkintiya maṉi’yum (EastIndia House) piravucu maṉuskiripṭcuṭaṉ (BrownesManuscripts) toṭarpu koḷḷa inta 'mekkaṉci cērkki' (Collection) veḷivantatu. ceṉṉi aracāṅka uṭimiyāka (1847) āṉa piṟaku, 1882-l ittokuti kīḻtticik kiyēṭṭup piratit tokuppil iṭam peṟṟatu. akkālattil atil 8,000 vakikaḷ iruntaṉavām. mekkaṉci avarkaḷē ittokuppik koṇṭu varattāmpaṭṭa pāṭṭik kūṟuvatik kāṇalām.

“From 1792 to 1799 it were tedious to relate the difficulties, the accidents and the discouragements that impeded the progress of this design. The slender means allottted from the necessity of a rigid, no doubt just, economy, the doubts and hindrance ever attendant on new attempts; difficulties arising from the nature of the climate of the country, of the Government, from conflecting interest and passions and prejudices, difficult to contend with, and unpleasant to recollect.”





“In the whole of this period, in which I have marched or wandered, ower the most of the provinces south of the ‘Krishna’, I look back with regret to objects now known to exist, that could have been then examined, and to trace of customs and of institutions that could have been explined, had time or means admitted of the enquiry.



“It was only after my expedition to Ceylon in 1796, that accident rather than design, though ever searching for light that were denied to my situation, threw in any way, those means that I have since unceasingly pursued (not without some Success, I hope) of penetrating beyond the common surface of the Antiquities, the History and the Institutions of the South of India.”



“How for the idea suggested was fulfilled it is not for me to say; for adverse cireumstance one part was nearly defeated;and the ‘Natural History’ was never analyzed in the manner I proposed and expected in concert with the survey; the suspense I was placed in from the reduction of the slender stipend allotted to myself, both for salary and to provide for increasing contingencies, was not only sufficiently modifying, but was aggravated by the overthrow of the establishment first arranged for the work, while other branches were favoured, in the application of the Director’s order. The efforts of these measures on the public mind and even on my assistants contributed to paralise every effort for its completion, but not withstanding these difficulties the success attending the first researches, and a convicition of its utility, induced me to persevere till 1808.”





﻿ ivvāṟu avarē ittuṟiyil tām mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟciyiyum ataṟku uṇṭāṉa palvēṟu iṭiyūṟukaḷiyum viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟar. avartam veḷiyīṭṭut tokuppil tamiḻil 192[1] kiyēṭṭuppaṭikaḷ appōtu tokukkap peṟṟaṉa. purāṇam, kaṭṭukkati, uḷnāṭṭu varalāṟu, vāḻkki varalāṟu, kati, nāṭakam, tattuvam, vāṉa nūl, maruttuvam, kali mutaliya palvēṟu pirivukaḷum itil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iviyaṉṟi ovvōr ūrilum vaḻaṅkum ciṟukatikaḷ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum (local tracts) tamiḻil 255 tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu avar tam kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭu mūli muṭukkukaḷil vaḻaṅkappeṟṟuḷḷa palvēṟu vaki vāḻvu muṟikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ aṉittiyum tāmē nēril ceṉṟu kaṇṭu, kēṭṭu, eḻutit taracceytu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ oru cila nām kāṇa irukkiṟōm. avi aṉittum (mikac cila tavirttu) urinaṭiyil uḷḷamiyiṉ namatu āyviṭiyil iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟāl nām veṟum uriṭiyip paṟṟi maṭṭum aṟiyavilli. attuṭaṉ akkālap paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum moḻi amippu, piṟamoḻik kalappu, kocci vaḻakkiṉ aḷavu mutaliya palavaṟṟiyum aṟintu koḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.

jāṉ morṭāk paṇi

ittuṟiyil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu pāṭupaṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷ palar eṉṟāēm. avarkaḷ aṉivarippaṟṟiyum eṇṇavum eḻutavum namakku nēramum iṭamum illi. eṉavē avaruḷ ciṟantavarākiya mekkaṉci paṟṟiyē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. avartam tokuppaṉittum pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil nāṭṭilvāḻntaṉa paṟṟiyaṉavēyām. itē tuṟiyil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭikālattil ayarātu uḻitta maṟṟeruvari iṅkē eṇṇip pārttal cālum eṉak karutukiṟēṉ. avarē jāṉ mor ṭāk (John Mordoch) eṉpavar. iṟṟikkuccariyāka oru nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ, 1865-il, nāṭṭil tamiḻmoḻiyil accēṟi veḷivanta nūlkaḷiyellām tokuttu, attokutikkut teḷinta tēviyāṉa oru muṉṉuriyiyum eḻuti avar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. eṉavē, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭikkālattil eḻutappeṟṟa tamiḻ nūl paṟṟiya viḷakkam tarum oru nalla viḷakkāka ivar tam tokuppu amikiṉṟatu. 'kiṟittavak kīḻtticik kalvik kuḻu’ eṉṟa amippiṉ muyaṟciyāl ceṉṉiyil ivartam nūl 1865-il veḷiyāyiṟṟu. itil pala tuṟikaḷum naṉku pakukkap peṟṟu ovvoṉṟilum veḷiyāṉa nūlkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mutalil ‘camayam’ eṉṟa talippilum, piṉ piṟa talippukaḷilum, avi uḷḷaṉa. 1755 nūlkaḷ ittokuppil uḷḷaṉa.

innūlkaḷ paṟṟi jāṉ morṭāk avarkaḷ tokuttuk kāṇa virumpiya vakiyiṉi avar vākkilēyē tarukiṟēṉ. The following might each form the subject of a volume.



1.﻿ The rise of the Saiva system in South India, notices of the Principal Saiva temples, a full list of Saiva books, with summaries of their content and some illustrative extracts.



2.﻿ A corresponding work on the Vaishnava system.



3.﻿ An account of the works on Philosophical Hinduism which analyses of their contents and copious extracts.



4.﻿ A complete account of the work on Astrology, Divination and Magic.



5.﻿ An account of Tamil Medicine.



6.﻿ A work on Tamil poetry specimens of versification should be given (Translation like Kambar). -



A book on the above subjects, will got up, would meet with remunerative sales in India, Europe and America and gin some name for the author.





ivvāṟu 1865 il ivartam muṉṉuriyil tamatu āciyi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. eṉiṉum - cariyāka oru nūṟṟaṇṭu kaḻintum - urimi peṟṟu moḻi vaḷarkkiṉṟāēm eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum iṉṟu variyum-ivar kāṭṭiya vakiyil ceyalāṟṟuvōri nāṭṭil iṉṉum kāṇa iyalavilli. ivartam muyaṟciyāl kāṇak kūṭiya vakiyil uḷḷa nūlkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉa urinaṭi nūlkaḷē. eṉavē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ urinaṭiyik kāṇa ivar tam tokuppum naṉku payaṉpaṭat takkatākum.

aracāṅka nūṟpativuc caṭṭam

1867kkup piṟaku aṉṟiya aracāṅkattār accuc caṭṭa aṭippaṭiyil, nāṭṭil accākum nūlkaḷil ovvoṉṟiṉ paṭiyum aracāṅkattiṟku (Registrar of Books) aṉuppivikka vēṇṭumeṉa āṇiyiṭṭaṉar. eṉavē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil veḷiyāṉa nūlkaḷ aṉittum aracāṅka muttiriyip peṟṟu, varicippaṭuttap peṟṟu vakiyāka eṇṇiṭap peṟṟuc cērttuvikkap peṟṟaṉa. aṇmiyil tamiḻ nāṭṭu aracāṅkattār avaṟṟit (1867–1900) tokuttu mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. attokuppilum pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil vaḷarnta tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarcciyi iṭiyīṭiṉṟi ārāya iviyum ivi pōṉṟa vēṟu cilavum namakku ātāramāka uḷḷaṉa.[2] avaṟṟiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu iṉṟu "potu makkaḷ ilakkiya" urinaṭi paṟṟi ārāyntu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

veṟṟiyum koṭiyum

veḷiyāṉa nūlkaḷum ēṭṭup piratiyil uḷḷaṉa pōka nāṭṭil aṅkaṅkē vaḻakkattil uḷḷa palvēṟu eḻuttukkaḷiyum eṇṇutal poruntum. aracāṅkattārum piṟarum vaṭitta kalveṭṭukkaḷum kaṭanta nūṟṟāṇṭil cila uḷḷaṉa. veṟṟi kuṟittum, vēṟu pālam mutaliyaṉa amittum uṇṭākkiya cācaṉaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil ōriraṇṭiṉik īṇṭuk kāṭṭal nalam payappatākum.

ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil neppōliyaṉ vīḻcciyuṟṟatum āṅkilēyar veṟṟi peṟṟatum nāṭaṟinta varalāṟu. avveṟṟiyik koṇṭāṭu mukattāṉ āṅkilēyar tam āṇi ceṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilellām veṟṟittūṇ nāṭṭi, avveṟṟiyik kallilum poṟittaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu tañcic carapōci maṉṉaṉ āṭci ellikku uṭpaṭṭa cāḷuvaṉkuppattil uḷḷati iṉṟum kāṇalām. āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum akkuṟippukkaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻkkuṟippu uri naṭiyākavē uḷḷatu. iraṇṭiyum appaṭiyē tarukiṟēṉ.

āṅkilēyar kalvi

1858-kkumuṉ kumpiṉi āṭci naṭipeṟṟatu. ataṟkup piṟaku āṅkilēyar nāṭṭu nēraṭi āṭciyākiya vikṭōriyā irāṇiyiṉ āṭci toṭarntatu. akkālaṅkaḷilellām āṅkilēyar palar vāṇipam poruṭṭum āḷutaṟ poruṭṭum innāṭṭukku vantaṉar. vantavar innāṭṭu moḻiyik kaṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kaṟṟa nūlkaḷum pala; kaṟpittōrum palar. avarkaḷip paṟṟiya aṉṟiya kuṟippoṉṟik kāṇalām.

tamiḻ ilakkaṇat teḷivu ⁠⁠1893

aṅkilēyar viyāpāra māḷikikaḷic ceṉṉi, kaṭalūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kaṭṭi viyāpārattip perukki, aracum kipaṟṟukiṉṟa camayattil, aṅkila uttiyōkastarkaḷ tamiḻik kaṟpatu yuktameṉa atikārikaḷ tīrmāṉittu, ceṉṉiyil ‘kālēj’ eṉṟa ōr





vittiyācāliyi stāpittārkaḷ. itutāṉ ‘Old College’ eṉappaṭṭa iṭam. itil ippōtu vittiyācālit talivarākiya ‘Director’ tamatu kaccēriyi vittu naṭatti varukiṟār. itil mutal aṅkilēyarukkut tamiḻ upāttiyāyarāka niyamikkappaṭṭavar ceṅkaṟpaṭṭut tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār. ivar akkālattil paṭṭaṇattil vijayam ceytu, naṉṉūlukkuk kāṇṭiki uri yiyaṟṟiṉa irāmāṉujak kavirāyariṉ cīṣaṉum, kaṟṟuc colliyumāyiruntu, mēṟpaṭi kavirāyariṭam naṉṉūṟpāṭam kēṭṭavar. ivar mūlamāyttāṉ aṅkilēyar naṉṉūl ciṟanta tamiḻ ilakkaṇam eṉat terintu koṇṭatu maṉṟi, carvakalācālip pariṣikaṭkum (University Examinations) in naṉṉūlē nalla ilakkaṇam eṉa vitikkavum talippaṭṭatu.



it tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyārē vācaka pustakam (Prose) aṅkilēya māṇākkarukku vēṇṭiyatāyirunta poḻutu, hitōpatēcattic camaskirutattiṉiṉṟu moḻip peyarttup pañca tantiram eṉṟa pustakam uṇṭu paṇṇik koṭuttavar. naṉṉūliluḷḷa puṇarcci vitikaḷ pulappaṭattakkatāy appustakattik koṭum puṇarppā yeḻutiviṭṭār. ivar ceṅkaṟpaṭṭil caturamīṉāka uttiyōkañ ceytu aṅkilēyarāl naṉkoṭi palavum peṟṟu, tēka viyōkamāṉār. ivaruṭiya kumāraṉ kiṟistavarākap paṭṭaṇattilirukkiṟār.

taṟkālattil tamiḻ apiviruttikkāka atikamāy uḻittu varupavarkaḷ (Mr.W.Thamotharam Pilli, B.A., B. L., Mr. P. Ranganatha Mudaliar, M. A., Mr. J. M. Vellu Pillay) yāḻppāṇikaḷāṉa kaṉam. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, kaṉam. jē. vēluppiḷḷi, periyapaṭṭam peṟṟa kaṉam iraṅkanāta mutaliyār mutaliyavarkaḷē. ivaruḷ kaṉam. tamōtarampiḷḷi ēṭṭuppiratikaḷ maṅkimaṟintu kiṭanta tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil anēkattik kaṇṇum tiruṣṭi kuṟiyattakkatāka veku kaṣṭattuṭaṉ vācittut tiruttip pērt teḻuti, mikunta poruṭ celavum ceytu, acciyaṟṟit tamiḻ pāṣiyiṉ ciṟappup piṟarukku viḷaṅka mikka karuttōṭu uḻittu varukiṟār.





itu 1893 ām āṇṭu tāvitu yōcēpu upāttiyāyarāl eḻutappeṟṟa ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇat teḷivu’ eṉṟa nūliliruntu eṭukkappeṟṟatu. akkālattiya urinaṭikkum ituvē cāṉṟākiṉṟatu. itil akkālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻntu moḻiyiyum urinaṭiyiyum vaḷampaṭuttiya palari avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. cilar āṅkilēyaric cārntum cilar avarkaḷic cārātum ciṟakka vāḻntuḷḷaṉar. āṅkilēyaric cārātu vāḻtalē perumiyuṭiyateṉa akkālattil karutappaṭṭateṉpati aṟikiṟōm. āṅkilēyaric cārātu urimiyōṭu tamiḻt toṇṭu ceytu vāḻnta periyavarkaḷic ‘ciṅka’ṅkaḷeṉavē avar aḻikkiṟār.

āṅkila aracu tamiḻ nāṭṭil pirapalyamāyirukkum pōtu, āṅkilēyarāl naṉku matikkappaṭṭuvanta tamiḻppaṇṭitarkaḷ irāmānujakkavirāyar, vicākap perumāḷiyar, tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār, a. capāpati mutaliyār, a. vīrācāmi ceṭṭiyār, vēlāyuta mutaliyār, caravaṇap perumāḷiyar.



āṅkilēyari aṭuttuṟavāṭātirunta tamiḻ ciṅkaṅkaḷ nallūr āṟumuka nāvalar, māyavaram mīṭṉāci cuntaram piḷḷi, vaṭalūr irāmaliṅkap paratēciyār, tiricirapuram muttuvīrak kavirāyar. ivarkaḷ koñca kālattukku muṉtāṉ[4] kālavi yōkamāṉarkaḷ. icciṅkaṅkaḷiṭam kaṟṟuc collikaḷāyiruntavarkaḷ ippōtu kalācālikaḷil tamiḻ muṉṣikaḷāka virukkiṟārkaḷ.





ivvāṟu akkālap pulavarkaḷ pārāṭṭap peṟukiṟarkaḷ. ivarkaḷuḷ perumpālōr tamiḻ urinaṭiyi vaḷampaṭuttiyavarkaḷē. eṉavē ivar tam urinaṭic ciṟappukaḷi iṉṟum nāḷiyum kāṇalām. āṅkilēyarukkāka amikkappaṭṭa caṅkamē ceṉṉik kalvic caṅkamākum.

ūr varalāṟukaḷ

mekkaṉci turi avarkaḷ palaviṭaṅkaḷukku nēril ceṉṟu kaṇṭavippaṟṟi mēlēyaṟintōm. avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉavi avvap pakuti paṟṟiya katikaḷum varalāṟum mutaliyaṉavumēyām. cilavaṟṟi makkaḷē eḻutik koṭuttārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟi nēril colla atikārikaḷ kuṟittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. oṉṟu kāṇpōm.

kēvi māvaṭṭattu antiyūr tālukkāvic cērnta varatanallūr kirāma makkaḷ avvūriṉ paḻiyavaralāṟṟiṉi “mekkaṉsi' turikku nēril coṉṉatu oṉṟu. itu avar tokuppil citiyātu uḷḷatu. toṭakkamum muṭivum maṭṭum īṇṭuk kuṟikkiṉṟēṉ.

⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰7௵ (1807) ēpral௴ ⁰92 mējar karṉal mekkaṉsi cākipu avarkaḷ carkkārukkuk kōyamuttūr jillā antiyūr tālukkāviṟ cērnta akrakāram opaṉi 'pāvāṇikūṭal’ miṭṭar payicimavucē ‘varatanallūr’ akkirakāram makā jaṉaṅkaḷ eḻutikkoṭutta ‘kipīyattu’ eṉṉaveṉṟāl:—

pūruvattil cālivākaṉa cakāptam ⁰⁰⁰ ⁰⁰ (1100). varuṣattukkumēl ūrāṭcikkōṭṭi kirāmattukkuc cērntatu ‘allappac ceṭṭi’ pāḷiyam eṉṟu oru pēṭṭiyiruntatu. ௸ pēṭṭip pāḷiyakkāra rājikattil kāliyāyp pōyiṟṟu.

appuṟam cakāptam ⁰⁰⁰3⁰⁰ kāḷayutti varuṣm tēvarāya makarāya mēṟpaṭi allappac ceṭṭi pāḷiyattil allāḷappaṭṭar. caṅkama paṭṭar vakiyarā pirāmaṇarkaḷukku akkirakāramum kaṭṭu vittuk koṭuttu ‘cōmakkirahaṉa’ puṇṇiya kālattil mēṟpaṭi pirāmaṇarkaḷukku muppattu nālu paṅkum tāṉam paṇṇic civappiratiṣṭiyum paṇṇuvittuk koṭuttār.atu mutal vekutāṉiya varuṣam variyil nirupātika caruva māṉiyamāy naṭantu vantatu.

ivvāṟu toṭaṅkiya varalāṟu, piṉ palvēṟu viḷakkaṅkaḷ kāṭṭi aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭapaṭi muṭikiṉṟatu.

niṉṟa carkkār iraṇṭu paṅkukkuc carkkār māṉiyam pōṭṭuk koṇṭārkaḷ. atumutal etatkakṣaṇam variyil ௸ pūmi aṉupavittu varukiṟōm. itu naṭanta kipīyattu. ik kipītu eḻutiṉatu ௸yūr jaṉam pēr. kuppaṉ eḻuttu. irāmiyaṉ kiyeḻuttu. iraṅkiyiṉ rucuvu, tirumalirāyaṉ rucuvu. nārāyaṇayyaṉ rucuvu (Mec. collections, D. 2877)

ivvāṟu ivvaralāṟṟuk kuṟippu muṭikiṉṟatu. itil kāṇum āṇṭuk kaṇakkuc cariyāka illi. itik kipiyattu allatu kipītu eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. iccoṟkaḷukku vivarak kuṟippu eṉṟum statement, report, detiled account, particulars, eṉṟum poruḷ uḷḷaṉa. kipīyattu nāmā eṉṟāl pirāmaṇap pāttiram allatu ‘Affidavit’ eṉṟu poruḷ.[5] 1839 il veḷiyāṉa nūlil 'news, particulars' eṉṟa poruḷkaḷ uḷḷaṉa[6]. eṉavē iccol iṉṟu nāṭṭil atikamāka vaḻakkattil illi eṉṟālum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil kirāma makkaḷiṭattil mikac cātāraṇamāka vaḻaṅkiya oṉṟu eṉat terikiṉṟatu. itil eḻutiṉavar, cāṭci tantavar ākiyōr peyarkaḷum iṟutiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ‘rucuvu’ eṉpatu cāṭciyākum. ‘miṭṭāpayici mavucē’ eṉpatu akkirāmam tāṉamākak koṭutta vakiyi viḷakkum toṭarpōlum, ittakiya ‘kipītu’kaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil tamiḻ nāṭṭil atikamāka vaḻaṅki vantamiyiṉ ic cācaṉa muṟiyāl tamiḻ urinaṭi ōraḷavu vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷati maṟukka muṭiyumō?

ivvāṟāṉa kipītukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi eṉak kaṇṭōm. mēlē nām kaṇṭatu nēril colliyatu; ūr varalāṟu paṟṟiyatu. iṉi inta aṭippaṭiyil mekkaṉci turi avarkaḷ ‘rayiṭṭar pāpu rāyar’ avaruṭaṉ ceṉṟu avvan nilattu makkaḷik kēḷvi kēṭṭup patil peṟum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu eṉak kāṇkiṟōm. turi avarkaḷ eḻuttaruṭaṉ (writer) ceṉṟu makkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu paḻaki, avarkaḷi kēḷvikaḷ kēṭṭup peṟṟa patilkaḷ pala. avaṟṟuḷ cīraṅka viṉāviṭiyik kāṇpōm.

viviaiṭi-piratiyuttaram

1. vatu kēḷvi: tarmavarmā eṉappaṭṭavar yār? avartāṉā tarmarācar? avarālētāṉā ātiyil srīraṅkam kōyil ēṟpaṭṭatu? ‘varmā’ eṉpataṟku arttam eṉṉa?

uttaram:—tarmavarmā veṉkiṟavar cōḷa tēcattu rājā. avar tarmarājā aṉṟu. intak kaliyukattukku muṉ tuvāparārttattil tarma rājāviṉuṭiya kālam. tarma varmāviṉuṭiya kālam 3⁰6 caturyukattukku muṉ tirētāyukattu rājā, vipīṣaṇarukkuk koṭutta srīraṅka tivya vimāṉattukkut tarmavarmā captapkāram, maṇṭapam, kōpurātikaḷ kaṭṭiṉavar. ‘varmā’ eṉṟa captattukkuk kavacam eṉṟu arttam. ākac(cē) ceyta tarmattik kavacamāka uṭiyavar, (varma captam kṣattiriyarik kuṟikkum oru kuṟippup pērām. atu carmā eṉpatu pirāmaṇarik kuṟippatu pōlā meṉka.) tarma varmāviṉuṭiya vācastāṉam uṟiyūr. ataṟku nikaḷāpuri eṉṟum peyar. avar turittaṉam mutaltāṉ antap paṭṭaṇattukkup piracitti. (Mec. Collections D. 3186) ivvāṟu tiruvaraṅka antaṇarkaḷ ‘mekkaṉsi’kku muṉ viḷakkum poḻutu cōḻar kālatti 36 yukattukku muṉpu (cumār 60 laṭcamx36) īrttuc cellukiṉṟaṉar. itupōṉṟa viḷakkaṅkaḷ pala avar kuṟippil uḷḷaṉa. cila cātikaḷ paṟṟiya kipītukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu kuṟumpar paṟṟiyatu. avar ūrkaḷ aṉittum iṉṟum ceṉṉikkup pakkattilēyē uḷḷaṉa.

kuṟumparkaḷuṭiya ﻿24﻿ kōṭṭikaḷ uṇṭāyiruntaṉa. avikaḷil upa kōṭṭikaḷ atika vicēṣa kīrttikaḷip peṟṟatu. inta irupattiṉālukkum puḻal kōṭṭi rājatāṉi eṉappaṭṭirukkutu. intak kuṟumparkaḷ pañca pāṇṭavarkaḷiṉ yuttattil aḷaṉākāḷar ākkappaṭṭu paṭukaḷattil tīkamāy toṇṭupaṭṭataṉāl yuttattukku atika paṇpaṭṭavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ āṉālivāḷanta yuttattil muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭu eṅkum paravi citaṟaṭikkappaṭṭārkaḷ.

ivarkaḷ kollappaṭṭaviṭam neṭumpūril. anta iṭam iṉṟum kuṟumparik koṉṟaviṭam eṉṟu collappaṭutu. kuṟumparukkuṇṭāṉa kōṭṭikaḷiṉ vivaram poḻil–cālap pākkam–neṭumpūr – aṇikkaṭṭu – paṭṭipulam – āmūr – puliyūr – maṟayicīkuḷam – kaḷattūr – cempūr–tamuṇuār–yīkārṭu.

(Mec. Collection D. 867)

varalāṟṟuk katikaḷ

ivar tokuppil māṇikka vācakar paṟṟiya oru varalāṟṟuk kuṟippum kāṇappeṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ

mukavuriyiyum mutalāvatu pattiyiyum maṭṭum īṇṭuk kāṇalām. mukavuri: kaṉam poruntiya mēcar kālaṉ mekaṉsi turiyavarkaḷ vētanāyaka vētākama cirōmaṇiyārukkuk kuṭutta tiṭṭam allatu memōraṇṭattiṉpaṭi māṇikkavācakar carittiram ēteṉpatit tiṭṭamāy vicāritteḻuta vēṇṭiyatāmē. itit toṭṭu aṟiya vēṇṭiyatu.

(ka. vatu) māṇikkavācakarteṭcaṇa rācciyattil maturippaṭṭaṇattil pirāmaṇa vaṅki cattilē piṟantu teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ eṉkiṟa pāṇṭiyaṉukku mantiriyāyiruntu avaruṭiya tiraviyattik koṇṭu āviṭiyār kōvileṉṟu collappaṭukiṟa tirupperuntuṟiyilirukkappaṭṭa tiruppaṇikaḷik kaṭṭiṉareṉṟum kaṭiciyilē citamparattukku vantu avviṭattilē ﻿yīḻattu﻿ rācāviṉiṭattiliruntu vanta puttar camayattāri vātu paṇṇi allatu tarkkam paṇṇiyaḻittup pōṭṭāreṉṟuñ civa matattārellām vāy vicēṣamākac collu kiṟārkaḷ.

ālivar enta varuṣam enta āṇṭilē yiruntāreṉṟu vāy vicēṣamākac collukiṟa varkaḷiyuṅ kāṇōm, purāṇattilum kāṇōm.

ivvāṟu māṇikkavācakar varalāṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itil moḻit tavaṟu maṭṭumallātu iṭam, varalāṟu, peyar mutaliyaṉavum piḻipaṭṭiruppatiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. eṉiṉum ivipaṟṟi yellām viḷaṅka aṟiyātavarākiya āṅkilēyarākiya mekkaṉci avarkaḷ avaravar eḻutiyatiyum kūṟiyatiyum appaṭiyē vakuttut tokuttu vittār. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil cevi vaḻiyākavum eḻuttu vaḻiyākavum nāṭṭil vāḻntu vaḷarntu vanta urinaṭiyi nammāl kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. inta urinaṭiyil pala piḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉavēṉum ituvum tamiḻ urinaṭi varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟṟup piṉ naṉku vaḷara irukkum urinaṭikku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amiyavilliyā!

makkaḷ piṇakku

iṉic cila makkaḷiṉ varalāṟu paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷiyum kirāmaṅkaḷ tōṟum makkaḷ cātiveṟikoṇṭu māṟupaṭṭa varalāṟukaḷiyum ikkuṟippukkaḷil kāṇkiṉṟāēm. ittuṟiyil pala ivar tokuppil uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟi maṭṭum kāṇalām. iṉṟum caturaṅkappaṭṭiṉatti aṭutta nilappakutikkuk kuṟumpari nāṭu eṉṟum kuṟumpar nāṭu eṉṟum peyar iruppati aṟivōm. āyiṉum kuṟumpar eṉṟa marapiṉar vāḻvik kāṇal aritu. itaṟkāṉa kāraṇatti iṉṟum nammāl aṟiya muṭiyavilli. piṉvarum varalāṟṟuk kuṟippu- ataṉ urinaṭiyil palvēṟu tavaṟukaḷ peṟṟiruntapōtilum-kuṟumpar vāḻvu vīḻnta varalāṟṟi appaṭiyē paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭuvatu pōṉṟu amintuḷḷatu.

kuṟumpar eṉṟa iṭavamisattāruṭiya

﻿carittiram:





ṟāyar camastāṉattilē kuṟumpar eṉṟa yiṭac cātikaḷ vekuiṭaṅkaḷilē ṟāṭciyapārampaṇṇiṉārkaḷ. avāḷ neṭumaram, aṇikkaṭṭu, cāla pākkam, nerumpūr, eṉṟa yiṭaṅkaḷilēyum yiṉṉam matta iṭaṅkaḷilēyum kōṭṭikaḷ kaṭṭikkoṇṭu pirapaliyamāy vāḻntārkaḷ=aṉṉāḷkaḷilē kuṟumpar kuṭimakkaḷ mutalikkuṭikaḷiyum veḷḷāḷakuṭikaḷiyum taṅkaḷi vaṇaṅkumpaṭiyāṉa piṟiyattiṉam paṇṇiṉārkaḷ āṉal mutalikaḷum veḷḷāḷarum atukkuc cammati koṭukkavilli appō kuṟumparkaḷ avāḷukku vekuyiṭukkaṅkaḷ paṇṇiṉārkaḷ





āṉālavāḷataṟkuc cammatikuṭukkavilli-atiṉ pērillavarkaḷ teruvilum muccantikaḷilum vettili tōṭṭaṅkaḷilum yiṉṉam veku yiṭaṅkaḷilum tiṭṭi vācal pōlē kaṭṭi atukketiṟik kuṟumpar uḷukkārntu koṇṭu caṟuvattiṟāḷum atil tali kuṉintu nuḻiyumpaṭiyākavum atiṉālē taṅkaḷukku vaṇakkam naṭakkavum ettiṉam paṇṇiṉarkaḷ-appō mutalimārum veḷḷāḷamārum talikuṉintu nuḻikiṟatukku vatiḷāka mutal kāli viṭṭu nuḻintu yitaṉalē yavāḷi avacaṅki paṇṇiṉarkaḷ-eṉṟa pōtikkum avāḷatikārastarkaḷāṉapaṭiyiṉalē palavitattilum veḷḷāḷariyum mutalikaḷiyum tuṉpappaṭuttiṉarkaḷ. appō tuṉpam atikarittapaṭiyiṉalē yivāḷellārum yōciṉi paṇṇi oṉṟilēyum muṭiyāmal kaṭiciyilē yoru ampaṭṭaṉiṭattilē pōya avaṉukku māṉṉiya cāṉṉiyam paṇṇi vikkiruēm kuṟumpari yatampaṇṇa yōciṉi kēṭṭārkaḷ appō avaṉ camāḷippuc colli tāṉatukku upāyam paṇṇukiṟēṉ eṉṟu tiṭārikkañ colli yivāḷi vīṭṭukku aṉuppi viṭṭāṉ atiṉ piṟaku avaṉ taṉṉiṉattārākiya ammaṭṭar vīṭukaḷukkellām pōy avāḷellārum taṉakku utavi ceyya vēṇumeṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ antappaṭiyē avāḷellārum utavi ceykiṟōmeṉṟu vākkuk kuṭuttārkaḷ kuṟumpa cātiyārukkuḷḷē oruvaṉ ceṉṟupōṉal iḻavukku varukiṟa vāḷak kūṭṭattāraṉivōrum taliyēkac cavaram paṇṇukiṟatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatu—



appaṭi orunāḷ ﻿taṟuvāyilē kuṟumparkaḷukkuḷḷē mūppāyirukkiṟa oruvaṉ viḻuntu pōṉaṉ appō kuṟumparkaḷ ellōrum





ēkōpittuk kūṭiyiruntu avaṉiḻavu caṭaṅku naṭappikkiṟatilē ellārum māmūlap paṭikku cavaram paṇṇikkoḷḷa oru mikka uḷukkārntārkaḷ appō yinta ammaṭṭaṉaṉavaṉ tāṉ pantu kaṭṭu paṇṇiyirunta ammaṭṭarkaḷ ellōrukkum tākkiti paṇṇi avāḷavāḷ ciṟikkiṟa maṉuṣari avāḷavāḷ koṉṟu pōṭak kaṟpittiruntāṉ antappaṭiyē yavāḷavāḷ ciṟitta maṉuṣaṉi avaṉavaṉ taliyi cirittu kaḻuttaṇṭiyil varukiṟapōtu kuravaḷikaḷi aṟuttuk koṉṟu pōṭṭārkaḷ appōyiṅkē māṇṭupōṉa kuṟumpaṟuṭiya srīkaḷellārum pelatta akkiṉi vaḷattu atil kutittu piruṇacētam paṇṇikkoṇ ṭārkaḷ-appō srīkaḷ nerumpūrilē muppōkam viḷintālum uppukku neṟ kiṭiyāl pōkaveṉṟu cāpamiṭṭārkaḷām-kuṟumparālē kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōṭṭikaḷiṉṉam mēlākac collappaṭṭa yiṭaṅkaḷilē muḻutum yiṭintu taviḻntu maṇmēṭāyk kiṭakkutu ati pōyp pārttavarkaḷirukkiṟārkaḷ avāḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kēṇikaḷ antanta yiṭaṅkaḷilē pāḻāyp pōyk kiṭakkutu atu yinta caturaṅkap paṭṭaṇattilēyum yirukkutu antak kalkaḷ pūṟuvaṅkaṇṭa veku nāḷiyiṉ kallukaḷāyirukkutu—





(Mec, Collection, D. 3838)



pañca tantiram

inta aḷavōṭu ittakiya kuṟippukkaḷi niṟutti vēṟu vakiyil urinaṭi vaḷarnta varalāṟṟik kāṇpōm. pañcatantirak katiyāṉatu tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār eṉpavarāl āṅkilēyar poruṭṭuc ceyyap peṟṟa oru nūl eṉpatik kaṇṭōm. innūli 1825–il avar eḻuti muṭittār. eṉiṉum 1850-il avar maṟintār eṉṟum kāṇkiṟōm. avarē kītā mañcari eṉṟa maṟṟāeru vacaṉa nūlum ilakkaṇa viṉā viṭi eṉṟāeru nūlum eḻutiṉār eṉpar. antap pañcatantiram eṉṟa kati āṅkilēyarukkākap paṭippaṭiyāka ilakkaṇap puṇarccikaḷ porunta amiyap peṟṟuḷḷamiyum mēlē kaṇṭōm. mutalil varum katikaḷ eḷiya iyalpup puṇarvuṭiya vākkiyaṅkaḷāka amiya, piṉ varavarap palvēṟu ilakkaṇap puṇarvukaḷiyuṭiya vākkiyaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟaṉa. intak katiyē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilum in nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum palvēṟu pāṭanūlkaḷil tavaṟātu iṭam peṟṟamiyi aṟikiṟōm. ataṉ naṭi muṉ kaṇṭami pōlaṉṟic cemmi nalam cārntirukkiṉṟatu. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ urinaṭikku atil oru ciṟu pakutiyiyēṉum kāṇal tēviyākiṉṟatu. itō anta naṭikkup puṇarcci ilakkaṇam nirampiya ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭu:—

aintāvatu. acampirēṣiya kāriyattuvam–kati 1.

uccayiṉi purattil vatinta tēvanāma vētiyaṉoruvaṉ maṉiviyōṭu illaṟam naṭāttum nāḷiṟ kuḻaviyillāk kuṟiyāl iruvarum oru kīrippiḷḷiyi mintaṉippōṟ karuti vaḷarppuḻi oru makavuṇṭāyiṟṟu. maṉivi ammataliyi ōr nāḷ tūṅkuvittuk kaṇavaṉip pārttu, “yāṉ utakattiṉ poruṭṭuc cellā niṉṟēṉ. nīr naṅ kāṉ muḷiyip pārttirum, oru vēḷi kīri kaṭikkum” eṉṟiṅṅaṉam vakuttut taṇṇīrkkuc cella, piṉṉar akkuḻanti toṭṭiliṟ kaṇ vaḷarntatu avvantaṇaṉum oru taṭākak karuma vayattaṉayp puṟañ cella, attaruṇattil oru kiruṣṇa caruppam pilattiṉiṉṟum puṟampōntu toṭṭiliṉ mēṟ cellā niṟkak kaṇṇuṟṟa nakulam ati akkaṇamē virintu paṟṟi utaṟik koṉṟu pala





kaṇṭalāṅkaṇṭu kurutiyāṟ pūcuṟṟa mukattuṭaṉē taṉatu āṟṟalik kāṭṭa virumpi virintu tuvāra mukamāka varukiyil, vanta pārppiṉi cennīrāl niṟinta ataṉ vataṉattip pārtta vaḷavil atu taṉ piḷḷiyik kaṭittu maṭittateṉṟu uḷḷit tuḷḷit tuṇukkuṟṟu nīrkkuṭañ citivuṟumpaṭi ataṉ talimēṟ pōṭṭuk koṉṟu uṭaṉē uṭpukā niṟkiyil, makaṉ uṟaṅkutaliyum arukē karumpāmpiṉ tuṇikkik kuviyiyuṅ kaṇṭu antō! keṭṭēṉ, yāṉ eṉ aruṅkuḻaviyi naṉkārāytaliṉṟik koli ceytēṉeṉac cōṉamuṟṟup pulampā niṟpuḻi avaḷ talivaṉ āṇṭu eyti, ‘kuḻanti itō irukkiyil nī veṟumiyeṉa makaviḻantē ṉeṉṟēṉ kataṟik kaluḻkiṉṟaṉa? eṉṉaṭi pētāy!” eṉa maṉivi, “nīr yātō pēravāvāl eṉ moḻi taḷḷip pōṉamiyāl ataṉ payaṉ intō vāyttatu, itu mikka avāviṉaṉ mūṭi mēṟ cakkiram cuḻaṉṟati yottatu” eṉa, avaṉ ‘aḵtevvaṇṇam? uritti’ eṉa urippāṉ.





–pak. 131, 132.

katikaḷ

ippaṭiyē vēṟu cila katikaḷum vaḻakkattil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil oṉṟu mariyātirāmaṉ kati. 1847–il citāppēṭṭi tēcappa ceṭṭiyār kumārar vīrapattira ceṭṭiyārāl jīvarakṣāmirta accakattil patippikkap peṟṟa katā cintāmaṇi eṉṟa nūlil oru pakuti itu. in nūl pala ciṟu katikaḷit taṉṉakattē koṇṭatu. itil naṭi avvaḷavu ciṟantatāka illi yeṉiṉum ittakiya katikaḷip potu makkaḷ virumpip paṭittārkaḷ eṉakkāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. eṉavē atiyum iṅkē tarukiṉṟēṉ.

mariyāti rāmaṉ kati:

⁰8 vatu. oruvaṉ kātuk kaṭukkaṉi maṟṟāevaṉ tiruṭiyatu.



oru cattirattal iraṇṭupēr vaḻippōkkar oruvaṉukkoruvaṉ etirmukamākat talivittup paṭuttuk koṇṭārkaḷ nittiri vēḷiyilē valatu kātu kīḻāp paṭuttiruntavaṉuṭiya iṭatu kātu kaṭukkaṉi iṭatu kātu kīḻākap paṭut tiruntavaṉ kaḻaṟṟit taṉ valatu kātilēpōṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ. viḻittukkoṇṭa piṟpāṭu kaṭutkaṉi yiḻantavaṉ eṉ kaṭukkaṉi ēṉ kaḻaṟṟik koṇṭā yeṉṟu kēṭka avaṉum eṉ kātuk kaṭukkaṉi nītāṉ kaḻaṟṟik koṇṭāyeṉṟāṉ, ippaṭik kalakappaṭṭuk kaṭukkaṉi yiḻantavaṉ mariyāti rāmaṉiṭattilē collik koṇṭāṉ. niyāyātipatiyākiya avaṉ tiruṭaṉi aḻittuk kēṭṭaviṭattil avaṉum appaṭiyē coṉṉapaṭiyiṉalē nīṅkaḷ iruvarum eppaṭip paṭuttuk koṇṭīrkaḷō appaṭi pakuttuk koṇṭu kāṇpiyuṅkaḷeṉṟāṉ. appaṭiyē kāṇpittapōtu tiruṭaṉip pārttu uṉ iṭatukātu kīḻē irunta paṭiyāl atiliruntu eppaṭi kaḻaṟṟalām. avaṉ iṭatu mēlē iruntapaṭiyāleḷitākak kaḻaṟṟik koṇṭāy. nītāṉ tiruṭaṉeṉṟu colli uṭamikkāraṉukkuk kaṭukkaṉi vāṅkik koṭuttut tiruṭaṉē irupattunālu aṭi aṭippittu āṟu mātam kāvalirukkac ceytāṉ. attiruṭaṉ poy coṉṉalum poruntac collavēṉum eṉṟapaṭi kāttuk koḷḷavum teriyāmaṟ pōyiṟṟēyeṉṟu tuṉpamaṭintāṉ.





﻿ ivikaḷēyaṉṟi﻿ naṭēca cāstirikaḷ﻿ eḻutiya tirāviṭa nāṭṭup pūrvakālak katikaḷ, mattiyakālak katikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum paṭittu makiḻattakkaṉa. nalla urinaṭiyil amintuḷḷaṉ.

ivvāṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ettaṉiyō vakiyāṉa katikaḷ eḻutappeṟṟum acciṭappeṟṟum nāṭṭil ulavivantaṉa. eḻutappeṟātu cevi vaḻiyākac ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭuvari vanta palvēṟu katikaḷum accuc cātaṉam uṇṭāṉa karāṇattālē nāṭṭil nūlkaḷāka uruppeṟṟu ulaviṉa. avi aṉittiyum varicip paṭuttik kūṟuvateṉpatu iyalāta oṉṟu. mariyātirāmaṉ kati (1812). vikkiramātittaṉ kati, muppattiraṇṭu patumi kati (1804), catamuka rāvaṇaṉ kati (1818), tāṭi veṇṇeykkāraṉ kati (1843) viṟalimāṟaṉ kati (1828), tamiḻaṟiyum maṭanti kati (1812) mutaliyavi cila. ivaṟṟuḷ cila ēṭṭup paṭikaḷākavum iruntu inta nūṟṟāṇṭil acciṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iviyuṅ ivi pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa piṟa katikaḷum nāvalkaḷum, vaḷarntu varukiṉṟa tamiḻ urinaṭi varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟattakkaviyē eṉpatu maṭṭum uṟuti.

nāval

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟaṇṭilē, ittakiya katikaḷ maṭṭuṉṟi iṉṟu nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum ‘nāval’ eṉṉum putuvaki nūlum iṭam peṟalāyiṟṟu. tamiḻil ‘piratāpa mutaliyār carittiram’ eṉṉum nāvali eḻuti (1835 il) veḷiyiṭṭa māyūram vētanāyakam piḷḷi avarkaḷ ittakiya nāvalkaḷ paṟṟiyum tamiḻil urinaṭi vaḷara vēṇṭiya tēvi paṟṟiyum tam mukavuriyil kuṟittuḷḷār. atuvariyil tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarāmikku uriya pala kāraṇaṅkaḷi viḷakki, ēṭṭil eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu eḻutum muṟiyil nīṇṭa viḷakka uri eḻuta iyalāt taṉmiyit toṭṭukkāṭṭi, tām uri naṭiyil innāvali eḻutiyataṟkuriya kāraṇanti avar viḷakkukiṟār.

My object in writing this work of fiction is to supply the want of prose works in Tamil, a want which is admitted and lamented by all.





(muṉṉuri)

urinaṭiyiṉ tēviyi avar tamatu nūliṉ maṟṟoru pakutiyilum eḻutikkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ‘vacaṉam’ eṉpatiyē avar āṇṭuḷḷār. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil urinaṭikku ivar eḻuttum ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu.

vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷāl jaṉaṅkaḷ tirutta vēṇṭumē yallātu ceyyuṭkaḷip paṭittut tiruntuvatu acāttiya mallavā? airōppiya pāṣikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmalirukkumāṉāl antat tēcaṅkaḷ nākarikamum naṟpāṅkum aṭintirukkak kūṭumā? appaṭiyē nammuṭiya cuya pāṣikaḷil vacaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ illāmalirukkiṟa variyil intat tēcam cariyāṉa cīrtiruttam aṭiyāteṉpatu niccayam.





(42 ām atikāram.)

itil āciriyar kūṟiya aṭippaṭiyilēyē pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭil tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa. ivvurinaṭipaṟṟiyum nāval paṟṟiyum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum eḻunta cila viḷakkaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil irunta ataṉ niliyi naṉku viḷakkuvaṉavākum. ‘cāvittiri carittiram’ ‘patmāvati carittiram’ mutaliya nāvalkaḷ eḻutiya mātaviyā avarkaḷ 1903 il ‘patmāvati carittiram’ iraṇṭām matippiṉ muṉṉuriyil nāvalpaṟṟiyum ataṉ naṭipaṟṟiyum kuṟikkiṉṟār. maṟṟellā uyartara kirantaṅkaḷiyum pōlavē nāvaleṉṉum kirantamum paṭippavar





maṉatik kavarntu makiḻvūṭṭali muṟkaruttākavum attuṭaṉ nallaṟivūṭṭali uṭkarut tākavum koṇṭatu. kirantattiliṭiyiṭiyē kīḻttarappaṭṭapāttiraṅkaḷ pēcanērumpoḻutu ilakkaṇavaḻuc ceṟinta avartam vāymoḻikaḷi avvaṇṇamē eḻutuvatu vaḻakkamāyiṉum, kiranta karttā nēriṟ kūṟum varalāṟukaḷ vaḻuviṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. kalvi tērcciyillātārum payaṉ peṟumpaṭi eḻutappaṭum kirantam ātaliṉ, evvaḷavu ilēcāṉa naṭiyil eḻutappaṭiṉum nalamē āyiṉ eḷitil poruḷ teḷital karuti ilakkaṇa vaḻukkaḷōṭu eḻutappaṭiṉ paṭippavaruḷ ilakkaṇam payiṉṟār veṟuppiyum, appayiṟciyillār—avarkaḷuḷ mukkiyamāy ivvitak katikaḷip perumpālum vācippōrākiya ilakkaṇa maṟiyāta peṇpālar—vaḻukkaḷi aṟiyamāṭṭārāy mayakkattiyum aṭivārātaliṉ naṉṉaṭiyil eḻutuvatē takutiyākum.





(patmāvati carittiram. muṉṉuri)



ivvāṟu urinaṭi eḻutum pōtu makkaḷ kiyāḷavēṇṭiya vakiyiṉiyum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil tōṉṟi urinaṭiyi vaḷarttavar tiṟattiyum kāṭṭiya, inta nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil vāḻnta maṟṟeru pulavar karuttiyum iṅkē kāṇalām. avar yāreṉṟu namakkut teriyātu. avar karuttum yāvarukkum ēṟkattakkatā eṉpatum ārāycciyilli. āyiṉum ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil urinaṭi vaḷarttavar paṟṟi inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil irunta intak karutti maṭṭum kāṇalām.

vacaṉameḻutuvōr pirammasrī cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyāriṉvaḻicceṉṟuvacaṉameḻutāmalirun tāltāṉ payaṉaṭiyalām. cāstirikaḷ eḻutiya





vacaṉam mikavum kaṭiṉa naṭiyuṭiyatu. ammātiriyāṉa kaṭiṉa vacaṉam perumpālārkkup payaṉ paṭātu. “viḷaṅka vittal” eṉpatik koṇṭu vacaṉam eḻuta vēṇṭum. kaviyāṉatu kaṭiṉamāyiruntālum kuṟṟamilli. vacaṉam kaṭiṉamāyiruntāl perum payaṉ aḷiyātu. ātaliṉ eḷiya naṭiyil eḻutuvatutāṉ ucitam. srīmāṉ vētanāyakam piḷḷi, srīmāṉ celvak kēcavarāya mutaliyār ākiya vittuvāṉkaḷ eḻutiya vacaṉantāṉ namakku vaḻikāṭṭak kūṭiyatu. avarkaḷuṭiya vacaṉa naṭi mikavum pārāṭṭaṟpālatu. ēṉeṉil, eḷiya naṭiyil eḻutuvatu kaṭiṉa naṭiyil eḻutuvatip pārkkilum varuttamāṉa kāriyam.



(rāmatās eṉṉum putiya kaṭṭuk kati—muṉṉuri, 1908)





ivvāṟu iṉṉum palarum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ nāval paṟṟiyum urinaṭi paṟṟiyum pala karuttukkaḷik kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. rupiṉcaṉ kurucō (1869), tacakumāra carittiram, pirēma kalāvatiyam mutaliya moḻipeyarppu nāvalkaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. ‘tīṉatayāḷu’ mutaliya nāvalkaḷum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil vantaṉa. kirupi cattiyanātaṉ eṉṟa kiṟuttuva ammiyārum iru nāvalkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷār. oṉṟu kiṟittava camayac cārpāṉatu; maṟṟatu intu camayac cārpāṉatu. ‘vinōta carittiram’ eṉṟu uyarnta ilakkiya naṭiyil nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷi eṉpār nāval eḻutiyuḷḷār eṉavum aṟikiṟōm. irājam aiyar avarkaḷ eḻutiya ‘kamalāmpāḷ carittiram’ nāṭaṟinta oṉṟu. 1875 mutal 25 āṇṭukaḷil cumār 50 nāvalkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉa. cila itaḻkaḷum palvēṟu toṭarkatikaḷic ciṟukac ciṟuka veḷiyiṭṭu vantaṉa. ivvāṟu ciṟukatiyum nāvalum tāmum vaḷarntu tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷaravum utaviṉa. makkaḷum ōraḷavu aṟivu viḷakkam peṟṟaṉar.

tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ

ivvāṟu taṉittaṉiyāṉa katikaḷiṉ amippu maṭṭumaṉṟi, pala ciṟu katikaḷit tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum cila iruntaṉa. nalla pulavar eḻutiya katikaḷ citaṟuṇṭupōkā vakiyil pala aṟiñar avaṟṟit tokuttu vittuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ cila acciṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu aṟiñar em. es. pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷi avarkaḷ tokuttatu. 1896il avar ‘vācakat tiraṭṭu’ eṉṟa peyaril pala kati kaṭṭurikaḷit tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭār. avaṟṟuḷ cila pērilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu curukkamāka eḻutap peṟṟaṉa; cila moḻipeyarppukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avi varumāṟu:



1. maṉōṉmaṇi kati jē. pi. vācutēva pantulu (cuntarampiḷḷiyiṉ maṉōṉmaṇiyam)

2. cātumati cariti kē. kuppucāmi mutaliyār (King Lear)

3. maṉōkaravalli kati em. es. pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷi (Teynnyson’s Princess)

4. taṟkāppu niyamam vi. kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār (Herbert Spen-cer’s Principle of Ethics)

5. vaḷanāṭu vitvāṉ jekarāv mutaliyār (tēmpāvaṇi)

6. māṇikkam viṟṟap paṭalac curukkam ñāṉappirakāca vaḷḷaḷār (tiruviliyāṭal purāṇam)

7. aracaṉum pulavaṉum vi. kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār (rūpāvati nāṭakam)

8. pirakalāta carittiram cīṉivācarākavācāriyār (viṣṇu purāṇam)

9. cāvittiri kati ṭi. es. kalacalōcaṉa ceṭṭiyār (pāratam)

10. srī rāmarum vāliyum aruṇācalak kavirāyar (irāmāyaṇam)

11. pāṇṭavar veḷippaṭutal caṇmukak kavirāyar (pāratam)

12. tantiram es. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷi (by Sri John Lubbork)

13. cīvakāruṇya viḷakkam citamparam irāmaliṅkam piḷḷi (aruṭpā)



ikkaṭṭurikaḷi utaviya aṉivarum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷara utavi ceytavarkaḷē. ivaruḷ cilar pala perunūlkaḷiyum yāttuḷḷami aṟivōm. ivarkaḷuḷ oru cilarip paṟṟi nāḷi teḷivākak kāṇalām. ivaṟṟil oriru cāṉṟu kāṭṭi iṉṟu amiyalām. moḻipeyarppāl urinaṭi vaḷarttamikkum itu oru cāṉṟākum. katikaḷ vaḻaṅkiya vakikkum avi cāṉṟu pakarum.

cātumati kati

ippūtalap pūvikkut tilakam pōla viḷaṅkā niṉṟa viruti mānāṭu eṉṉum tivviya tēcatti maṉunīti tavaṟāmalum, ceṅkōl vaḻuvāmalum maṉṉuyirit taṉṉuyir pōl pātukāttu aracu purintu vanta vēntaṉoruvaṉ iruntāṉ. cūci varmaṉ eṉṉum peyar pūṇṭa ivvaracaṉ taṉ maṉiviyuṭaṉ illaṟam vaḻuvātu naṭatti, pūrva caṉmāntarattiṟ ceyta kaṉma vacattāṟ puttira pākkiyam peṟātu maṉavaruttamuṟuṅ kālattuk kaṇmaṇikaḷ pōṉṟa puttirikaḷ mūvar tōṉṟiṉar.

(mutal pakuti)

maṉōkaravalli kati

﻿teṉ tēcamāṇṭu vanta kuṇa vīraṉeṉṉum vēntaṉukku maṉēkaravalli eṉṟa arumip putalvi utittaṉaḷ. avaḷ tālāṭṭup paruvattilēyē taṉ aṉṉiyi iḻakkap peṟṟa nimittama, avaḷ pitā avaḷi nāḷoru mēṉiyāy vaḷarttut tāyiḻanta taṉi nili maṉattiṟ ciṟitum pukāvaṇṇam avaḷ avāk koṇṭu kēṭkum aṉittiyum aṭṭiyiṉṟi avaṭkuc cēkarittuk koṭuttu avaḷ maṉōṣṭap paṭiyē naṭantu vantāṉ,

ittakiya toki nūlkaḷāl-ciṟappākak kati nūlkaḷāl-ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urinaṭi naṉku vaḷarntatu eṉpatu uṟuti.

nāṭakam

ciṟukatikaḷ, nāvalkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi nāṭakaṅkaḷ pala ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivantaṉa. avi perumpālum urinaṭi nūlkaḷē. oru cila pāṭalkaḷ iṭiyiṭiyē viravap peṟṟaṉa. cila nāṭakaṅkaḷ muḻuvatum pāṭalāl āṉapōtilum toṭakka uri, aṟimuka urikaḷ aṉittum urinaṭiyilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa. attakiya nāṭakaṅkaḷ palavāka nāṭṭil naṭikkappeṟṟaṉa. eṉṟālum oru cilavē acciṭappeṟṟu nūl vaṭivil vantuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ‘tāciltār’ eṉṉum nāṭakam. itaṉ āciriyar māvaṭṭa naṭuvarāka (District Munsift) iruntavar. aracāṅka ūḻiyarkaḷil cilar naṭantu koṇṭa vakiyi nēril kaṇṭu kaṇṇīr vaṭittavar eṉat terikiṟatu. itaṉ mutal patippu 1857lum iraṇṭām patippu 1868lum veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. innūl avar makaṉārāl veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. avarum ōyvupeṟṟiruntār eṉak kāṇkiṉṟāēm. nūliṉ mukappup pakkattil piṉvarum kuṟippu uḷḷatu.

tāciltār nāṭakam

citāpuram kāci visvanāta mutaliyār iyaṟṟiyatu

iḵtu kōyamputtuār jillā uṭumalip pēṭṭi muṉicīpāyiruntu ippōtu kevarṉmeṇṭu upakārac campaḷam peṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṟa citāpuram kācivisvanāta mutaliyār avarkaḷāliyaṟṟappaṭṭu avar puttiraṉākiya cōmacuntara mutaliyāratu 'sṭār āp intiyā pirasil' iraṇṭāvatu muṟi acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭatu.

⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰⁰8 ௵ jaṉavari mātam.

103, Armenian Street,

Copy right.

inta mutaṟ pakka viḷakkam akkāla urinaṭikkum cāṉṟāka amikiṉṟatu. itaṉ muṉṉuriyil āciriyar innāṭakam eḻuta nērnta varalāṟṟik kūṟuvatum aṟiya vēṇṭuvatākum.

mukavuri: cilar oru erumiyāṉatu taṇṇīr kuṭikkap pōṉa kuṭṭiyik kalakkic cērākkit taṉṉuṭalelāñ cēṟṟip pūcikkoṇṭu naṭakkiṟa vaḻiyelāñ cēṟākki, vaḻiyiṟ pōkiṟavarkaḷ varukiṟavarkaḷ pēriluñ cēṟṟip

pūcittaṉṉik kaṭṭukiṟa koṭṭattil – kaṭṭuttaṟi – pul – itukaḷiyellāñ cēṟākki – kaṭṭip pul pōṭa vanta taṉ ejamāṉaṉukkuñ cēṟu pūci vippatu pōlak kiṭitta uttiyōkattil paṇa āciyiṉāl lañcam vāṅka ārampittut taṅkaḷuṭiya pērkaḷiyum turittaṉattārkaḷuṭiya pērkaḷiyum keṭuppatumallāmal anēka akkiramaṅkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭu anēka jaṉaṅkaḷ aniyāyamum tuṉpamum naṣṭamum aṭiyumpaṭi ceytu varukiyāl avikaḷip pala vivakāraṅkaḷilum, avaravar naṭattikaḷilumaṟintum aṉupavamuṭiya anēkarālum nontavarkaḷālum collak kēṭṭum uṇarntavaṉā yirukkiṟēṉ.

inta nāṭakattil pāṭalkaḷukkiṭiyil uḷḷa urinaṭip pakutikaḷ cilavaṟṟāl urinaṭi paṟṟiyum nāṭṭu nili paṟṟiyum aṟikiṉṟāēm.

﻿(1) 6vatu pakkam, 5 vatu variyil cēra vēṇṭiya nōṭṭu,

kuṭikaḷuṭiya cavukkiyattukkākak kuṭikiṇaṟukaḷi marāmattuc ceyyac carkkāril 5⁰–6⁰ rūpāy koṭutta pōtilum vīṭṭukkoru oṭṭaṉ veṭṭikku vantu vēli ceytu pōkiṟatuṇṭu. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰4⁰4 ākasṭu ௴ 82 riviṉyū pōrṭṭu carkkiyular uttaraviṉāl kūlikkārarip palavantamākap piṭittu vēlivāṅkivarum vāṭikkiyi niṟuttak kaṭṭaḷi. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰4⁰2 ௵ mē ௴ 92 uttaraviṉal taṇṭu caraparāvukku vaṇṭikkārarit tontaravupaṭuttak kūṭātu. vāṭaki cariyāyum tāmacamillāmalum paṭṭavāṭa ceyyak kaṭṭaḷi.

﻿(2) 8-vatu pakkam, ⁰4-vatu variyil cēravēṇṭiya nōṭṭu, ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰5⁰6 ௵ mē ௴ 42 riviṉiyu pōrṭṭu carkkiyular uttaraviṉāl



kalviyum naṉṉaṭattiyumuḷḷōrip pārttut tācīl uttiyōkattukku niyamittu aluvalkaḷi nitāṉamā yōcittu naṭattumpaṭic ceyyak kaṭṭaḷi.

(3) ⁰–vatu pakkam, 27–vatu variyil cēra vēṇṭiya nōṭṭu. riviṉyu ṭipārṭṭumeṇṭil perumpālum tēcasta pirāmaṇāḷ irukkiṟārkaḷeṉpatik kāṭṭat kūṟiyatu. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰5⁰5 ௵ mārcci ௴ 52 riviṉiyu pōrṭṭu uttaraviṉāl kalakṭṭar kaccēriyil iraṇṭu cirastātārarkaḷum tēcasta pirāmaṇāḷāka irukkak kūṭātu. pirāmaṇāḷip pōlavē maṟṟa jāti jaṉaṅkaḷum tācīltārarkaḷāka niyamikka vēṇumeṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi.

﻿(4) ⁰2–vatu pakkam, 4–vatu variyil cēra vēṇṭiya nōṭṭu. ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰5⁰ ௵ navampar ௴ ⁰42 uttaraviṉāl muṉ eccarikki koṭāmala tālukā kajāṉāvi appōtik kappōtu kalaṭṭarkaḷ jaṭti pārttu varavēṇṭumeṉak kaṭṭaḷi.

(itil varum eṇkaḷ aṉittum tamiḻ eṇkaḷākavē uḷḷaṉa.)

﻿(5) kuṟippu: tāciltār mikunta kōpattōṭu ēmakkāḷi utikkac colliccoṉṉa māttirattil yamakiṅkirarkaḷpōla avariṭattilirukkum cēvakaril – raṅkaṉ – ēmakkāḷik kaṉṉattilaṭikka, kavaṉappaṉ – aṭivayiṟṟil kālālutikka, ēmakkāḷ–pēccaṟṟu mūccittut tariyil viḻuntapōtu cēvakar collukiṟa vitaṅ kāṇka.

— aṭuttatu pāṭṭu (tāḷarākattuṭaṉ)



itupōṉṟa nāṭakaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. aṇmiyil maṟinta pammal campanta mutaliyāratu nāṭakaṅkaḷum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē nāṭṭil ulavat toṭaṅkiya niliyiyum aṟikiṟām. avartam līlāvati culōcaṉā nāṭakam 1895il acciṭap peṟṟatākum. atil āciriyar tam muṉṉuriyil,

﻿teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuriya tamiḻ ilakkiyamāṉatu iyal ici nāṭakameṉa namatu muṉṉērāl mūvakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭirunta pōtilum taṟkālattil yātu kāraṇam paṟṟiyō nāṭakat tamiḻ atika vaḻakkattil illāmi kaṇṭu ciṟiyēṉ eṉatu ciṟṟaṟivik koṇṭu ‘līlāvati-culōcaṉā allatu iraṇṭu cakōtarikaḷ’ eṉṉum ōr navīṉa nāṭakatti iyaṟṟiṉēṉ. taṟkālattiya nilimikkut takkatāyirukkum vaṇṇam āṅkilēya pāṣiyiluḷḷa nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ taṉmi vāyntatāyum nammavar ellōrum vācittaṟiyumpaṭi eḷiya naṭiyāka vacaṉattilum innāṭakam eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. ivar naṭi mika eḷitāka ellōrum aṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiyatu. itupaṟṟi oṉṟum kāṭṭat tēviyilli.

tamiḻil pāṭanūl eḻutap paricu

pammal campanta mutaliyāriṉ tantiyārākiya vijayaraṅka mutaliyārum tamiḻ uri vaḷarccikku utaviṉār eṉpatiyum iṅkē kuṟippiṭal poruttamāṉatākum. ‘amerikkā kaṇṭattik kaṇṭupiṭitta varalāṟu’ paṟṟiya nūl oṉṟu vijayaraṅka mutaliyārāl 1852il eḻuti veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. avartam muṉṉuriyil tam āciriyar tiruttaṇiki vicākap perumāḷiyar eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṟār. innūlum avarāl paricōtikkappaṭṭatu eṉak kuṟikkiṉṟār. avar tamatu mukavuriyil,

﻿innūl irāparṭcaṉ eṉpavarāl iṅkilīṣil eḻutappaṭṭa amerikkak kaṇṭattiṉ caritattiliruntu eṭutteḻutappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ceṉṉap paṭṭaṇam yūṉivarciṭi eṉṉum cāstirak kalvic cāliyil iṅkilīṣ mutaliya pāṣiyik kaṟkum māṇākkaril, iṅkilīṣ pāṣiyiliruntu yātorupayōkamāṉa nūlit tamiḻ mutaliya pāṣikaḷil naṉṟāka eḻutuvōrukkum aḷikkappaṭumpaṭi kā. pacciyappa mutaliyār avarkaḷ tarumatti vicāraṇi ceyyuñ capiyārāl mēṟpaṭi cāstirak kalviccāli akkirā caṉātipati attiyakṣar kaḷiṭattil vikkappaṭṭirukkum poruḷ tokiyil vilipeṟṟa pustakaṅkaḷip peṟumpaṭi inta varuṣārampattil eṉṉāl eḻutappaṭṭatu: (1852)

eṉṟu viḷakkantarukkiṉṟār. eṉavē akkālattilēyē pāṭanūlkaḷit tamiḻākkuvōrukkup paricaḷikkap pacciyappar aṟaniliyattār ippalkalik kaḻakattāriṭam oru tokiyit tantuḷḷārkaḷ eṉak kāṇkiṟōm. atu paṟṟi āyntu aṟital tēviyāṉa oṉṟu.

campanta mutaliyār nāṭakaṅkaḷuṭaṉ vittu eṇṇat takka vakiyil pala nāṭakaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ piratāpa cantira vilācam eṉpatu camūka campantamāṉa oṉṟu. pa. va. irāmacāmi rājū, pi. ē., pārisṭar-aṭ-lā eṉpavar eḻutiya nāṭakam itu. itu eḻutappeṟṟa kālam tiṭṭamāka aṟiya muṭiyāviṭṭālum avar maṟivu 17—8—1897 eṉa aṟivatāl ataṟku muṉpē itu eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉpatu tuṇipu. ittakiya nāṭakaṅkaḷaṉṟi vaḷḷiyammi nāṭakam, iṭampāccāri vilācam, mataṉacuntara piratāpa cantāṉa vilācam mutaliya cumār 35 nāṭakaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil vaḻakkattil iruntaṉa. ivi aṉittum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷarccikku utaviṉa eṉpatu kaṇ kūṭu.



ittakiya nāṭakaṅkaḷ, nāval, ciṟukatikaḷ tavirttuc ‘carittiraṅkaḷ’ eṉappaṭṭaṉavum cila accilum ēṭṭilum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ulavi vantuḷḷaṉa. tērūrnta cōḻaṉ carittiram, civāji carittiram, nācikētu carittiram (1880), maturic caṅkattār carittiram, nālu mantiri carittiram (1839), ṭilli rājākkaḷ carittiram mutaliya varalāṟu kalanta katikaḷ urinaṭiyil iṭampeṟṟu nāṭṭil vāḻntu vantaṉa. avi iṉṟiya carittira nāvalkaḷ pōṉṟaṉapōlum. ivvāṟu, potumakkaḷ eḷitil uṇarntu aṟintu koḷḷum vakiyil pala ciṟukatikaḷum nāvalkaḷum nāṭakaṅkaḷum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟi makkaḷ aṟivi vaḷarttatōṭu tamiḻ uri naṭi taḻikkavum cātaṉamāka iruntaṉa. potumakkaḷiṉ kaṭitappōkku varavu vaḷarnta kāraṇattāl attuṟiyiṉ vaḻiyum urinaṭi vaḷarntatu eṉpati nāḷi kāṇalām. ittuṟikaḷil kaṭanta pala nūṟṟaṇṭukaḷikkāṭṭilum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ urinaṭi atika vaḷarcci aṭintatu eṉalām.

aracāṅka veḷiyīṭukaḷ

iṉi, aracāṅkattār tamiḻ urinaṭi vaḷartta vakiyiṉik kāṇpōm. aracāṅka viḷamparaṅkaḷ, uttaravukaḷ, pattiraṅkaḷ, caṭṭaṅkaḷ, kirāmak kaṇakkukaḷ, veḷiyīṭukaḷ mutaliya pala ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil–tamiḻil–urinaṭiyil veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pala vaṭacoṟkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi arapiya, urutu, parciyac coṟkaḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mukalāya āṭciyi oṭṭi aminta kālamātalāl anta vāṭiyiṉi aṅkē kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. attakiya coṟkaḷuḷ cilavaṟṟip piṉ tantuḷḷēṉ. aracāṅka veḷiyīṭukaḷi oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākak kāṇalām. avi potuvākavum ciṟappāṉa oṉṟip paṟṟiyaṉavumāka vuḷḷaṉa; viḷamparam, ‘ṭeṇṭar’, nalattuṟi, caṭṭam, mīṉpaṇṇi, kāvaltuṟi vaṇikattuṟi, cālittuṟi, ūrāṭci, nāṭṭāṭci . nakarāṭci mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷip paṟṟiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. potuvākac ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ellāttuṟikaḷilum aracāṅkattār āṅkilattil maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻilēyum ellā naṭavaṭikkikaḷiyum mēṟkoṇṭaṉar eṉpatu teḷivu.

(1) caṭṭanūl muṉṉuri: 1882 ām ௵ttiya 8vatu ākṭiṉāl tiruttupāṭāṉa intiya pīṉal kōṭāṉatu 1883m ௵ aiṉavari ௴ 12௳ mutal amulukkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu.

appōtikkappōtu ௸ ākṭi tiruttuvataṟkuṇṭāṉa 1870m ௵ttil 27vatu ākṭ 1872m ௵ttil 19vatu ākṭ 1882m ௵ttiya 8vatu ākṭṭukaḷum intap patippil......

(2) 1835 ௵ttu patiṉēḻāvatu ākṭu eṉṉum caṭṭam (tamiḻil)

⁠1835 ௵ ākaṣṭu ௴ 17m ௳ ālōcaṉi capiyāl-īntiyāviṉ āṉarapil kavarṉar jeṉaralavarkaḷālē vitikkappaṭṭatu.

⁠⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰2⁰5m௵ cepṭampar ௴ 12 mutaṟ koṇṭu kampeṉiyāruṭiya tēcaṅkaḷukkuḷḷēyirukkappaṭṭa taṅkaccālikaḷil aṭiyiṟ colliyirukkiṟa veḷḷi nāṇayaṅkaḷ accaṭikkappaṭumeṉṟu vitikkappaṭṭatu—rupāyāṉatu – kampeṉiyāruṭi rūpāyeṉṟu collappaṭum ari rūpāy–kāl rūpāy–iraṭṭi rūpāy–coṉṉa rūpāy ⁰⁰8⁰ kireyiṉs tirāy niṟiyuḷḷatumāy aṭiyiṟ colliyirukkiṟapaṭi māṟṟuḷḷatumāyirukka vēṇṭiyatu.



⁰⁰9⁰5 kireyiṉskaḷ ﻿—﻿ cokkaveḷḷi

﻿⁰5 kireyiṉskaḷ ﻿—﻿ cempu mutalāṉa kalappaṭam



maṟṟa nāṇayaṅkaḷ atataṟkut takunta niṟiyum attiṉuṭiya māṟṟiyuṅ koṇṭirukka vēṇṭiyatu.

J. C. Mooris

(3) kamicēriyāṭṭu viḷamparam matiṟācu ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰52 ௵ akṭōpar ௴ 2⁰8 ௳.

ituṉālē piracittap paṭuttukiṟa teṉṉameṉṟāl ceṉavari mātam mutal tēti mutal varukiṟa varuṣattukku tēviyāṉa mutal taṟam nāṭṭariciyum-iṟaṇṭāntaram teṟkittariciyum-veḷḷic carkkariyum-uppum-caṟpaṟā paṇṇuvataṟkāka kuttaki ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰5⁰2 ௵ navampar mātam ⁰52 tiṅkaḷ kiḻami pakal paṉṉiraṇṭu maṇikku yinta āpīcil piṟacittamāṉa yēlam pōṭṭu yēlattil ittakiya kuṟinta viliyāka kēḻkkuṟavarukku kamicēri ceṉaṟal turiyavarkaḷ ottukkoṇṭatiṉ pēril kuttaki koṭukkappaṭum.

piratitiṉam tēviyāṉatu yivvaḷa veṉṟu kuttakikārarukku terivettatiṉ pēril-piraci ṭeṉciyilum - paṟaṅkimaliyilum - pallāvarattilum-pūntamalliyilumirukkiṟa yīṟōpiyāṉ ṟā ṇukkaḷukkum-ācupattiṟiyaṉ mutaliya yiṭaṅkaḷukkum kuttakikāraṉ mēl kaṇṭa tiṉucukaḷ caṟpaṟā paṇṇavēṇṭiyatu.

mātāntaram-tēviyāṉa caṟācaṟi mottam yitiṉaṭiyil vavaṟittikkuṟapaṭi.

taṉṉuṭiya kuttakiyi cemmiyāka niṟivēttuvataṟkāka kuttakitāraṉ āyiṟam ṟūpāyi kumpaṉi pattiṟamāyāvatu pāṅku ṣēraṟā yāvatu allatu metta yōkkiyamāṉa cāmīṉā vatu inta āpīcil ṭēvaṇi vikkavēṇṭiyatu.

kuttakiyi yēlattil kēḻkkuṟatukku muṉṉutāka yiṟaṉūṟu ṟūpāyi ṭēviṇi vikka vēṇṭiyatu. yēlattil kuttakiyi yeṭukkuṟavar kuttakiyi yeṭuttukkoḷḷa tavirntu pōṉāl taṉṉuṭiya yiṟaṉūṟu ṟūpāyi ṭēviṇiyiyum pōkkaṭittuk koḷḷuvar-mittavarkaḷuṭiya ṭēviṇikaḷ yēlam mukinta piṉpu tiruppi viṭappaṭum.

(4)﻿kalvi paṟṟi

itiṉālē cakalamāṉa jaṉaṅkaḷukkum teriyappaṭuttukiṟa teṉṉaveṉṟāl varukiṟa jaṉavari mātam mutal tēti mutal hiskūlil paṭikkum vittiyārttikaḷiṉ campaḷam mātam oṉṟukku iraṇṭu rūpāyākak kuṟikkappaṭum- mēlum orē kuṭumpattil oruvaṉukku atikamākap paṭikkum pakṣattil appaṭikku atikamāka varum vittiyārttikaḷiṭattil ௴ 1-kku oru rūpāy campaḷam vāṅkik koḷḷappaṭum.

varukiṟa varuṣam piṟanta mēl kūṭiyatu variyil ārampa cikṣiyiṉ poruṭṭu hiyiskūlic cārntatāy pirimērīskūl eṉṉum oru kalviccāli yēṟpaṭuttappaṭum. atu cilakāla pariyantam hiskū lippōtirukkum iṭattilēyē vikkappaṭum.



mēṟkaṇṭa pirimēri skūl eṉṉum kalviccāliyil kaṟkuñ ciṟuvaṉiṭattil mātam oṉṟukku oru rūpāy vītam campaḷam vāṅkik koḷḷappaṭum . pālarkaḷ paṭikkum puttakaṅkaḷi varukiṟa jaṉavari mātam mutal avāḷē contamāy vāṅkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu.

yūṉivarsiṭi pōrṭār vicāraṇiyiṉ kīḻirukkiṟa kalvic cālikaḷiṉ āṟu mācattiṟkorutaraṅ koṭukkum viṭumuṟi tinaṅkaḷukkaṭutta oru oru pakṣattiṟkuḷḷāka māttiramē vittiyārttikaḷi aṅkīkarikkumpaṭiyāy anta capiyārukku eṇṇamuṇṭāyirukkiṟatu. atik kuṟittiṉimēl teriyapaṭuttupvārkaḷ-āṉālippōtu terivikkiṟa teṉṉaveṉil varukiṟa āṟu mātattil hiyiskūlukkāvatu pirimēri skūlukkāvatu puttirarkaḷēyāvatu pantukkaḷiyāvatu aṉuppa virumpukiṟavarkaḷellām hiskūl eṭmēsṭarukku varukiṟa mī ⁰82 yilāvatu ataṟku muṉṉāvatu teriyappaṭutta vēṇṭiyatu.

(5)﻿ viḷamparap pattiriki

ceṉaṅkaḷil yēḻikaḷāyirukkappaṭṭavarkaḷ yētāvatu viyātikaḷāl tuṉpappaṭumpōtu avarkaḷi ceṉal ācupattiriyil aṅkīkarikkiṟa viṣayattil nāṉāvita atikāra stāṉaṅkaḷilē yiruntu yenta vakiyāyāvatu oru cīṭṭup peṟṟuk koṇṭē pōka vēṇumeṉṟu potuppaṭa oru pecakāṉa yeṇṇaṅ koṇ ṭiruppatāka kāṇappaṭukiṟa paṭiyiṉālē yitiṉāl

aṟivikkiṟateṉṉaveṉṟāl irōppu tēcattārākilum paṟaṅkikkārākilum allatu inta tēcattārākilum yārāvatu ātiravattu vayittiyaṅ kōrappaṭṭavarkaḷ ācupattiriyi cārnta yenta ṭākṭar turiyavarkaḷiṭattukkāvatu tāṅkaḷē pōy ājarākuvatē pōtum mitta yātoru cīṭṭu mutalāṉatum tēviyilli appaṭi (viyātiyiliruntu yēḻi ceṉaṅkaḷ yenta viyātikkāvatu vayittiyaṅ kōṟiṉālum ṟaṇa vayittiyaṅ kōṟiṉālum yenta nērattilum avāḷavāḷ collik koṇṭa uṭaṉē oppukkoṇṭu utavi ceyyappaṭum.

(9)﻿ muttukkuḷiyal paṟṟi

tirunelvēlic cīmiyiṉ kalikkiṭṭuqkiya mēstar ruparṭṭu yīṭaṉ turiyavarkaḷ

ituṉālē cakalamāṉa pērkaḷukkut teriyumpaṭi piracittam paṇṇukiṟateṉṉaveṉṟāl tirunelvēli cīmiyic cērnta kaṭal turimukattil varukiṟa akṭōpar ௴ ⁰52 mutal iṅku kuḷikkiṟataṟkāka oru varuṣam reṇṭu varuṣam allatu mūṉṟu varuṣam kuttakikku nāḷatu ⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰35 ௵ akṭōpar ௴ 5௳ varikku nammuṭiya acūr kaccēriyil taṟkāstu kuṭuttāl vāṅkit koḷḷappaṭum.

(17–8–1885).





R. Eden. Collector





ivvāṟāka aracāṅka āṇi mutaliyaṉavaṉṟi, taṉiṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ nilam, vīṭu mutaliyaṉa vāṅkavum viṟkavum pativu ceyya āvaṇakkaḷariyil eḻutiya pattiraṅkaḷuḷ pala tamiḻ urinaṭiyilēyē irukkiṉṟaṉa.

(10)﻿ aḷam kaṇakku:—(pala eḻuttukkaḷukkup puḷḷiyilli)

ceṉṉappaṭṭaṇam kalekṭarākiya ma-⁰⁰-⁰⁰ ē rōparṭcaṉ turiyavarkaḷ camukattukku eṇṇūr aḷam kaṇakku muttappiḷḷi eḻutik koṭutta attāṭci.

eṉṉaveṉṟāl eṇṇūr aḷaṅkuṭiyākiya tōṇi ciṉṉaveṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ, iruṅkuṉṟam kiruṣṇappa nā. kiyil matattuvāṅkik koṇṭatāy, taṉ pērilirukkiṟa aḷam-kate-um avaṉ pērālum carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceytu koṭukka vēṇumeṉṟu ācūril arji koṭukka anta arijip paṭikkuc ceytu koṭukkak kūṭā teṉṟu, muṉṉē akṣēpittiruntavarkaḷākiya, mēṟpaṭi ciṉṉa veṅkaṭṭa

rāmaṉuṭiya takappaṉ tōṇimallā reṭṭiyum mēṟpaṭi veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ peṇ cātiyākiya kāmākṣiyammāḷum, ippōtu cammatippaṭṭu, mēṟpaṭi kiruṣṇappa nā. pēriyum carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceytukoḷḷalāmeṉṟu maṟupaṭiyum ujūril arj koṭuttuk kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟapaṭiyālum ௸ aḷattukkāka vēṟē āṣēpaṇi oṉṟumillāta paṭiyālum ௸ kiruṣṇappa nā. pēriyum carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceytukoḷḷukiṟa tiṉālē yātoru vittiyācamu milli. intappaṭi nāṉ cammatittu attāṭci

itaṟkut tanta aracāṅka uttaraviṉiyum uṭaṉ kāṇal naṉṟu:-

ijatācār muttucāmi piḷḷi aḷattu atikāri koṭṭār eṇṇūr.

tarivillā eṇṇūr aḷaṅkuṭi tōṇi ciṉṉa veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ pērālirukkiṟa-ka te-um, iruṅkuṉṟam kiruṣṇappa nā. pērālum, kūṭṭāy carkkār kaṇakkil tākkal ceyvataṟku, yātoru ākṣēpaṇiyumilli yeṉṟu nīr ௸ ௴ ⁰82 ummuṭiya aāj rippōrṭu ceytirukkiṟa paṭikku ௸ ciṉṉa veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ pērāl carkkār kaṇakkil tākkalāyirukkiṟa-kate-atu iruṅ kuṉṟam kiruṣṇappa nā. pērum ௸ tētiyil arj rijiṣṭar pustakattil takkal ceytirukkiṟōm ākiyār koṭṭār majkūr aḷam ācāmi

vāri pustakattilum, kaṇakkuppiḷḷi kaṇakkilum, antappaṭikku tākkal ceyvittu, avarkaḷik koṇṭu vārakkam cākupaṭi mutalāṉa kāriyamum jākkiratiyāy naṭappittuk koṇṭu varavum.

cila vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ

akkālattil-ceṉṟa nūṟṟaṇṭil-vaḻaṅkiya cila coṟkaḷukkuriya poruḷkaḷi aṟitalum nalamākum. avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟik kīḻē tarukiṟēṉ. ikkālattilum perumpālum vaḻakkattil uḷḷa coṟkaḷi īṇṭu nāṉ kāṭṭavilli. ivi piṟamoḻikaḷiliruntu tamiḻil-ciṟappāka aracāṅkattuṟiyil-vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷākum. ‘aṟamaṉi vārttikaḷ’ eṉavē avi kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

camutāya nūlkaḷ

ivimaṭṭumaṉṟi makkaḷ camutāya vāḻvu paṟṟiya pala nūlkaḷ urinaṭiyil vantuḷḷaṉa. 1898il ‘intiyaṉ kirimiṉal lāc curukkam’ eṉṟa nūl tamiḻil veḷiyāyiṟṟu. innūl kumpakōṇam eṉ. vittiyanāta aiyarāl, pōlīcu āpīcarkaḷ, kirāma muṉicipukaḷ, piri vēṭ vakkīlkaḷ mutaliya kirimiṉal viyavakāra ñāṉamaṭiya virumpuvōr yāvarukkum mukkiya upayōkappaṭumpaṭi iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu’ ivvāṟu 1897il es. kumāracāmi mutaliyār eṉapavarāl 'jamīntāri eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa stitiyic cārnta caṅkiraka kirantam’ eṉṟa nūl jamīntār muṟi paṟṟi eḻutap peṟṟup pativu ceyyap peṟṟatu. ivi maṭṭumiṉṟi, 'vitavā vivākam’ pōṉṟa cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya nūlkaḷum eḻuntaṉa. annūlkaḷukku aṭippaṭi akkālattu attakiya tirumaṇaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟṟamiyē eṉpatu annūlkaḷip payilvār naṉku uṇarvārkaḷ. nākarkōyilic cērnta cēṣiyaṅkār tam makaṉukku ittakiya maṇam ceytār eṉṟum ati ātarittu 1874il 'vitavōtvāka vivēkam’ eṉṟa nūl eḻutappaṭṭateṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. annūli maṟuttu aṭutta āṇṭē (1875il) raṅkāc raṅkāccāri eṉpār ‘vitavōtvāka kaṇṭaṉam’ eṉṟa nūli eḻuti acceyalik kaṇṭittirukkiṟar.

kaṇavaṉi iḻanta kimpeṇṇic cuṟṟi aḻuvatip paṟṟiya kuṟippu oṉṟu amarampēṭu muttucāmi mutaliyār eḻutiya camūka iyal paṟṟiya ‘apimāṉam eṉpatu eṉṉa?’ eṉṟa 1886il veḷiyāṉa nūlil uḷḷatu. itupōṉṟē vāḻviyalip paṟṟiya pala kuṟippukaḷ innūlil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

aḻuki:- peṇkaḷ mayiriḻavu, māriḻavu, kālaṭavu, kaṭṭiyaḻal eṉṉum ācāraṅkaḷi naṭattukiyil, kaṭṭiyaḻum pōtu vācal peritāyirukkumāyiṉ, tiṭṭutiṭṭākap pattup patiṉintu pēr vaṭṭamāka niṉṟu, kikōttu acintāṭi oruvita rākattōṭu pāṭik koṇṭu, koñca kālaṅ kaḻinta piṟaku, vēṟu naṭic cantattōṭu pāṭikkoṇṭu, kālaṭavu pōṭṭu mārpilaṭittuk koṇṭu cuṟṟi varukiyil putitāyk kaṟṟuk koḷḷukiṟavaḷāvatu vitaraṇi illātavaḷākavāvatu iruntu, aṭavu cariyāyp pōṭātavaḷik kiyippiṭittiḻuttu veḷiyil viṭṭu viṭukiṟarkaḷ. iḻavukku aḻappōkiyil talimayiri cikkalillāmal uṭittuk kōtikkoṇṭu pōy, etiretir mukamāka niṉṟu, etuki mōṉi tavaṟāmal ataṟkicinta vēṟu rākattuṭaṉē pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭikkoṇṭu talimayiri iraṇṭu kikaḷālum viralkaḷik koṇṭu cīvukiṟārkaḷ. kaṇavaṉip paṟikoṭuttut tavikkiṉṟavaḷik kīḻē taḷḷi maṟṟavarkaḷ avaḷic cūḻntu ‘lapalapa'veṉṟu vāyilaṭittuk koḷḷukiṟarkaḷ. mārmēlirukkum tuṇiyi eṭuttu iṭuppil varintu kaṭṭik koṇṭum mārpilaṭittuk koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ.

ivvāṟē peṇkaḷ malaṭāka uḷḷaṉarā, āṇkaḷ malaṭāka uḷḷaṉarā eṉpati aṟiyac ‘ciṟṟiṉpac cirōṉmaṇi’ eṉṟa vacaṉa nūlil vaḻivakuttuḷḷaṉar. innūl ‘va. u. ci.’ avarkaḷāl avartam toṭakka kālattil eḻutappaṭṭatām. inta muṟi iṉṟuḷḷa pala ṭākṭarkaḷ aṟiyāta oṉṟākum.

ciṟṟiṉpac cirōṉmaṇi nūl:-āṇ peṇṇiruvaril malaṭar iṉṉār eṉa aṟiyum tantiram mocci viriyi iraṇṭu pāttiraṅkaḷiṟ pōṭṭu, puruṣaṉuṭiya ciṟunīri oru pāttirattilum stirīyiṉuṭiya ciṟunīri oru pattirattilum taṉittaṉi viṭṭu vantu 5 tiṉattiṟkup piṟaku yāruṭiya nīrviṭṭa koṭṭi muḷikkavilliyō avarkaḷi malaṭeṉṟu kaṇṭu takunta cikicci ceyya vēṇṭiyatu. ivvāṟē curikkoṭip payirilum tiruṣṭāntaṅ kāṇalām. āṉāl oru nīr viṭṭu oru mātam kaḻinta piṉṉartāṉ maṟu nīr viṭṭup pārkka vēṇṭum. vevvēṟu koṭikaḷāṉāl orē kālattil pakṣīkkalām.

itupōṉṟa palappala vakiyāṉa karuttukkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyāṉa tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi namakku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

ivi maṭṭumaṉṟic cāṭcikaḷ paṟṟiya ‘ruju cāstira caṅkirakam’, kuṭumpattil makkaḷ vāḻa vēṇṭiya vaki paṟṟiya 'illat tōḻaṉ’ (1845), pōlīs naṭantu koḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟi paṟṟiya 'pōlīs nipuṇaṉ’ (1894) mutaliya pala nūlkaḷ ovvoru tuṟikkum eṭuttuk kāṭṭākavum viḷakkaṅkaḷākavum aṉivarum eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷum vakiyilum eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. āciriyat toḻiliyaṟṟuvōrukku utavi ceyyum vakiyil pala nūlkaḷum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivi aṉittum makkaḷ eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷum poruṭṭu urinaṭi nūlkaḷākavē amintuḷḷaṉa. paḻamoḻi, vēṭikki, viṭukatikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟic cārnta urinaṭi nūlkaḷum nāṭṭil ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ulaviṉa.

kāṅkiras ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē nāṭṭil tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ atu paṟṟik ‘kāṅkiras viṉā-viṭi’yum (1890), āciyāviṉ aikkiyamum (1888) veḷivantaṉa.

pāṭa nūlkaḷ

iviyaṉṟi, kallūriyilum paḷḷiyilum kaṟkum māṇavarkaḷukkeṉa eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ciṟappākap-piṟpakutiyil-veḷivantuḷṉaṉa. avi pala nūṟṟuk kaṇakkil iruppatāl avipaṟṟi viḷakkam tēviyilli. avaṟṟi veḷiyiṭa aracāṅkattār pala caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷum, vaṟiyaṟikaḷum ceytiruntārkaḷ eṉa nammāl avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉurikaḷiliruntu aṟiyamuṭikiṉṟatu. ‘pāla pāṭam’ mutaliya nūlkaḷi āṟumuka nāvalar mutaliya nallaṟiñarkaḷ eḻuti-aracāṅkap pāṭat tiṭṭattil illiyāyiṉum-iḷampiḷḷikaḷ uḷḷattil oḻukkam, paṇpāṭu, camaya uṇarvu mutaliya nalliyalpukaḷ arumpumāṟu ākkappaṇi ceytuḷḷaṉar. avipaṟṟiyellām virippiṟ perukum. (oru cila nāḷi kāṇalām)

potu makkaḷ paṭittaṟiyat takkatāka aṟiviyal, tāvaram, nilam, maruntu, varalāṟu paṟṟiya nūlkaḷum pala ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyāyiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉa āṅkila nūlkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷākavē amikiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cila kalappu moḻiyil amintuḷḷaṉa eṉiṉum oru cila nalla tamiḻil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatiyum kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatu. cila paḷḷikūṭa nūlkaḷil pala viḷakkaṅkaḷ mutalil āṅkilattilum, piṟaku tamiḻilum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cila nūlkaḷil poruḷaṭakkam mutaliyaṉavum āṅkilattilēyē . amintuḷḷaṉa. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattār ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil paḷḷi iṟutit tērvukkeṉap (Metriculation Examination) pala nūlakaḷ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaḷaṉar. āṇṭutōṟum tamiḻppāṭa nūl oṉṟu pāṭṭumuriyum kalantu veḷivantatu. urinaṭi eḻutiyavarkaḷ peyar kāṭṭappeṟavilli. kiṭitta kuṟippukkaḷiliruntu, avi aṉittum āṅkilattiliruntu-palvēṟu āṅkila nūṟpakutikaḷiliruntu poṟukki eṭukkap peṟṟaṉavāki-oruvarālēyē moḻipeyarkkap peṟṟu nūlākkap peṟṟa niliyi aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē pala moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷum, kaṭṭurikaḷum tamiḻil urinaṭi vaḷarkka utaviyuḷḷaṉa.

pāṭanūlkaḷip paṟṟi eṇṇumpōtu attuṟiyiluḷḷa palvēṟu nūlkaḷum nam niṉivukku varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻppāṭa nūlkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyumē tamiḻil collik koṭuttārkaḷ eṉpatu akkālattil uṇṭāyirunta pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ amippiliruntu uṇarkiṟōm. nila nūl, varalāṟu mutaliya pāṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ āṅkila nūlkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷākavē amintaṉa. eṉavē moḻi peyarppu vakiyil katikaḷum pāṭanūlkaḷum amintu urinaṭiyi vaḷarttaṉa.

viṉaviṭi vakiyilum makkaḷukku eḷiya vakiyil uyarnta poruḷkaḷi viḷakkum cila urinaṭi nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil uṇṭāyiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ‘cārīra viṉaviṭi’ eṉpatu 1866il veḷivantatu. in nūl ceṉṉappaṭṭaṇattu vittiya uttiyōka vakuppic cērnta ma. jakannāta nāyuṭu avarkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. pala uyarnta poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi eḷiya vakiyil viṉāviṭuttu viṭi peṟum vakiyil innūlum itupōṉṟa piṟa vi-aiviṭi nūlkaḷum payaṉ aḷittamiyik kāṇkiṉṟāēm. ittakiya nūlkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavaṟṟit tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ allāta piṟa tuṟikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟiyōrē eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.

āṅkilēyar kalvi peṟuvataṟkāka amikkappeṟṟa. kallūri oṉṟiṉippaṟṟi mutalil (The College of Fort St. George) kaṇṭōm. atit tamiḻil ‘ceṉṉik kalvic caṅkam’ eṉṟa peyariṭṭu aḻittaṉar. atu 1812il toṭaṅkappeṟṟu, 1854 vari paṇipurintatu. ataṉ vaḻiyākavum pala nalla tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷ nāṭṭu makkaḷukkākavum-ciṟappāka āṅkila nāṭṭiliruntu iṅku vantu paṇi purintavarkaḷukkākavum acciṭap peṟṟaṉa eṉak kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. pañca tantirak kati (1826), katā mañcari (1826), miruti cantiriki (1826), tamiḻ ariccuvaṭi (1827) ākiyavi avaṟṟuḷ cila. 'ceṉṉi centamiḻ uri caṅkam’ eṉavum oru caṅkam iruntu urinaṭiyi vaḷarttami kāṇkiṟōm.

taṉ muyaṟci eṉṟa nūl ‘sāmiyuyal smils’ eṉpār āṅkilattil eḻutiyataṉ moḻipeyarppu nūlākat tamiḻil (1882) vantuḷḷatu. nāṭu uṇmiyil nāṭāka vēṇṭumāṉal, taṉi maṉitaṉum camutāyamum mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭiya pala uṇmikaḷum, viḷakkaṅkaḷum itil kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. appaṭiyē ci. viruttup piḷḷi avarkaḷ eḻutiya ‘ērāṇmi nūl’ (1885) eṉpatu nāṭṭiṉ accāṇiyākiya uḻavu paṟṟik kūṟuvatu. ituvum āṅkila moḻipeyarppu nūlēyākum. in nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil kirantac coṟkaḷit taḻuvāmal āṅkilac coṟkaḷi atikamāka vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷamikku, moḻippeyarppāḷar kāraṇam kāṭṭukiṟar. ippaṭiyē vāṉamaṇṭalatti viḷakkac cōti cāstiram (1848) oṉṟu yāḻppāṇattil veḷiyāyiṟṟu. vittiya muṟi paṟṟiyum (1863), varalāṟu, nila nūl paṟṟiyum (1858) uri naṭi nūlkaḷ veḷivantaṉa. ivvāṟu maṉita vāḻviṉ aṭippaṭit tēvikaḷ aṉittum paṟṟiya tamiḻ uri naṭi nūlkaḷ veḷivanta kālam ceṉṟa pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭē eṉalām. iṉṟu atikamāka vaḷarntuviṭṭatāka eṇṇum nam kālattik kāṭṭilum ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil ellāt tuṟikaḷum vaḷam peṟṟiruntaṉa eṉak kūṟuvēṉ.

itaḻkaḷ

iṉi, iṉṟu nāṭṭiṉ mutukelumpākak karutum pattiriki vaḷarcciyip paṟṟik kaṇṭu amiyalām eṉa eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ. kaṭanta nūṟṟaṇṭil nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa itaḻkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṟkup pala camayaṅkaḷil aracāṅkam añcal kuṟippu mutaliya vacatikaḷic ceytu tantuḷḷatu. iruntum avaṟṟuḷ pala cila āṇṭukaḷē iruntu maṟintuviṭṭamiyik kāṇkiṉṟāēm.

cila itaḻkaḷ makkaḷukku nalla toṇṭāṟṟi vantaṉa. palvēṟu pakutikaḷit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭa pattirikikaḷ cila. camaya campantamāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷum maṟuppukaḷum cila pattirikikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. yāḻppāṇattiliruntu tōṉṟiya utaya tāraki paṟṟiya kuṟippiṉik kaṇṭu amiyalām.

utayatāraki 1841: Morning Star (gp36b L;#6b 2–6i 6m (5.6LLI) (Published in the 1st and 3rd Thursday of every month at one shilling a Quarter, payable in advance).

cañciki 3.⁰⁰⁰8⁰⁰41 ௵ māci ௴ 4 tikati viyāḻakkiḻami.

kaṭicip pakkattil uḷḷa kuṟippu:

Printed and Published at the American Mission Press, Jaffna, by Eastman Strong Minor— Edited by Henry Marly and Seth Payson.

pakka eṇkaḷ tamiḻilum, āṅkilattilum toṭar eṇkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.

ivvitaḻil intu tēcacarittiram, pirapañca nūl (nila nūl),kāriki, vilaṅkiyal, pañcatantirak kati, kalvip poruḷ, putiṉac ceytikaḷ mutaliyaṉa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. pañcatantirak katiyum, putiṉac ceytikaḷum āṅkilattilum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cila taṉitta āṅkilak kaṭṭurikaḷum uḷḷaṉa.

pattiriki paṟṟi ataṉ kuṟippu: utaya tāraki pattirattil kaṟki, carittiram, potuvāṉa kalvi, payircceyki, aracāṭci mārkkam mutalāṉavi paṟṟiyum piratāṉa putiṉac ceytikaḷip paṟṟiyum accaṭikkappaṭum. atu tamiḻp pāṣiyilum iṭikkiṭiyē tamiḻum, iṅkilicum kūṭiṉatāyum eṭṭuppuṟa muḷḷatāka nāṉkāy maṭitta tāḷ aḷavil ovvoru mātam mutalām mūṉṟām viyāḻakkiḻamikaḷil piracittam paṇṇappaṭum.

itaṉ vili pattiram oṉṟukku 2 peṉcu allatu ⁰6 veḷḷic calli allatu mūṉṟu mātattiṟkum mūṉṟik koṭuttiruntāl appattiraṅkaḷiṉ vili oru ciliṅku.

ivvāṟu ataṉ viḷakkam virintu koṇṭē pōkiṟatu.

itu itaḻ urinaṭikku oru cāṉṟākum.

tamiḻ nāṭṭu itaḻkaḷum inta amippilēyē ceṉṟaṉa. cila camaya vaḷarccikkum, cila peṇkaḷ, iḷiñar pakutikkum, cila tattuvam pōṉṟavikaḷukkum ciṟappākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟi uṭaṉ tokuttuḷḷēṉ. iṉṉum pala nāṭṭil ulaviṉa eṉpatu tēṟṟam.

﻿ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivanta itaḻkaḷil cila:



1. upāttiyāyar (1887) āciriyarukku vēṇṭiya pala ceytikaḷ

2. kirāma viyavahāriṉi (1899) mutaṟ pakutiyil ilakkiyac ceytikaḷum vēṭikki vinōtaṅkaḷum iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭām pakuti kirāma nirvākam paṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu.

muttiṅkaḷukkoru muṟi (1900) potuc ceytikaḷum kirāma vivakāram paṟṟiya taṉic ceytikaḷum.

4. kirāmātikāri (tiṅkaḷ) (1900) ‘ceṉṉi irājatāṉiyiṉ kirāma paripālaṉam cīrpaṭum poruṭṭup piracurikkap paṭuvatu’.

5. kōyamuttur kalāniti (tiṅkaḷ) (1890)

6. cittānta ratnākaram āticivap pira pāvam) (1880) āticiva marapiṉar paṟṟaṟiya ceytikaḷi vakippaṭutti urippatu.

7. cittānta ratnākaram (cittānta pūṣaṇam) (1881) cittānta pūṣaṇam paṟṟip pala nūl mēṟkōḷkāṭṭi viḷakkum ārāycci.



8. cittānta ratnākaram paṟpala mata (uttama vātatūpa vātūlam) (1881) paṟpala mata uṇmikaḷum iṇintavi eṉa āciriyar āyntu aḷaviṭṭurikkiṟār.

9. cittānta ratnākaram (camaraca ñāṉa tīpam) (1881) ‘civa camayamē camayam’ eṉat toṭaṅkum tāyumāṉavar pāṭal karutti viḷakki aminta piracaṅkak kaṭṭuri.

10. cittānta ratnākaram (āpāca ñāṉa nirōtam) (1883)

16. pakavatkītā pāṣyārtta pōtiṉi (1895) vaṭamoḻi mūlattiliruntu tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭatu.

17. ñāṉapōtiṉi (1897-1905) ilakkiya, aṟiviyal, tattuva, camaya itaḻ.

nāḷitaḻ:

1. cutēcamittiraṉ (1880)

iṉṉum cila pattirikikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷum uḷḷaṉa, ivaṟṟuḷ perumpālaṉa kālameṉum kaṭuṅ kāṟṟiṉ etir niṟkamāṭṭāmal talicaryntu viṭṭaṉa. oru cila, cila āṇṭukaḷ veṟṟimuracu koṭṭi vāḻntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritāki viṭṭatu allavā? ivaṟṟil cila āṇṭu malarkaḷum veḷiyiṭṭaṉa. 'cutēcamittiraṉ’ tōṉṟiya nāḷtoṭṭu (1880) iṉṟu vari nammiṭiyē vāḻntu varukiṉṟatu. taṟpōtu (1978) niṟuttappeṟṟuḷḷatu.

ivvāṟu makkaḷoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷa palvēṟu tuṟikaḷilum avar tam vāḻvoṭu vāḻvāy vaḻakkoṭu vaḻakkāyk kēḷviyoṭu kēḷviyāyc colloṭu collāy ellā nilikaḷilum, ceyal kaḷilum, muṟikaḷilum tamiḻ uri kalantu avarkaḷi vāḻavittu, tamiḻ vāḻvi vaḷamuṟac ceytu, tāṉum vaḷarntu vāḻntu inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ etirkālatti nōkki 1901 il kāl vittatu.

itaṉ vēṟu cila pirivutaḷi nāḷi kāṇalām.
